local luraph_runtime = {}

-- Functions of the script that Luraph keeps as plain (non-virtualized) Lua inside its VM chunk, quoted
-- verbatim from that chunk: their local names are not recoverable. Each is built by a maker that receives
-- the VM chunk's helper table `c` (below); luraph_runtimeN(nil, table.pack(upvalues)) instantiates one.
luraph_runtime[1] = (function(c)
	return function()
	  return function()
	    local y = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G

	    local C = UDim2.fromOffset

	      if typeof(l) == "Instance" then
	        s = l
	      end
	    end)

	    local l = s:FindFirstChild("ChilliHubLoading")

	    if l then
	      l:Destroy()
	    end

	    local function l(e, a, q)
	      local O = Instance.new(e)

	      for e, P in next, q, nil do
	        O[e] = P
	      end

	      O.Parent = a
	      return O
	    end

	    local function e(a, q)
	      l("UICorner", a, {CornerRadius = UDim.new(q, ((q == 1) and 0) or 10)})
	    end

	    local a = l("ScreenGui", nil, {Name = "ChilliHubLoading", ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true, DisplayOrder = 1000, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling})
	    local q = l("Frame", a, {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = T(0.5, 0, 0, 24), Size = C(260, 64), BackgroundColor3 = A(18, 18, 22), BackgroundTransparency = 0.05})
	    e(q, 0)
	    l("UIStroke", q, {Color = A(255, 60, 60), Thickness = 1.5, Transparency = 0.2})

	    local function O(P, i, m, d, B, b, g)
	      return l("TextLabel", q, {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = C(12, P), Size = T(1, -24, 0, i), Font = m, TextSize = d, TextColor3 = B, TextXAlignment = b, Text = g})
	    end

	    local P, i = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	    O(8, 18, P, 15, A(255, 255, 255), i, "Chilli Hub")
	    local m = O(8, 18, P, 15, A(255, 120, 90), Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, "0%")
	    local P = O(27, 14, Enum.Font.GothamMedium, 12, A(170, 170, 185), i, "Loading part 1 / 10")
	    i = l("Frame", q, {Position = C(12, 46), Size = T(1, -24, 0, 8), BackgroundColor3 = A(42, 42, 50)})
	    e(i, 1)
	    local C = l("Frame", i, {Size = T(0, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = A(255, 255, 255)})
	    e(C, 1)
	    l("UIGradient", C, {Color = ColorSequence.new(A(255, 50, 50), A(255, 150, 60))})
	    a.Parent = s
	    local s, l = "ChilliLibrary/SAE_LoadProgress.txt"

	    pcall(function()
	      if isfile(s) then
	        l = tonumber(readfile(s):match("^(%d+)"))
	      end
	    end)

	    local function e(q)
	      pcall(function()
	        if not (isfolder("ChilliLibrary")) then
	          makefolder("ChilliLibrary")
	        end

	        writefile(s, q .. (" " .. os.time()))
	      end)
	    end

	    e(0)
	    y.ChilliHubLoading = os.clock() + 30

	    y.ChilliHubLoadStep = function(s)
	      local q = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(s) or 0), 0, 10)
	      e(q)

	      pcall(function()
	        C.Size, m.Text = T(q / 10, 0, 1, 0), (q * 10) .. "%"
	        P.Text = ((q >= 10) and "Loaded") or ("Loading part " .. ((q + 1) .. " / 10"))
	      end)

	      task.wait()
	      if q < 10 then
	        return
	      end
	      y.ChilliHubLoadStep = nil
	      y.ChilliCanvasHold = nil
	      y.ChilliHubLoading = nil

	      if l and (l < 10) then
	        pcall(function()
	          P.Text, P.TextColor3 = "Last load stopped at " .. ((l * 10) .. ("% (part " .. ((l + 1) .. " / 10)"))), A(255, 170, 90)
	        end)

	        task.delay(8, pcall, a.Destroy, a)
	      else
	        pcall(a.Destroy, a)
	      end
	    end
	  end
	end
end)(nil)

	          end
	        end
	      end
	    end

	          break

	            return false

	        if bN.Magnitude <= (aN or 6) then
	          mN = s[42]

	          return true

	          dN, PN = bN.Magnitude, os.clock()
	        else
	        end

	          end

	            end
	          end

	          if not ((if WN then not WN else os.clock() < tN) and XN()) then
	            break
	          end

	            return false
	          end

	            kN = os.clock() + 0.2

	              return false, "Egg went back to its nest"
	            end

	              return false, "Guard is bringing it back"
	            end
	          else

	          if wN then
	            if wN:IsA("Model") then

	                WN = s[29]
	                _N = WN(kN.X, kN.Y - (IN.Y / 2), kN.Z)
	              else
	              end
	            else
	            end
	          else
	          end

	          WN = s[29]
	          if WN(_N.X - rN.X, 0, _N.Z - rN.Z).Magnitude > 400 then
	            return false, "Egg went back to its nest"
	          end
	          WN = s[19]
	          wN = WN()

	          if wN and wN.Anchored then
	            s[13](XN)
	          else
	            if (vN > 0) and ((os.clock() - vN) > 0.6) then
	              vN = 0

	                local uN = XN()
	                local XN

	                if uN then
	                  local kN = s[25]
	                  XN = not kN(EN)
	                else
	                  XN = uN
	                end

	              end, 3, 3, 0.95)

	                XN(kN(_N + IN(0, ((uN and uN.HipHeight) or 2) + 1, 0)) * HN)
	              end
	            end
	          end

	            KN = os.clock() + 0.15

	              wN = mN[32]
	              XN = (wN(_N))
	            else
	              XN = uN
	            end

	              pcall(function()
	                XN.HoldDuration = 0
	                fireproximityprompt(XN)
	              end)
	            end
	          else
	          end

	            GN += 1

	            task.spawn(function()
	              local uN = s[26]
	              local XN, XN = uN("Egg", "CarryFieldEgg", EN)

	                  vN = os.clock()
	                end
	              else
	                vN = 0
	              end

	              if XN:find("trusted") or XN:find("closer") then
	                if LN == 0 then
	                  LN = os.clock()
	                end
	              else
	                LN = 0
	              end
	            end)
	          else
	          end

	          if LN > 0 then
	            if (os.clock() - LN) > 4 then
	              return false, "Server pulled you back"
	            end
	          end

	            DN = os.clock() + 1
	            WN, wN = s[23], false

	            for uN, uN in pairs(WN()) do
	              wN = if type(uN) == "table" then if uN.Uid == EN then uN.State ~= "Slot" else wN else wN
	            end

	            if not wN then
	              return false, "Egg went back to its nest"
	            end
	          else
	          end

	          wN()
	        end

	        _N = s[25]
	        if not (_N(EN)) then
	          return false
	        end

	          _N(KN(uN()) * HN)

	            else

	            return true
	          end

	          EN = 0

	            XN = s[45]
	            if not (EN < (0.3 + XN())) then
	              break
	            end
	            uN = s[20]
	            EN += uN()
	          end
	        end

	        return false
	      end

	      local uN, EN, XN, QN, rN, HN, KN, tN, DN, GN = y:Mo(13, 14, {[1] = s, [19] = s, [15] = s, [21] = s, [13] = s, [10] = mN, [12] = mN, [11] = s, [17] = s, [18] = s, [20] = s, [51] = s, [33] = s, [25] = s, [26] = s, [52] = s}, A, C, nil), {}, {}, 0, {}, 0, false, {}, 0, s[15]

	      GN.indexMissing = function()
	        if os.clock() > DN then

	      local function tN()
	        if os.clock() < HN then
	          local DN = rN
	          return DN
	        end
	        HN, rN = os.clock() + 5, {}
	        local HN = s[64]
	        local DN = HN()
	        if not (DN) then
	          HN = rN
	          return HN
	        end
	        local GN, LN, vN = ((type(DN.Index) == "table") and DN.Index) or {}, {}, {"Inventory", "EggInventory"}

	        for kN, kN in ipairs(vN) do

	            if type(_N) == "table" then

	            end

	            vN = (type(wN) == "table") and (wN[1] or wN.Category)

	      local function DN(GN)
	        if (GN.State == "Dropped") or (GN.State == "Carried") then
	          local LN = workspace:FindFirstChild(GN.Uid) or (HN and HN:FindFirstChild(GN.Uid))

	          local HN, vN = pcall(function()
	            return LN:GetPivot().Position
	          end)

	          if HN then
	            if vN then
	              return vN
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        return GN.BottomCFrame.Position
	      end

	      local function HN(GN, LN, vN, kN, _N, wN)
	        local WN = rN[LN]

	        if if WN then WN.r ~= GN else not WN then
	          WN = {r = GN, e = D(GN)}
	          rN[LN] = WN
	        else
	        end

	        local IN, zN, zN = WN.e, s[15], table.find(s[15].pick, LN)
	        local WN, JN = ((zN and "pick") or ((vN and vN[IN.cat]) and "index")) or (kN and (kN(IN)))

	        if WN then
	          JN = WN
	        else
	          kN = s[28]
	          local RN, YN = kN.steal

	          if RN then
	            vN = s[67]
	            YN = (vN(pN, IN))
	          else
	            YN = RN
	          end

	          JN = YN and "filter"
	        end

	        kN = nil

	        if JN then
	          kN = JN
	        else
	          vN = s[15]
	          WN = vN.ufoEgg

	          if WN then
	            local RN = s[15]
	            kN = RN.ufoEgg(IN, LN)
	          else
	            kN = WN
	          end
	        end

	        JN = nil

	        if kN then
	          JN = kN
	        else
	          vN = s[67]
	          JN = vN(pN, IN) and "filter"
	        end

	        vN, WN = zN ~= nil

	        if vN then
	          WN = vN
	        else
	          kN = s[15]
	          local pN = kN.skip[LN]
	          local RN = if pN then not pN else JN

	          if RN then
	            local YN, SN = JN == "ufo"

	            if YN then
	              SN = YN
	            else
	              pN = s[28]
	              SN = not pN.zones
	            end

	            if SN then
	              WN = SN
	            else
	              YN = s[28]
	              WN = YN.zones[tostring(GN.AreaId):gsub("%s", "")]
	            end
	          else
	            WN = RN
	          end
	        end

	        IN.tg = WN or false
	        IN.kind = (IN.tg and JN) or nil
	        kN, vN = (GN.State == "Carried") and tonumber(GN.CarrierUserId)

	        if kN then
	          WN = s[1]
	          vN = kN ~= WN.UserId
	        else
	          vN = kN
	        end

	        IN.held = (vN and a:GetPlayerByUserId(kN)) or nil
	        local pN, kN, RN, YN = DN(GN), IN.n, IN.tg

	        if RN then
	          WN = s[15]
	          YN = ((100 - (WN.rank["Steal:" .. JN] or 9)) * 1000) - (zN or 0)
	        else
	          YN = RN
	        end

	        GN, WN = YN or 0, -IN.v
	        IN.Uid = LN
	        IN.Pos = pN
	        IN.Name = kN
	        IN.b = GN
	        IN.lo = WN

	        if IN.tg then
	          if JN == "ufo" then
	            vN = s[15]
	            pN = vN.UFO.st.Ufo
	            IN.b = IN.b + ((9000 - math.abs(IN.Pos.X - ((pN and pN.Position.X) or 0))) / 10)
	          end
	        end

	        if _N == "near" then
	          zN = nil

	          if wN then
	            pN = s[29]
	            zN = -pN(IN.Pos.X - wN.X, 0, IN.Pos.Z - wN.Z).Magnitude
	          else
	            zN = wN
	          end

	          IN.near = zN or 0
	        end

	        if _N == "mv" then
	          if not (IN.mv) then
	            IN.mv = ((#IN.m > 0) and (IN.v / math.max(t(IN.cat, IN.s, {}), 1))) or 1
	          end
	        end

	        return IN
	      end

	      local function pN(GN, LN)
	        local vN, kN = {}, os.clock()

	        if if GN then not GN else w() <= 0 then
	          QN = kN
	          local _N = KN
	          local wN, WN = not _N

	            WN = wN
	          else
	            local IN = s[26]
	            WN = IN("Cycle", "IsNightPhase", f())
	          end

	            wN, _N = pairs, s[23]
	            local WN = y[91](_N())

	            for _N, IN in wN(y:Co(WN)) do
	              if type(IN) == "table" then
	                if IN.Uid then
	                  _N = XN
	                  _N[IN.Uid] = true
	                end
	              end
	            end
	          end

	          if not (LN) then
	            return vN
	          end
	        end

	        if (kN - QN) > 8 then
	          KN = false
	        end

	        local KN = s[28]
	        local _N, wN = KN.index and tN(), s[15]
	        local tN, WN = wN.wantEgg, s[28]
	        local IN = fN[WN.order] or "v"
	        WN, KN, wN = (kN - QN) > 8, {}, s[19]
	        local QN = wN()
	        wN = nil

	        if QN then
	          local zN = s[19]
	          wN = zN().Position
	        else
	          wN = QN
	        end

	        local zN, JN = pairs, s[23]
	        local RN, YN = y[91](JN()), 0

	        for SN, xN in zN(y:Co(RN)) do
	          SN = (type(xN) == "table") and xN.Uid
	          QN, JN = type(SN) == "string"

	          if QN then
	            local RN, jN = (xN.State == "Slot") or (xN.State == "Dropped")

	            if RN then
	              jN = RN
	            else
	              local cN = s[15]
	              jN = (SN == cN.tpKeep) and (xN.State == "GuardCarried")
	            end

	            RN = nil

	            if jN then
	              RN = jN
	            else
	              local cN = s[15]
	              RN = ((SN == cN.mgUid) and (xN.State ~= "Carried")) and (xN.State ~= "Claimed")
	            end

	            jN = nil

	            if RN then
	              jN = RN
	            else
	              local cN

	              if GN then
	                local VN = s[15]
	                cN = SN == VN.curUid
	              else
	                cN = GN
	              end

	              jN = cN and (xN.State == "Carried")
	            end

	            if jN then
	              JN = jN
	            else
	              local jN, cN = xN.State == "Carried"

	              if jN then
	                RN = tonumber(xN.CarrierUserId)
	                local VN

	                if RN then
	                  VN = RN
	                else
	                  local RN = s[1]
	                  VN = RN.UserId
	                end

	                local RN = s[1]
	                cN = VN ~= RN.UserId
	              else
	                cN = jN
	              end

	              if cN then
	                jN = nil

	                if GN then
	                  jN = GN
	                else
	                  local RN = s[28]
	                  jN = RN.thief
	                end

	                if jN then
	                  JN = jN
	                else
	                  local RN
	                  RN, JN = s[15], table.find(s[15].pick, SN)
	                end
	              else
	                JN = cN
	              end
	            end
	          else
	            JN = QN
	          end

	          if JN then
	            if SN:find("^FirstAreaEgg") then
	            else
	              if typeof(xN.BottomCFrame) == "CFrame" then
	                KN[SN] = true
	                YN = if XN[SN] then YN + 1 else YN
	                local JN

	                if GN then
	                  JN = GN
	                else
	                  local RN = (EN[SN] or 0) < kN

	                  if RN then
	                    local jN = WN or not XN[SN]

	                    if jN then
	                      JN = jN
	                    else
	                      local XN
	                      XN, JN = s[15], table.find(s[15].pick, SN)
	                    end
	                  else
	                    JN = RN
	                  end
	                end

	                if JN then
	                  local XN = HN(xN, SN, _N, tN, IN, wN)

	                  if GN then
	                    vN[#vN + 1] = XN
	                  elseif XN.tg then
	                    local HN = s[28]

	                    if HN.steal then
	                      vN[#vN + 1] = XN
	                    elseif XN.kind == "wisp" then
	                      vN[#vN + 1] = XN
	                    elseif XN.kind == "pick" then
	                      vN[#vN + 1] = XN
	                    elseif XN.kind == "ufo" then
	                      vN[#vN + 1] = XN
	                    end
	                  end
	                end
	              else
	              end
	            end
	          else
	          end
	        end

	        if LN then
	          wN = s[15]
	          wN.fieldFresh = YN <= 2
	        end

	        for XN in next, rN, nil do
	          if not (KN[XN]) then
	            rN[XN] = nil
	          end
	        end

	        wN = s[15]
	        GN = #wN.pick

	        for XN = GN, 1, -1 do
	          zN = s[15]

	          if not (KN[zN.pick[XN]]) then
	            table.remove(s[15].pick, XN)
	          end
	        end

	        kN, LN = next, s[15]
	        WN = LN.skip

	        for XN in kN, WN, nil do
	          if not (KN[XN]) then
	            QN = s[15]
	            QN.skip[XN] = nil
	          end
	        end

	        table.sort(vN, function(XN, QN)
	          if XN.b ~= QN.b then
	            return XN.b > QN.b
	          end

	          if XN[IN] ~= QN[IN] then
	            return XN[IN] > QN[IN]
	          end
	          return XN.v > QN.v
	        end)

	        return vN
	      end

	      local XN = s[15]

	        local XN = s[28]

	      end

	      local XN = s[15]

	      XN.stealSort = function(XN)
	        local QN = s[28]
	        local rN = fN[QN.order] or "v"

	        table.sort(XN, function(fN, QN)
	          if fN.b ~= QN.b then
	            return fN.b > QN.b
	          end

	          if (fN[rN] or 0) ~= (QN[rN] or 0) then
	            return (fN[rN] or 0) > (QN[rN] or 0)
	          end
	          return fN.v > QN.v
	        end)

	        return XN
	      end

	      local fN
	      local XN = 0

	        if fN then
	          if (os.clock() - XN) < 0.3 then

	        local rN, HN, KN = pN(false), os
	        KN, fN = HN.clock(), rN
	        XN = KN
	        rN = fN
	        return rN
	      end

	      NN = s[15]

	      local pN = 0

	      local function NN()
	        local fN = 0

	        for XN in pairs(v()) do
	          fN += 1
	        end

	      end

	      local function oN(fN)
	        if (os.clock() - pN) < 1 then
	          return true
	        end
	        local XN, rN = pairs, y[91](v())
	        local HN

	        for KN, tN in XN(y:Co(rN)) do
	          if type(tN) == "table" then
	            if tN.Placement then
	            else
	              local XN = s[15]

	              if XN.reserved[KN] then
	              else
	                local XN = s[15]
	                local GN, LN = XN.protect

	                if GN then
	                  XN = s[15]
	                  LN = XN.protect(KN, tN)
	                else
	                  LN = GN
	                end

	                if LN then
	                else
	                  XN = D(tN)

	                  if #XN.m == 0 then
	                    if XN.v < fN then
	                      GN = s[28]

	                      if XN.v < (GN.bagMax or (1 / 0)) then
	                        local tN = XN.v
	                        fN, HN = tN, KN
	                      else
	                      end
	                    else
	                    end
	                  else
	                  end
	                end
	              end
	            end
	          else
	          end
	        end

	        if not (HN) then
	          return false
	        end
	        p("RE/PetSatchel/SellSelection", {Eggs = {HN}, Assets = {}})
	        pN = os.clock()
	        rN = s[11]
	        rN("Sold a cheap egg to make room", "ok")
	        return true
	      end

	      local pN
	      local fN = s[15]

	      fN.bagCheck = function()
	        pN = nil
	        local fN = s[28]

	        if fN.bagSell then
	          if NN() then
	            oN(1 / 0)
	          end
	        end
	      end

	      bN = s[33]

	      if bN.Egg then
	        FN = s[33]

	        if FN.Egg.OwnerRefreshed then
	          ZN = s[33]

	          b(ZN.Egg.OwnerRefreshed, function(bN)
	            local fN = s[1]

	            if bN == fN.UserId then
	              local bN = s[28]

	              if bN.bagSell then
	                if not (pN) then
	                  pN = true
	                  task.defer(s[15].bagCheck)
	                end
	              end
	            end
	          end)
	        end
	      end

	      function PN.ready()
	        if w() <= 0 then
	          return nil
	        elseif (PN.full or 0) > os.clock() then
	          return nil
	        end

	        local bN = QN()[1]
	        if not (bN) then
	          return nil
	        end
	        local pN = s[15]
	        pN.stealNext = bN.kind
	        pN = s[15]
	        return pN.rank["Steal:" .. bN.kind] or 9
	      end

	      local bN = s[15]

	      bN.stealWants = function()
	        local bN = (PN.inq == true) or (s[4] == PN)
	        return bN
	      end

	      function PN.want()
	        local bN = s[15]
	        bN.curUid = nil
	        bN = w()

	        if bN <= 0 then
	          QN()
	          local pN = s[11]
	          return pN("Waiting for daytime")
	        end

	        if (os.clock() - yN) < 1.5 then
	          return "wait"
	        end

	        if (PN.full or 0) > os.clock() then
	          bN = s[11]
	          return bN(PN.fullTx or "Bag full, retrying soon", "warn")
	        end
	        bN = QN()
	        PN.empty = ((#bN == 0) and (PN.empty or os.clock())) or nil
	        if #bN == 0 then
	          local yN = s[11]
	          return yN("No egg matches your targets")
	        end
	        local yN, pN, fN = ipairs

	        for FN, XN in yN(bN) do
	          FN = XN.held
	          local QN, ZN = not FN

	          if FN then
	            local FN = s[15]
	            ZN = FN.bat()
	          else
	            ZN = QN
	          end

	          if ZN then
	            pN = pN or XN
	            fN = if PN.again then if XN.Uid == PN.again.uid then if PN.again.n < 3 then if XN.b >= pN.b then XN else fN else fN else fN else fN
	          else
	          end
	        end

	        pN = fN or pN
	        if not (pN) then
	          yN = s[11]
	          return yN("Matching eggs are in other players' hands")
	        end

	        if NN() then
	          bN = s[28]

	          if bN.bagSell then
	            if oN(pN.v) then
	              return "wait"
	            end
	          end

	          PN.full = os.clock() + 30
	          local NN, fN = s[11], s[28]
	          return NN((fN.bagSell and "Bag is full, no cheaper egg to sell") or "Bag full, place or sell eggs", "warn")
	        end

	        bN, yN = s[15]
	        yN, bN.rank.Steal = s[15], s[15].rank["Steal:" .. pN.kind] or 9
	        return pN
	      end

	      local function yN(bN)
	        local pN = s[23]
	        local NN = pN()
	        if type(NN[bN]) == "table" then
	          return NN[bN]
	        end

	        for pN, pN in pairs(NN) do
	          if type(pN) == "table" then
	            if pN.Uid == bN then
	              return pN
	            end
	          end
	        end
	      end

	      local function bN(pN)
	        local NN = yN(pN)
	        local fN, FN = (NN and (NN.State == "Carried")) and a:GetPlayerByUserId(tonumber(NN.CarrierUserId) or 0)

	        if fN then
	          NN = s[1]
	          FN = fN ~= NN
	        else
	          FN = fN
	        end

	        NN = (FN and fN.Character) and fN.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	        if NN then
	          return NN, fN
	        end
	        FN = workspace:FindFirstChild(pN)
	        if not FN then
	          return
	        end
	        pN, fN = ipairs, y[91](FN:GetDescendants())

	        for XN, QN in pN(y:Co(fN)) do
	          if (QN:IsA("JointInstance") or QN:IsA("WeldConstraint")) or QN:IsA("RigidConstraint") then
	            NN, XN = ipairs, {QN.Part0, QN.Part1}

	            for pN, QN in NN(XN) do
	              local XN, ZN = (QN and not QN:IsDescendantOf(FN)) and QN:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")

	              if XN then
	                pN = s[1]
	                ZN = XN ~= pN.Character
	              else
	                ZN = XN
	              end

	              pN = ZN and (a:GetPlayerFromCharacter(XN))
	              if pN then
	                return XN:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or QN, pN
	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        NN = FN:FindFirstChild("Hitbox", true)
	        local pN, XN, QN, ZN, rN = (NN and NN.Position) or FN:GetPivot().Position, ipairs, y[91](a:GetPlayers()), 40

	        for NN, HN in XN(y:Co(QN)) do
	          FN = s[1]
	          NN = ((HN ~= FN) and HN.Character) and HN.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

	          if NN then
	            if (NN.Position - pN).Magnitude < ZN then
	              fN = (NN.Position - pN).Magnitude
	              ZN, rN = fN, HN
	            else
	            end
	          else
	          end
	        end

	        if rN then
	          return rN.Character.HumanoidRootPart, rN
	        end
	      end

	      local function pN(NN)
	        local fN = yN(NN)
	        local FN, XN = fN and (fN.State == "Carried")

	        if FN then
	          NN = nil
	          NN, XN = s[1], (tonumber(fN.CarrierUserId) or 0) ~= s[1].UserId
	        else
	          XN = FN
	        end

	        return XN
	      end

	      local NN = {}

	      local function fN(FN, XN, QN)
	        local ZN, rN = FN.Uid, s[1]
	        local HN, KN = rN.Character, s[8]
	        local tN, GN = KN(), s[28]

	        if GN.thief or (FN.kind == "pick") then
	          rN = s[15]

	          if rN.bat() then
	            if (NN[ZN] or 0) > os.clock() then
	              return false, "Taken by someone"
	            end
	          else
	            return false, "Taken by someone"
	          end
	        else
	          return false, "Taken by someone"
	        end

	        GN = s[15]

	        if GN.invisible() then
	          rN = s[11]
	          rN("Turning visible to take " .. (FN.Name .. " back"))
	          KN = s[15]
	          KN.keepVis = os.clock() + 120
	          local LN = s[15]

	          if LN.body(false) then
	            if not (XN()) then
	              return false, "visible"
	            end
	          else
	            return false, "visible"
	          end
	        end

	        if s[13](XN) then
	          KN = s[14]
	          if not (KN(XN)) then
	            return false, "Stuck on treadmill"
	          end
	        else
	          return false, "Stuck on treadmill"
	        end

	        GN, rN = bN(ZN), s[19]
	        KN = rN()

	        if GN then
	          if KN then
	            if (GN.Position - KN.Position).Magnitude > 700 then
	              rN = s[11]
	              rN("Ragdoll jump to the carrier")

	              CN(function()
	                local LN = bN(ZN)
	                local vN

	                if LN then
	                  local kN
	                  kN, vN = s[29], LN.Position + s[29](0, 3, 0)
	                else
	                  vN = LN
	                end

	                return vN
	              end, XN)
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        rN = s[15]
	        rN.godHunt = true
	        local LN
	        KN = s[59]

	        local function vN(kN)
	          if LN then
	            pcall(s[15].huntStep, LN[1], LN[2], kN)
	          end
	        end

	        local kN, _N, wN = KN.PreSimulation:Connect(vN), os.clock(), false
	        rN, KN = 0, 0

	        while true do
	          vN = XN()
	          local WN

	          if vN then
	            local IN = s[1]
	            WN = IN.Character == HN
	          else
	            WN = vN
	          end

	          if not (WN and ((os.clock() - _N) < 90)) then
	            break
	          end
	          local IN, zN = s[25], wN
	          if IN(ZN) then
	            wN = true
	            break
	          end
	          IN = yN(ZN)
	          if if IN then IN.State ~= "Carried" else not IN then
	            wN = (IN ~= nil) and ((IN.State == "Slot") or (IN.State == "Dropped"))
	            break
	          end
	          WN, IN = bN(ZN)
	          local bN, JN = WN and IN

	          if bN then
	            local RN = s[21]
	            JN = not RN(WN.Position)
	          else
	            JN = bN
	          end

	          LN = (JN and {IN, WN}) or nil

	          if not LN and tN then
	            tN.PlatformStand = false
	          end

	          rN = (WN and 0) or rN
	          bN = s[11]
	          bN((WN and ((LN and ("Chasing %s for %s"):format(IN.DisplayName, FN.Name)) or "The carrier reached their base")) or "Looking for the egg carrier")
	          IN = s[20]
	          JN = IN()

	          if WN then
	          else
	            rN += JN
	            if rN >= 3 then
	              wN, GN = zN, WN
	              break
	            end
	          end

	          KN = ((WN and not LN) and (KN + JN)) or 0

	          if KN > 1.5 then
	            NN[ZN] = os.clock() + 60
	            wN, GN = zN, WN
	            break
	          end

	          GN = WN
	        end

	        LN = nil
	        kN:Disconnect()
	        KN = s[15]
	        KN.godHunt = false

	        if tN then
	          tN.PlatformStand = false
	        end

	        tN, kN = wN and (XN())

	        if tN then
	          vN = s[1]
	          kN = vN.Character == HN
	        else
	          kN = tN
	        end

	        if kN then
	          local function bN(LN)
	            local kN = s[11]
	            kN(FN.Name .. " went back to its nest, going for it")
	            FN.Pos = LN.BottomCFrame.Position
	            LN, kN = gN(FN, XN)
	            if LN then
	              return MN(ZN, XN, FN.Pos)
	            end
	            return LN, kN
	          end

	          vN = s[25]
	          KN, HN = vN(ZN), yN(ZN)

	          if (if KN then not KN else HN) and (HN.State == "Slot") then
	            KN = (bN(HN))
	          else
	            if KN then
	            else
	              if HN then
	                GN = s[11]
	                GN("The egg fell, taking it")
	                local GN, LN, vN, kN = DN(HN), s[17], s[18], s[29]
	                LN(vN(GN + kN(0, 3, 0)))
	                KN = (MN(ZN, XN, GN))
	              else
	              end
	            end

	            if KN then
	              _N = s[26]
	              _N("Egg", "DropFieldEgg", "PlayerRequest")
	              rN = 0

	              while true do
	                tN = s[20]
	                rN += tN()
	                HN = yN(ZN)
	                local tN = s[45]
	                if ((rN > (1 + tN())) or not HN) or (HN.State ~= "Carried") then
	                  break
	                end
	              end

	              if HN then
	                if HN.State == "Dropped" then
	                  local rN
	                  KN, rN = MN(ZN, XN, DN(HN)), yN(ZN)
	                  HN = rN
	                else
	                end
	              else
	              end

	              local rN = s[25]
	              KN = if rN(ZN) then KN else if HN then if HN.State == "Slot" then bN(HN) else KN else KN
	            else
	            end
	          end

	          if KN then
	            local bN, rN = hN(ZN, XN)

	            if not (bN) then
	              if XN() then
	                if (QN or 0) < 4 then
	                  if pN(ZN) then
	                    return fN(FN, XN, (QN or 0) + 1)
	                  end
	                end
	              end
	            end

	            return bN, rN
	          end
	        end

	        NN[ZN] = math.max(NN[ZN] or 0, os.clock() + 0.5)
	        return false, (wN and "Could not take the fallen egg") or "The carrier got away with the egg"
	      end

	      function PN.run(bN, NN)
	        local FN = s[15]
	        FN.stealKind = bN.kind

	        if bN.kind == "ufo" then
	          local XN, QN = s[15]
	          QN, XN.UFO.d0 = s[15], s[15].UFO.st.Delivered or 0
	        end

	        FN = s[15]
	        FN.flyY = nil
	        FN = s[15]
	        FN.curArea = tostring(bN.a)
	        PN.cv = bN.v
	        FN = s[15]
	        FN.curUid = bN.Uid
	        FN = s[15]

	        if FN.hud then
	          local XN = s[15]
	          XN.hud.Refresh()
	        end

	        FN = s[25]
	        local XN = FN()
	        FN = nil

	        if XN then
	          local QN = s[25]
	          FN = not QN(bN.Uid)
	        else
	          FN = XN
	        end

	        if FN then
	          XN = s[26]
	          XN("Egg", "DropFieldEgg", "PlayerRequest")
	          local QN = 0

	          while true do
	            local ZN = s[25]
	            if not (ZN() and (QN < 1.5)) then
	              break
	            end
	            ZN = s[20]
	            QN += ZN()
	          end
	        end

	        XN = s[43]
	        XN.cur = bN.Name
	        FN = s[15]

	        if FN.greenLight then
	          XN = s[15]
	          XN.greenLight(bN.Uid, NN)
	        end

	        XN = s[1]
	        local QN, ZN, rN, HN, KN, tN = XN.Character, NN, false
	        FN = s[15]
	        FN.tpKeep = bN.Uid
	        XN = s[28]
	        local DN, GN = XN.adminSteal

	        if DN then
	          GN = DN
	        else
	          FN = nil
	          FN, GN = s[15], bN.Uid == s[15].mgUid
	        end

	        XN, FN = GN and (TN())

	        if XN then
	          DN = mN[41]
	          FN = not DN()
	        else
	          FN = XN
	        end

	        if FN then
	          HN = uN(bN, NN)

	          if HN then
	            GN = s[11]
	            GN("Stole " .. bN.Name, "ok")
	            return
	          end

	          XN = s[25]

	          if XN() then
	            DN = s[26]
	            DN("Egg", "DropFieldEgg", "PlayerRequest")
	          end

	          if NN() then
	            local uN = s[1]
	            if uN.Character ~= QN then
	              return
	            end
	          else
	            return
	          end
	        else
	          local uN = s[28]

	          if uN.adminSteal then
	            local uN = mN[41]

	            if uN() then
	            else
	              local function uN()
	                local LN = s[25]

	                if not (LN()) then
	                  if TN() then
	                    rN = true
	                    return false
	                  end
	                end

	                return ZN()
	              end

	              NN = uN
	            end
	          else
	          end
	        end

	        if bN.held then
	          KN = "Taken by someone"
	        else
	          FN, XN = bN.kind == "ufo"

	          if FN then
	            GN = s[15]
	            XN = GN.ufoTP(bN)
	          else
	            XN = FN
	          end

	          DN = nil

	          if XN then
	            DN = XN
	          else
	            local uN, LN = bN.kind ~= "ufo"

	            if uN then
	              local vN = s[28]
	              LN = vN.moveTo == "Teleport"
	            else
	              LN = uN
	            end

	            uN = nil

	            if LN then
	              local vN
	              vN, uN = s[15], table.find(s[15].TZ, tostring(bN.a))
	            else
	              uN = LN
	            end

	            LN = nil

	            if uN then
	              local vN
	              vN, LN = s[28], table.find(s[28].tpZones or {}, tostring(bN.a))
	            else
	              LN = uN
	            end

	            uN = nil

	            if LN then
	              local vN = s[19]
	              uN = (vN())
	            else
	              uN = LN
	            end

	            if uN then
	              LN = s[19]
	              DN = (LN().Position - bN.Pos).Magnitude >= 600
	            else
	              DN = uN
	            end
	          end

	          if DN then
	            local uN = s[15]
	            tN = true
	            uN.tpKeep = bN.Uid
	            local LN, vN = BN(bN, NN)

	            if vN == "grabbed" then
	              HN, KN = hN(bN.Uid, NN)
	            else
	              HN, KN = LN, vN
	            end

	            if HN then
	            else
	              if NN() then
	                LN = s[1]

	                if LN.Character == QN then
	                  if pN(bN.Uid) then
	                    HN, KN = fN(bN, NN)
	                  else
	                    if KN == "Dropped on the way" then
	                      uN = mN[49]

	                      if uN(bN.Uid, NN) then
	                        HN, KN = hN(bN.Uid, NN)
	                      else
	                      end
	                    else
	                    end
	                  end
	                else
	                end
	              else
	              end
	            end
	          else
	            for BN = 1, 2, 1 do
	              local uN, LN = gN(bN, NN)

	              if uN then
	                HN, KN = uN, LN
	                break
	              elseif LN == "Taken by someone" then
	                HN, KN = uN, LN
	                break
	              else
	                BN = s[1]

	                if BN.Character ~= QN then
	                  HN, KN = uN, LN
	                  break
	                elseif NN() then
	                  local gN = s[30]
	                  if not (gN()) then
	                    HN, KN = uN, LN
	                    break
	                  end
	                else
	                  HN, KN = uN, LN
	                  break
	                end
	              end

	              BN = s[11]
	              BN("Knocked down, trying again")
	              HN, KN = uN, LN
	            end

	            if HN then
	              local BN = s[23]

	              for gN, gN in pairs(BN()) do
	                if type(gN) == "table" then
	                  if gN.Uid == bN.Uid then
	                    if gN.State ~= "Slot" then
	                      if gN.State ~= "Dropped" then
	                        local BN, gN = false, "Taken by someone"
	                        HN, KN = BN, gN
	                      else
	                      end
	                    else
	                    end
	                  else
	                  end
	                else
	                end
	              end
	            else
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        if KN == "Taken by someone" then
	          HN, KN = fN(bN, NN)
	        else
	          if HN then
	            if tN then
	            else
	              XN, GN = MN(bN.Uid, NN, bN.Pos, true)
	              DN, FN = nil

	              if XN then
	                DN, FN = hN(bN.Uid, NN)
	              else
	                DN, FN = XN, GN
	              end

	              local BN = s[15]
	              BN.tainted = ((if DN then not DN else bN.kind ~= "ufo") and ((FN == "Egg went back to its nest") or (FN == "Dropped on the way"))) or nil

	              if DN then
	                HN, KN = DN, FN
	              else
	                if NN() then
	                  BN = s[28]

	                  if BN.thief then
	                    if pN(bN.Uid) then
	                      HN, KN = fN(bN, NN)
	                    else
	                      HN, KN = DN, FN
	                    end
	                  else
	                    HN, KN = DN, FN
	                  end
	                else
	                  HN, KN = DN, FN
	                end
	              end
	            end
	          else
	          end
	        end

	        GN = nil

	        if rN then
	          XN = s[25]
	          GN = not XN()
	        else
	          GN = rN
	        end

	        if GN then
	          if ZN() then
	            XN = s[1]

	            if XN.Character == QN then
	              DN = s[11]
	              DN("Admin treadmill is up, switching to Instant steal")
	              return PN.run(bN, ZN)
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        DN = s[25]

	        if DN() then
	          XN = s[1]

	          if XN.Character == QN then
	            if not (HN) then
	              GN = mN[41]

	              if not (GN()) then
	                FN = s[26]
	                FN("Egg", "DropFieldEgg", "PlayerRequest")
	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        GN = s[28]

	        if GN.steal then
	          if not (HN) then
	            if bN.kind ~= "ufo" then
	              FN = s[15]
	              FN.stealRetry = os.clock() + 6
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        if HN then
	          DN = s[15]
	          DN.tpKeep = nil
	        end

	        if not (HN) then
	          XN, GN = yN(bN.Uid), 0

	          if XN then
	            if XN.State == "Carried" then
	              FN = s[1]

	              if tonumber(XN.CarrierUserId) ~= FN.UserId then
	                DN = s[15]
	                DN.tpKeep = nil
	              end
	            end
	          else
	            local BN = s[15]
	            BN.tpKeep = nil
	          end

	          local BN

	          if tN then
	            local gN
	            gN, BN = s[28], tostring(s[28].tpDel):lower() ~= "walk"
	          else
	            BN = tN
	          end

	          if BN then
	            GN = 12
	          else
	            if XN then
	              if XN.State == "GuardCarried" then
	                local gN = s[11]
	                gN("Waiting for the guard to bring " .. (bN.Name .. " back"))
	              end
	            end
	          end

	          while NN() and (GN < 12) do
	            BN = yN(bN.Uid)

	            if BN then
	              if not (BN.State == "GuardCarried") then
	                break
	              end
	            else
	              break
	            end

	            local yN = s[20]
	            GN += yN()
	          end
	        end

	        if KN == "visible" then
	          return
	        end
	        XN, FN = ((PN.again and (PN.again.uid == bN.Uid)) and PN.again.n) or 0, KN == "Egg went back to its nest"
	        DN = FN or ((HN == nil) and (KN == nil))
	        GN = (DN and ((((PN.again and (PN.again.uid == bN.Uid)) and PN.again.nb) or 0) + 1)) or 0
	        PN.again = ((((HN == false) or (KN ~= nil)) or DN) and {uid = bN.Uid, n = (FN and 0) or (XN + 1), nb = GN}) or nil

	        if GN >= 3 then
	          if not (tN) then
	            EN[bN.Uid] = os.clock() + 30
	            PN.again = nil
	          end
	        end

	                yN += mN()

	    P("12_hud#1", function()
	      local yN = e("TweenService")
	      local qN = Color3.fromRGB
	      local mN = UDim2.fromScale
	      local BN = UDim2.fromOffset
	      local bN = Vector2.new
	      local gN = T.q
	      local oN = TweenInfo.new
	      local pN = Enum.EasingStyle
	      local NN = Enum.EasingDirection
	      local fN, hN, FN = (gethui and gethui()) or e("CoreGui"), {"Best Rarity", "Biggest Weight", "Best Mutation", "Highest Value", "Lowest Value"}, {}
	      FN[hN[1]] = "Rarity"
	      FN[hN[2]] = "Weight"
	      FN[hN[3]] = "Mutation"
	      FN[hN[4]] = "Value"
	      FN[hN[5]] = "Lowest"
	      local MN = W.ByPath["Farm > Auto Steal > Steal Priority"]
	      local uN = (MN and MN.Value) or hN[4]

	      if MN then
	        table.insert(MN.ls, function(EN)
	          uN = EN
	        end)
	      end

	      local function EN()
	        return m:GenerateGUID(false):gsub("-", ""):sub(1, 14)
	      end

	      local function XN(QN, ZN)
	        return QN and (QN:FindFirstChild(ZN))
	      end

	      local function QN(ZN)
	        return ZN:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
	      end

	      local function ZN(rN)
	        return (rN and rN.Value) == true
	      end

	      local function rN()
	        local HN, KN, tN, DN = {}, {}, 0, s[15]

	        for GN, GN in ipairs(DN.pick) do
	          if not (KN[GN]) then
	            KN[GN] = true
	            HN[#HN + 1] = GN
	          end
	        end

	        DN = s[28]

	        if DN.steal then
	          local GN, LN = s[15], s[15]

	          for vN, kN in ipairs(GN.stealSort(LN.allEggs())) do
	            if kN.tg then
	              if kN.kind ~= "ufo" then
	                if KN[kN.Uid] then
	                else
	                  if tN < 8 then
	                    vN = tN + 1
	                    KN[kN.Uid] = true
	                    HN[#HN + 1] = kN.Uid
	                    tN = vN
	                  else
	                  end
	                end
	              else
	              end
	            else
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        DN = s[15]
	        KN, tN = DN.stealKind ~= "ufo"

	        if KN then
	          DN = s[15]
	          tN = DN.curUid
	        else
	          tN = KN
	        end

	        return HN, tN or nil
	      end

	      local function HN()
	        local KN = s[15]
	        KN.rank.Steal = 0
	        KN = s[15]

	        if KN.order then
	          local KN = s[15]
	          KN.order()
	        end
	      end

	      local function KN(tN)
	        local DN, DN = s[15], table.find(s[15].pick, tN)

	        if DN then
	          table.remove(s[15].pick, DN)
	        end
	      end

	      local function tN(DN)
	        KN(DN)
	        table.insert(s[15].pick, 1, DN)
	        local GN = s[15]
	        GN.skip[DN] = nil
	        HN()
	        GN = s[15]
	        GN.retarget()
	      end

	      local function DN(GN)
	        KN(GN)
	        local KN = s[15]

	        if KN.curUid == GN then
	          local KN = s[15]
	          KN.stopSteal()
	        end
	      end

	      local function KN()
	        local GN = s[28]

	        if not (GN.steal) then
	          local GN = s[15]

	          if GN.autoSteal then
	            local GN = s[15]
	            GN.autoSteal:Set(true)
	          end
	        end
	      end

	      local function GN(LN, vN)
	        local kN = rN()
	        local _N = table.find(kN, LN)
	        LN = _N and (_N + vN)

	        if LN then
	          if not (kN[LN]) then
	            return
	          end
	        else
	          return
	        end

	        kN[_N], kN[LN] = kN[LN], kN[_N]
	        local vN, wN = math.max(_N, LN), #kN

	        for _N = 1, wN, 1 do
	          LN = nil
	          LN, vN = s[15], if table.find(s[15].pick, kN[_N]) then math.max(vN, _N) else vN

	      local function HN(KN)
	        local tN = (((KN >= 1000) and "%.0f") or "%.2f"):format(KN)
	        local KN, DN = tN:match("^(%-?%d+)(%.%d+)$")
	        local GN, wN = 1, KN or tN

	        while GN > 0 do
	          wN, GN = wN:gsub("^(%-?%d+)(%d%d%d)", "%1,%2")
	        end

	        return wN .. ((DN or "") .. " Kg")

	              qN = 0
	            else

	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end)

	      end

	        if v3 then
	          v3:Destroy()
	        end

	        fn4()
	        v3, v4, v5 = nil, nil, nil
	      end)

	        gN(fn6)
	      end, Refresh = M1}

	            end

	          end

	        end

	    end)

	    P("14_pred#1", function()
	      local yN, qN = {EggState = "Egg", EggRecords = "Rec", Assets = "Assets", FuseKernel = "Fuse", Mutations = "Mut"}, {}
	      local mN, BN, bN = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), {}

	      local gN, oN, pN, NN, fN = s[15], s[15], setmetatable, {__index = function(hN, FN)
	        if FN == "Save" then

	          return MN and setmetatable({Get = function(...)
	            return MN.Peek(...)
	          end}, {__index = MN})
	        end

	        if MN then
	          hN = s[33]
	          uN = hN[yN[FN]]
	        else
	          uN = MN
	        end

	        return uN
	      end}

	      fN, gN.PS = pN({}, NN), qN
	      oN.GM = fN

	      qN.Seq = function(...)
	        local yN, gN = {...}, {}

	        for oN = 1, #yN, 4 do
	          gN[#gN + 1] = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(yN[oN], Color3.fromRGB(yN[oN + 1], yN[oN + 2], yN[oN + 3]))
	        end

	        return ColorSequence.new(gN)
	      end

	      qN.NameFont, qN.RarityFont = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.ExtraBold), Font.new("rbxassetid://12187365977", Enum.FontWeight.Bold)
	      qN.SecretGradient = qN.Seq(0, 255, 255, 255, 0.2, 206, 212, 224, 0.42, 74, 80, 94, 0.58, 42, 46, 56, 0.78, 158, 166, 182, 1, 250, 252, 255)
	      qN.SecretRotation = 90

	      qN.Paint = function(yN, gN)
	        return ("<font color=\"%s\">%s</font>"):format(yN, gN)
	      end

	      qN.Bold = function(yN)
	        return "<b>" .. (tostring(yN) .. "</b>")
	      end

	      qN.Escape = function(yN)
	        return (tostring(yN):gsub("[<>&]", {["<"] = "&lt;", [">"] = "&gt;", ["&"] = "&amp;"}))
	      end

	      local yN = qN.Paint("#7A8CC0", "  " .. (utf8.char(8226) .. "  "))

	      qN.Separator = function()
	        return yN
	      end

	      qN.FormatRate = function(yN)
	        yN = tonumber(yN) or 0
	        return (((((yN >= 1000000000000) and ("%.2fT/s"):format(yN / 1000000000000)) or ((yN >= 1000000000) and ("%.2fB/s"):format(yN / 1000000000))) or ((yN >= 1000000) and ("%.2fM/s"):format(yN / 1000000))) or ((yN >= 1000) and ("%.1fK/s"):format(yN / 1000))) or ("%d/s"):format(math.floor(yN))
	      end

	      qN.FormatWeight = function(yN)
	        yN = tonumber(yN) or 0
	        local gN = (((yN >= 1000) and "%.0f") or "%.2f"):format(yN)
	        local yN, oN, pN = gN:match("^(%-?%d+)(%.%d+)$")
	        yN = yN or gN

	        repeat
	          yN, pN = yN:gsub("^(%-?%d+)(%d%d%d)", "%1,%2")
	        until pN == 0

	        return yN .. ((oN or "") .. " Kg")
	      end

	      qN.FormatClock = function(yN)
	        yN = math.max(0, math.floor(tonumber(yN) or 0))
	        return ("%02dh %02dm %02ds"):format(yN // 3600, (yN % 3600) // 60, yN % 60)
	      end

	      qN.ScaleFactor = function(yN)
	        return ((yN > 5) and (((yN / 5) ^ 1.2) * 19.637875755794113)) or (yN ^ 1.85)
	      end

	      qN.MutationMultiplier = function(yN)
	        local gN, oN = pcall(function()
	          local pN = s[33]
	          return pN.Mut.EarningsFor(((type(yN) == "table") and yN) or {})
	        end)

	        return ((gN and (type(oN) == "number")) and oN) or 1
	      end

	      local yN, gN = {Golden = "#FFD34D", Silver = "#E6EEF7", Sakura = "#FF9ED8", GreatBloom = "#7CFFC4", Boss = "#FF7A7A", Monstrous = "#C08BFF"}, {"#FF6B6B", "#FFB36B", "#FFF06B", "#6BFF8A", "#6BC8FF", "#B96BFF"}

	        local pN = {}

	        for NN, fN in ipairs(((type(oN) == "table") and oN) or {}) do

	          if (fN == "Rainbow") or (fN == "Prismatic") then

	              hN[FN] = qN.Paint(gN[((FN - 1) % 6) + 1], oN:sub(FN, FN))
	            end

	            pN[NN] = qN.Bold(table.concat(hN))
	          else
	            pN[NN] = qN.Bold(qN.Paint(yN[fN] or "#8FE3FF", qN.Escape(oN)))
	          end
	        end

	        return table.concat(pN, " ")
	      end

	      local function yN(gN)
	        if type(gN) == "table" then
	          if typeof(gN.RarityGradient) == "Instance" then
	            return gN.RarityGradient
	          end
	        end

	        if bN == nil then
	          local oN = q:FindFirstChild("Assets")
	          local pN = oN and (oN:FindFirstChild("UI"))
	          bN = (pN and pN:FindFirstChild("RarityGradients")) or false
	        end

	        if bN then
	          if type(gN) ~= "table" then
	            return nil
	          end
	        else
	          return nil
	        end

	        local oN = bN
	        local bN = oN:FindFirstChild(tostring((gN._id or gN.DisplayName) or ""))
	        return (bN and bN:FindFirstChild("RarityGradient")) or nil
	      end

	      local function bN(gN)
	        return gN:lower():gsub("[^%a%d]", "")
	      end

	      local function gN(oN, pN)
	        pN = bN(pN)

	        for NN, fN in pairs(oN) do
	          if type(fN) == "table" then
	            local oN, hN = ipairs, {NN, fN._id or "", fN.DisplayName or "", ((type(fN.Egg) == "table") and fN.Egg.ModelName) or ""}

	            for NN, NN in oN(hN) do
	              NN = tostring(NN)

	              if NN ~= "" then
	                if bN(NN) == pN then
	                  return fN
	                end
	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end
	      end

	      qN.AssetInfo = function(bN)
	        local oN = tostring(bN)
	        if BN[oN] then
	          return BN[oN]
	        end
	        bN = s[33]
	        local pN, NN = bN.Assets

	        if pN then
	          bN = s[33]
	          NN = bN.Assets.Directory
	        else
	          NN = pN
	        end

	        NN = ((type(NN) == "table") and NN) or {}
	        pN = NN[oN] or gN(NN, oN)
	        pN = ((type(pN) == "table") and pN) or {}
	        bN = ((type(pN.Rarity) == "table") and pN.Rarity) or nil
	        local gN, NN = (bN and tostring((bN.DisplayName or bN._id) or "Common")) or "Common", ((bN and (typeof(bN.Color) == "Color3")) and bN.Color) or mN
	        local mN = {Name = tostring(pN.DisplayName or oN), Category = oN, Rarity = gN, Up = gN:upper(), RarityNumber = (bN and tonumber(bN.RarityNumber or bN.Rank)) or 0, Color = NN, Hex = "#" .. NN:ToHex():upper(), Gradient = yN(bN), EarningRate = tonumber(pN.EarningRate) or 0, Icon = (((type(pN.Icon) == "string") and (pN.Icon ~= "")) and pN.Icon) or nil}
	        BN[oN] = mN
	        return mN
	      end

	      qN.Income = function(yN, mN, BN)
	        if type(mN) ~= "number" then
	          return 0
	        elseif mN <= 0 then
	          return 0
	        end

	        return math.max(math.round((yN.EarningRate * qN.ScaleFactor(mN)) * qN.MutationMultiplier(BN)), 1)
	      end

	      qN.IsShown = function(yN)
	        if typeof(yN) ~= "Instance" then
	          return false
	        elseif not (yN:IsDescendantOf(l)) then
	          return false
	        end

	        while yN do
	          if yN:IsA("GuiObject") then
	            if not (yN.Visible) then
	              return false
	            end
	          end

	          if yN:IsA("LayerCollector") then
	            return yN.Enabled
	          end
	          yN = yN.Parent
	        end

	        return false
	      end
	    end)

	      local function bN()
	        xN = false
	        local gN, uN, EN = math.max(1, (zN - 0.7) - 0.5), cN(LN.v), 2.6

	        if uN then
	          GN.su = uN + 0.1
	        else

	        end

	        uN = math.min(GN.su or 4, gN * 0.45)

	        tN.Width = math.max(1, (gN - uN) - 0.3)
	        XN(tN)
	        GN.v({X = (0.7 + gN) - uN, Width = math.max(0.5, uN)})
	        GN.d({Width = gN})

	        if wN then
	          XN, tN = cN(LN.x), math.max(1, (zN - ZN) - 0.5)

	          if XN then
	            GN.cu = XN + 0.9
	          else
	            xN = true
	          end

	          GN.l({Width = gN})
	          local gN, uN = GN.n, {}
	          uN.Width = math.max(1, (tN - XN) - 0.3)
	          gN(uN)
	          gN, uN = GN.h, {}
	          uN.X, uN.Width = (ZN + tN) - XN, XN
	          gN(uN)
	          GN.x({Width = XN})
	          GN.f({Width = tN})
	          EN = 5.9
	        else
	        end

	        GN.c({Width = zN, Height = EN})
	        GN.a({Height = EN})

	        if EN ~= SN then
	          SN = EN
	          WN:SetContentLines(EN)
	        end
	      end

	      mN:Surface({Name = "Finder Status", ShowTitle = false, Layout = "free", Style = {TextScale = 1, LineHeight = 1.1, MinLines = 2, MaxLines = 7, BackgroundTransparency = 0.5, TextColor = QN, TextStrokeTransparency = 0.7}, Build = function(gN)
	        WN = gN

	        local function uN(EN, XN, QN)
	          QN.Parent = QN.Parent or LN.c
	          QN.Wrap = false
	          local tN = gN[XN](gN, QN)
	          LN[EN] = tN

	          GN[EN] = function(EN)
	            local XN

	            for kN, wN in next, EN, nil do
	              if QN[kN] ~= wN then
	                XN = XN or {}
	                XN[kN] = wN
	              else
	              end
	            end

	            if XN then
	              tN.Set(XN)
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        uN("c", "Frame", {Height = 2.6, Corner = 0.35})
	        uN("a", "Frame", {Width = 0.22, Height = 2.6, Corner = 0.11, Background = "#9AA3B5"})

	        uN("d", "Text", {X = 0.7, Y = 0.3 + rN, Height = 1})

	        uN("n", "Text", {X = ZN, Y = 2.9, Height = rN, Scale = 1.2, Font = MN.NameFont, TextStrokeTransparency = 1, StrokeTransparency = 0.2, Visible = false})
	        uN("h", "Frame", {Y = 2.9 + ((rN - 0.95) / 2), Height = 0.95, Corner = 0.3, BackgroundTransparency = 0.35, StrokeThickness = 0.0909, Visible = false})
	        uN("x", "Text", {Parent = LN.h, Height = 0.95, Scale = 0.78, Align = "Center", Font = MN.RarityFont, TextStrokeTransparency = 1})
	        uN("f", "Text", {X = ZN, Y = 2.9 + rN, Height = 1, Visible = false})
	        IN = {GN.l, GN.n, GN.h, GN.f, GN.i}

	        gN:OnResize(function(uN, EN, XN)
	          if EN == RN then
	            if XN == YN then
	              return
	            end
	          end

	          uN = nil
	          uN, RN, YN = EN / math.max(XN, 1), EN, XN
	          zN, JN, vN = uN, XN, 2
	          uN, EN = GN.n, {}
	          EN.StrokeThickness = 0.9 / math.max(gN:TextSize(), 1)
	          uN(EN)
	        end)
	      end})

	      b(d, function(gN)
	        if WN then
	          if not (MN.IsShown(WN:Root())) then
	            return
	          end
	        else
	          return
	        end

	        _N += gN

	        if _N >= 1 then
	          _N = 0
	          pcall(VN)
	        end

	        pcall(DN)

	        if (vN > 0) or xN then
	          vN = math.max(vN - 1, 0)
	          pcall(bN)
	        end
	      end)

	      local bN, gN = {["Until An Egg Matches"] = HN, ["Steal Then Hop"] = KN, ["After A Rare Spawns"] = "A rare egg spawned"}, s[28]
	      gN.hopWhen = KN

	      mN:Dropdown("Hop Mode", {"Until An Egg Matches", "Steal Then Hop", "After A Rare Spawns"}, "Steal Then Hop", function(MN)
	        local uN, EN, XN = s[28], bN[MN]

	        if EN then
	          XN = EN
	        else
	          MN = s[28]
	          XN = MN.hopWhen
	        end

	        uN.hopWhen = XN
	      end, false, "When to move on to the next server", hN)

	      gN, yN = {}, s[15]
	      y:lo(gN, 0, table.unpack(yN.RANY, 2))
	      j(mN, "Rarity To Wait For", "hopRare", gN, "Cosmic", nil, false, "For After A Rare Spawns: this rarity or higher", hN)
	      local bN, MN = x(mN, "Sync With Auto Steal Filters", "hopSync", true, nil, "Changing a filter here also changes it in Auto Steal, and back", hN), {}

	        local QN = W.ByPath["Farm > Auto Steal > " .. XN]
	        if not QN then
	          return
	        end

	                QN:Set(ZN)
	                XN = false
	              end

	          local rN = s[28]

	          if rN.hopSync then
	            if not (XN) then
	              XN = true
	              EN:Set(ZN)
	              XN = false
	            end
	          end

	      local function yN()
	        local bN = s[28]
	        local hN, MN = bN.hopSync

	        if hN then
	          bN = s[15]
	          MN = bN.stealFilter
	        else
	          MN = hN
	        end

	        local hN = s[26]
	        qN.Window = hN("Cycle", "NextNightTime", f()) or (f() + 300)
	      end

	      gN = s[15]
	      local hN, MN = gN.onSteal, s[15]

	      MN.onSteal = function(gN)
	        local MN = s[28]

	        if MN.hopWhen == KN then
	          if type(gN) == "table" then
	            local MN, uN = {cat = gN.AssetCategory, r = H(gN.AssetCategory), v = 1 / 0, m = {}}, s[67]

	            if uN(yN(), MN) then
	              oN()
	              BN()
	            end
	          end

	          hN(gN)
	        end

	      H.on = function()
	        local BN = s[28]
	        return BN.autoHop
	      end

	      H.fn = function()
	        local H = w()
	        if H <= 0 then
	          return FN("Night, waiting for the field to reset", "night")
	        end
	        local BN = s[28]
	        if (os.clock() - mN) < BN.hopDelay then
	          return FN("Looking around this server", "scan")
	        end
	        BN = s[25]

	        if BN() then
	          return FN("Busy stealing", "steal")
	        elseif s[4] then
	          if s[4].name == "Steal" then
	            return FN("Busy stealing", "steal")
	          end
	        end

	        BN = s[15]
	        if BN.lock() then
	          return FN("Busy with an event, hop waits", "race")
	        end
	        BN = s[15]

	        if BN.starWants then
	          local mN = s[15]
	          if mN.starWants() then
	            return FN("Catching a Shooting Star, hop waits", "star")
	          end
	        end

	        BN = s[15]

	        if BN.ufoWants then
	          local mN = s[15]
	          if mN.ufoWants() then
	            return FN("UFO in this server, hop waits", "star")
	          end
	        end

	        local mN, gN, gN, hN = yN(), s[28], table.find(u, s[28].hopRare) or 7, s[28]
	        local yN, MN = hN.hopSync

	        if yN then
	          hN = s[28]
	          MN = hN.zones
	        else
	          MN = yN
	        end

	        hN = nil

	        if MN then
	          hN = MN
	        else
	          BN = s[28]
	          yN = BN.hopSync
	          hN = if yN then not yN else fN
	        end

	        yN, MN = pairs, s[23]
	        local fN, uN, EN, XN, QN = y[91](MN()), false, 0, 0

	        for ZN, rN in yN(y:Co(fN)) do
	          if type(rN) == "table" then
	            if rN.State ~= "Claimed" then
	              if rN.Uid then
	                if rN.Uid:find("^FirstAreaEgg") then
	                else
	                  MN = D(rN)
	                  uN = if MN.r >= gN then true else uN
	                  BN = s[67]

	                  if BN(mN, MN) and (if hN then hN[tostring(rN.AreaId):gsub("%s", "")] else not hN) then
	                    if rN.State == "Carried" then
	                      ZN = EN + 1
	                      EN = ZN
	                    else
	                      local mN = XN + 1
	                      XN = mN
	                    end

	                    QN = if if QN then MN.v > QN.v else not QN then {v = MN.v, Record = rN, Area = tostring(rN.AreaId), State = rN.State} else QN
	                  else
	                  end
	                end
	              else
	              end
	            else
	            end
	          else
	          end
	        end

	        fN = nil
	        fN, pN = XN + EN, QN
	        NN = fN

	        if uN then
	          BN = s[28]

	          if BN.hopWhen == "A rare egg spawned" then
	            oN()
	          end
	        end

	        MN, BN = XN > 0

	        if MN then
	          yN = s[28]
	          fN = yN.steal

	          if fN then
	            BN = fN
	          else
	            gN = s[28]
	            BN = gN.hopWhen == HN
	          end
	        else
	          BN = MN
	        end

	        if BN then
	          return FN(XN .. " matching egg(s) here", "found")
	        end

	        if EN > 0 then
	          if (os.clock() - (bN or os.clock())) < 20 then
	            bN = bN or os.clock()
	            return FN("Someone is carrying a match, waiting", "found")
	          end
	        end

	        bN = nil
	        if H < 12 then
	          return FN("Night is close, staying", "night")
	        end
	        gN = s[28]

	        if gN.hopWhen ~= HN then
	          if f() > (qN.Window or 0) then
	            return FN("Waiting for a reason to hop", "rare")
	          end
	        end

	        FN("Hopping", "joining")
	        fN = s[15]

	        if not (fN.hop("Least Players")) then
	          task.wait(1.5)
	        end
	      end
	    end)

	    s[15].reserved = {}

	    local function qN(mN)

	        else
	          bN = mN
	        end
	      end

	    end

	    local function mN(BN)

	        return false

	      return true

	    local function BN(bN, gN)
	      local oN = ({Biggest = "s", ["Best rarity"] = "r"})[gN] or "v"
	      local pN = ({Smallest = "s", ["Lowest value"] = "v"})[gN]

	      table.sort(bN, function(gN, NN)
	        if pN then
	          return gN[pN] < NN[pN]
	        end

	        if gN[oN] ~= NN[oN] then
	          return gN[oN] > NN[oN]
	        end
	        return gN.v > NN.v
	      end)

	      return bN
	    end

	    P("20_farm#1", function()
	      local bN = n({name = "Place", mover = true, gap = 8, pri = 4, soft = true, bad = {}})

	      end, "log", nil, "Skip eggs worth less than this (0 = off)", pN)

	        NN.maxV = (((fN > 0) and (fN < 1000000000000)) and fN) or nil
	      end, "log", nil, "Only place eggs worth less than this, keep the better ones (far right = no limit)", pN)

	      local hN, FN = fN.onSteal, s[15]

	        pN = os.clock()

	        if hN then
	          hN(fN)
	        end
	      end

	      local function fN()
	        local hN = s[16]
	        local FN, MN, uN, EN, XN = hN(), s[42], next, {}, s[15]
	        y:lo(EN, 0, workspace:FindFirstChild("PlacedEggRenders"), XN.hidden)
	        XN = 0

	        for QN, ZN in uN, EN, nil do
	          QN, hN = ipairs, y[91](ZN:GetChildren())

	          for uN, EN in QN(y:Co(hN)) do
	            if EN:IsA("Model") then
	              uN, ZN = s[1], EN.Name:find(s[1].UserId .. "_", 1, true)

	              if ZN == 1 then
	                local hN, uN = XN + 1, MN + EN:GetPivot().Position
	                XN, MN = hN, uN
	              else
	              end
	            else
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        if XN > 0 then
	          return MN / XN
	        end
	        return (FN and FN.PetArea) and FN.PetArea.Position
	      end

	      local hN = y:Mo(17, 18, {[16] = s, [29] = s, [18] = s}, A, C, nil)

	      local function FN(MN)
	        local uN = s[26]
	        if uN("Egg", "WearEggTool", MN) == false then
	          return false
	        end
	        uN = os.clock() + 2

	        while os.clock() < uN do
	          if yN() == MN then
	            return true
	          end

	          if os.clock() > (uN - 1.3) then
	            local uN, EN = ipairs, (L() and L():GetChildren()) or {}

	            for XN, XN in uN(EN) do
	              if XN:GetAttribute("UID") == MN then
	                pcall(s[8]().EquipTool, s[8](), XN)
	              end
	            end
	          end

	          task.wait(0.1)
	        end

	        return false
	      end

	      gN = s[15]
	      gN.wear = FN

	      local function gN()
	        local MN = s[28]
	        local uN = MN.placeWhen

	        if uN == "Steal is idle" then
	          MN = s[28]
	          local EN = MN.steal
	          return if EN then PN.empty ~= nil else not EN
	        end

	        if uN == "Right after a steal" then
	          MN = os.clock() - pN
	          return MN < 12
	        end

	        if uN == "Only at night" then
	          return w() < 14
	        end
	        return true
	      end

	      local function PN()
	        local pN, MN, uN, EN = v(), {}, {}, s[28]
	        local XN, QN = EN.placeIdx

	        if XN then
	          EN = s[15]
	          QN = EN.indexMissing()
	        else
	          QN = XN
	        end

	        local ZN, rN, HN = QN or {}, {}, 0

	        for KN, tN in pairs(pN) do
	          if type(tN) == "table" then
	            if tN.Placement then
	              KN = HN + 1
	              rN[(tN.AssetCategory or tN.Category) or ""] = true
	              HN = KN
	            else
	            end
	          else
	          end
	        end

	        EN = pairs

	        for KN, tN in EN(pN) do
	          if type(tN) == "table" then
	            if not (tN.Placement) then
	              if (bN.bad[KN] or 0) < os.clock() then
	                XN = s[15]

	                if not (XN.reserved[KN]) then
	                  QN = s[15]

	                  if not (QN.protect(KN, tN)) then
	                    local XN = D(tN)
	                    XN.uid = KN

	                    if ZN[XN.cat] and not rN[XN.cat] then
	                      rN[XN.cat] = true
	                      uN[#uN + 1] = XN
	                    else
	                      local rN = s[28]

	                      if rN.place then
	                        local rN = s[67]

	                        if rN(NN, XN) then
	                          MN[#MN + 1] = XN
	                        end
	                      end
	                    end
	                  end
	                end
	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        ZN = s[64]
	        local XN, rN = ZN(), 0

	        for KN in next, ((XN and (type(XN.EquippedAssets) == "table")) and XN.EquippedAssets) or {}, nil do
	          rN += 1
	        end

	        QN = 0

	        for KN, KN in pairs(pN) do
	          QN = if type(KN) == "table" then if KN.Placement then QN else QN + 1 else QN
	        end

	        ZN, EN = pcall(function()
	          local KN = s[33]
	          return KN.Bases.GetAssetEquipCapacity((XN and tonumber(XN.BaseUpgradeLevel)) or 0)
	        end)

	        oN(((NN.maxV and (NN.maxV <= (NN.minV or 0))) and "Max Place Value must be above Min Place Value, nothing will be placed") or ("Eggs placed %d/%d  -  %d/%d pets equipped, %d in bag"):format(HN, 30, rN, (ZN and tonumber(EN)) or 0, QN))
	        return HN, MN, pN, uN
	      end

	      local oN = n({name = "PlaceInfo", gap = 2, info = true})

	      oN.on = function()
	        return ON("Farm")
	      end

	      oN.fn = PN

	      bN.want = function()
	        local oN, pN, NN, MN = PN()

	        if oN >= 30 then
	          return
	        elseif (#pN + #MN) == 0 then
	          return
	        elseif #MN == 0 then
	          if not (gN()) then
	            return
	          end
	        end

	        local PN = s[28]

	        if PN.placeFirst ~= "Backpack" then
	          local PN = s[28]
	          BN(pN, PN.placeFirst)
	        end

	        for PN, gN in ipairs(MN) do
	          table.insert(pN, PN, gN)
	        end

	        return {list = pN, room = 30 - oN, recs = NN}
	      end

	      bN.run = function(PN, gN)
	        local oN = fN()
	        if not (oN) then
	          return
	        end
	        local pN = s[11]
	        pN("Walking to your pen")
	        pN = s[29]
	        if not (AN(oN + pN(0, 3, 0), gN)) then
	          return
	        end
	        local NN, fN, MN, uN = hN(PN.recs), math.min(PN.room, #PN.list), 0, 0

	        for hN = 1, fN, 1 do
	          if not (gN()) then
	            break
	          end
	          oN = PN.list[hN]

	          if FN(oN.uid) then
	            hN = false

	            for PN = 1, 8, 1 do
	              MN += 1
	              pN = NN[MN]
	              if not pN then
	                break
	              end
	              PN = s[72]

	              if PN("RF/EggWorld/AskPlaceEgg", {Uid = oN.uid, LocalCFrame = pN[1]}) then
	                uN += 1
	                local PN = s[43]
	                PN.placed = PN.placed + 1
	                hN = true
	                break
	              end
	            end

	            if not hN then
	              bN.bad[oN.uid] = os.clock() + 60
	            end

	            task.wait(0.25)
	          else
	            bN.bad[oN.uid] = os.clock() + 20
	          end
	        end

	        NN = s[26]
	        NN("Egg", "DoffEggTool")

	        if uN > 0 then
	          fN = s[11]
	          fN("Placed " .. (uN .. " egg(s)"), "ok")
	        end
	      end

	      bN.on = function()
	        local PN = s[28]
	        local bN, gN = PN.place

	        if bN then
	          gN = bN
	        else
	          PN = s[28]
	          gN = PN.placeIdx
	        end

	        return gN
	      end
	    end)

	    P("20_farm#2", function()
	      local PN = n({name = "Hatch", gap = 2, bad = {}, info = true})

	                bN[#bN + 1] = {fN, hN}
	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        oN, gN = {["Ready now"] = tostring(#bN)}
	        gN, oN.Hatched = s[43], tostring(s[43].hatched)
	        Y("hs", pN, oN)
	        gN = s[28]

	        if not (gN.hatch) then
	          oN = s[28]
	          if not (oN.placeIdx) then
	            return
	          end
	        end

	        gN = s[28]

	        if gN.hatch then
	          if NN.maxV then
	            if NN.maxV <= (NN.minV or 0) then
	              oN = s[11]
	              oN("Max Hatch Value must be above Min Hatch Value, nothing will hatch", "warn")
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        oN = s[28]
	        local pN, fN = oN.placeIdx

	        if pN then
	          gN = s[15]
	          fN = gN.indexMissing()
	        else
	          fN = pN
	        end

	        oN, pN, gN = fN or {}, ipairs, 0

	        for hN, FN in pN(bN) do
	          if gN >= 4 then
	            break
	          end
	          hN, fN = FN[1], D(FN[2])
	          local bN, pN = (PN.bad[hN] or 0) < os.clock()

	          if bN then
	            local MN = s[28]
	            local uN, EN = MN.hatch

	            if uN then
	              MN = s[67]
	              EN = (MN(NN, fN))
	            else
	              EN = uN
	            end

	            pN = EN or oN[fN.cat]
	          else
	            pN = bN
	          end

	          if pN then
	            bN = s[15]

	            if bN.protect(hN, FN[2]) then
	            else
	              local bN = gN + 1

	              if mN(hN) then
	                local mN = s[43]
	                mN.hatched = mN.hatched + 1
	              else
	                PN.bad[hN] = os.clock() + 10
	              end

	              task.wait(0.2)
	              gN = bN
	            end
	          else
	          end
	        end
	      end

	      local PN = n({name = "Equip", gap = 8})

	      PN.on = function()

	        return mN.equip
	      end

	        PN = s[28]

	          if type(gN) ~= "table" then
	            return false
	          elseif not (mN) then
	            if not (bN) then
	              return false
	            end
	          end
	        else
	          return false
	        end

	        if pN then
	          PN = mN
	          NN = PN[oN.r] == true
	        else
	          NN = pN
	        end

	        pN = NN or ((bN ~= nil) and (oN.v >= bN))
	        return pN
	      end

	      local function gN()
	        local hN, FN, MN, uN, EN, XN = {}, {Boss = 0, Scrambled = 0, Enchanted = 0, Nitro = 0, Spectral = 0, Alien = 0}, ipairs, {}, s[1], s[1]
	        y:lo(uN, 0, EN.Character or (L()), XN.Character and (L()))

	        for EN, EN in MN(uN) do
	          for MN, MN in ipairs((EN and EN:GetChildren()) or {}) do

	                XN = (MN:GetAttribute("MutationId") or MN:GetAttribute("MutationTemplate")) or "Boss"
	                hN[XN] = hN[XN] or MN
	                FN[XN] = (FN[XN] or 0) + ((tonumber(MN:GetAttribute("Uses")) or tonumber(MN.Name:match("%[X(%d+)%]"))) or 1)
	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        return hN, FN
	      end

	      end

	        hN = hN or (gN())
	        if not (next(hN)) then
	          oN("No consumables in your bag")
	          return
	        end
	        local FN, MN, uN = {}, pairs, y[91](v())

	        for EN, XN in MN(y:Co(uN)) do

	        BN.Nitro = BN.Boss
	        BN.Scrambled = require(pN).SuccessPercent:Get()
	      end)

	      local function gN(pN, NN)
	        local fN, hN = qN(pN.uid), s[15]
	        local FN = hN.eggPos(pN.uid)

	        if fN then
	        else
	          if FN then
	            oN("Going to " .. pN.n)
	            hN = s[29]
	            if not (AN(FN + hN(0, 3, 4), NN)) then
	              return
	            end
	            local MN = fN

	            for uN = 1, 15, 1 do
	              MN = qN(pN.uid)
	              if MN then
	                break
	              end
	              task.wait(0.2)
	            end

	            fN = MN
	          else
	          end
	        end

	        if not (fN) then
	          mN.bad[pN.uid .. pN.k] = os.clock() + 30
	          return oN(pN.n .. " is not in your pen")
	        end
	        hN = s[15]
	        if not (hN.needBody()) then
	          return oN("Turning visible to use " .. PN[pN.k])
	        end
	        oN("Mutating " .. (pN.n .. (" with " .. PN[pN.k])))
	        hN = s[11]
	        hN("Mutating " .. pN.n)
	        hN, FN = s[29], fN:GetPivot().Position + s[29](0, 3, 4)
	        if not (AN(FN, NN)) then
	          return
	        end

	            qN, pN.tool.Parent = s[1], s[1].Character
	          end)
	        end

	            oN(("%s roll missed on %s (%s%% chance)"):format(PN[pN.k], pN.n, tostring(BN[pN.k] or "?")))
	          end
	        else
	          mN.bad[pN.uid .. pN.k] = os.clock() + 600
	          oN(("%s refused on %s: %s"):format(PN[pN.k], pN.n, ((type(FN) == "table") and tostring(FN.Message or "no reason")) or "no answer"))
	        end

	        task.wait(math.max(0, 1 - (os.clock() - qN)))

	        if NN then
	          pcall(NN.UnequipTools, NN)
	        end

	        return true
	      end

	      mN.run = function(qN, PN)
	        if qN.pick then
	          return gN(qN, PN)
	        end
	        local BN

	        while qN and (PN()) do
	          BN = gN(qN, PN)
	          qN = (BN and PN()) and bN()
	        end

	        return BN
	      end

	          mN = PN
	        else

	          mN = qN.mutPick ~= nil
	        end

	      qN.want = function()

	        local oN, pN, NN = PN(), mN()

	        if oN then

	            return
	          end
	        else

	        end

	        if NN then
