	      end

	      qN.run = function(PN, oN)
	        local pN, pN = mN()

	        if pN then
	          local mN = s[15]

	          if mN.onAdmin then
	            gN += 1

	            if gN >= 3 then
	              local mN = s[15]

	              if mN.respawn then
	                gN = 0
	                local mN = s[11]
	                mN("Admin treadmill will not take you, respawning", "warn")
	                mN = s[15]
	                return mN.respawn()
	              end
	            end

	            local mN = s[11]
	            mN("Admin treadmill speed is not going up, getting back on", "warn")
	            if not (s[13](oN)) then
	              return
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        local mN = s[11]
	        mN((pN and "Going to the admin treadmill") or "Going to the treadmill")
	        mN = s[29]
	        if not (AN(PN + mN(0, 1, 0), oN)) then
	          return
	        end
	        mN, PN = s[15], s[15]
	        mN.hold = (PN.hold or 0) + 1
	        mN = os.clock() + ((pN and 2.5) or 3)
	        BN = 0
	        PN = false

	        while ((os.clock() < mN) and (oN())) and not PN do
	          if pN then
	            local gN = s[15]
	            PN = gN.onAdmin == true

	            if PN then
	            else
	              if os.clock() > (mN - 0.1) then
	                gN = s[9]
	                local NN, fN, hN = gN("RF/Treadmill/AskWearStill")
	                local FN, MN, uN = ((NN and ((fN == true) or ((type(hN) == "string") and hN:lower():find("already")))) and true) or false, s[15], s[15]
	                MN.onAdmin = uN.onAdmin or FN
	                PN = FN
	              else
	              end
	            end

	            if not PN then
	              gN = s[20]
	              gN()
	            end
	          else
	            local gN = s[9]
	            local NN, fN, hN = gN("RF/Treadmill/AskWearStill")
	            gN = s[19]
	            local FN = gN()
	            gN = (FN and FN.Anchored) or (NN and ((fN == true) or ((type(hN) == "string") and hN:lower():find("already"))))
	            PN = gN or ((os.clock() - BN) < 3)

	            if not PN then
	              task.wait(0.3)
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        mN = s[15]
	        mN.hold = mN.hold - 1

	        if not (oN()) then
	          mN = s[13]

	          return mN(function()
	            return true
	          end)
	        end

	        if PN then
	          bN = 0

	          if pN then
	            BN = os.clock()
	          end

	          mN = s[11]
	          return mN((pN and "Training on the admin treadmill") or "Training speed", "ok")
	        end

	        bN += 1

	        if bN >= 2 then
	          pN = s[15]

	          if pN.respawn then
	            bN = 0
	            mN = s[11]
	            mN("Treadmill will not take you, respawning", "warn")
	            PN = s[15]
	            PN.respawn()
	          end
	        end
	      end

	      local PN = s[15]

	      PN.treadOn = function()
	        return qN.on()
	      end

	      qN.on = function()

	          BN = mN
	        else
	          PN = s[28]
	          BN = PN.adminTread and (TN() or (workspace:FindFirstChild("AdminTreadmill") ~= nil))

	      end

	      x(TN, "Auto Upgrade Treadmill", "upTread", false)

	        local PN = s[64]

	                  y:lo(hN, 0, FN, MN)

	                    end

	                      qN = s[9]
	                      qN("RF/Codex/AskRedeemLimitedEgg", fN[1])
	                    end

	              end
	            end
	          end

	                  return
	                end
	              else

	                return
	              end

	                gN("RF/Treadmill/AskTierRaise", qN._id)

	              end
	            end

	          else

	          end

	            gN = (type(pN) == "table") and tonumber(pN.Price)
	            PN = ((type(pN) == "table") and pN._id) or PN
	            mN = if gN then if gN <= BN then if qN[PN] ~= true then if TN.fail[PN] then mN else if mN then if gN < mN[2] then {PN, gN} else mN else {PN, gN} else mN else mN else mN
	          end

	            end
	          end

	      local function k(I)

	              end
	            end

	          end

	          return G1.Tint
	        end

	        local function RN(G1, L1)

	            return keypoints[1].Value
	          end

	            local _1, v8 = keypoints[i2 - 1], keypoints[i2]
	            if L1 <= v8.Time then
	              G1 = v8.Time - _1.Time
	              return _1.Value:Lerp(v8.Value, ((G1 > 0) and ((L1 - _1.Time) / G1)) or 0)
	            end
	          end

	          return keypoints[#keypoints].Value
	        end

	          end

	          if #arg > 0 then
	            local W1 = {}

	            for I1, v9 in ipairs(arg) do
	              W1[I1] = tostring(X[v9] or v9):gsub("%s*[Mm]utation$", ""):upper()
	            end

	            _1, v8 = table.concat(W1, arg2 or " + "), JN[tostring(arg[1])] or zN
	          else
	          end

	          Z1(L1, _1)

	          if v8 then
	            if L1:GetAttribute("MutationKey") ~= _1 then
	              L1:SetAttribute("MutationKey", _1)
	              ;(L1:FindFirstChild("MutationPaint") or o("UIGradient", {Parent = L1, Name = "MutationPaint", Rotation = 90})).Color = v8
	            end
	          end

	          return _1 ~= ""
	        end

	        end

	        local function JN(SN, xN, L1, arg, arg2)

	        end

	        local function D1()

	          if not G1 or N1 then
	            return
	          end

	              if not (pcall(function()
	                local W1 = s[33]
	                return W1.Egg.WearEggTool(str)
	              end)) then
	                local W1 = s[9]
	                W1("RF/EggWorld/AskWearTool", str)
	              end
	            elseif G1.Status == "Ready" then
	              local G1 = s[72]

	              if G1("RF/EggWorld/AskHatch", str) then
	                task.wait(0.35)
	                local G1 = s[9]
	                G1("RF/EggWorld/AskFinishHatch", str)
	              end
	            else
	              local G1 = workspace:FindFirstChild("PlacedEggRenders")
	              local W1
	              local I1, children = ipairs, (G1 and G1:GetChildren()) or {}

	              for J1, J1 in I1(children) do
	                if J1.Name:find(str, 1, true) or (J1:GetAttribute("Uid") == str) then
	                  W1 = J1
	                  break
	                end
	              end

	              G1, I1 = pcall(function()
	                return (W1:IsA("Model") and W1:GetPivot()) or W1.CFrame
	              end)

	              if G1 then
	                oN = I1.Position
	              end
	            end
	          end)

	          N1, pN = false, true
	        end

	        local function BN(tN, LN, vN)
	          local zN = jN.Spot and (tN ~= nil)
	          fn4(B1.Frame, zN)
	          B1.Focus = (zN and tN) or nil
	          local JN = (zN and 7.6) or 0

	          if B1.Dock ~= JN then
	            B1.Dock = JN
	            v2:SetDock(JN, {Gap = 0.35, Divider = false})
	          end

	          if not (zN) then
	            return
	          end

	          if _1(B1, tN) then
	            JN = tN.Info.Up == "SECRET"
	            B1.Shade.Transparency = SN(0.5, (JN and 0.92) or 0.82, (JN and 0.72) or 0.25)

	          fn4(B1.Mutation, zN)
	          local RN = (zN and B1.NameRow) or B1.Body

	            JN.Parent, JN.Size = RN, C(0, (zN and 1) or 0.12)
	          end

	          UN(B1, tN)
	          JN = IN[tN.Status]

	          if B1.ActionStyle ~= JN then
	            zN = B1
	            zN.ActionStyle, zN.ActionGradient.Color = JN, JN[1]
	            Z1(B1.ActionText, JN[2])
	          end

	          Z1(B1.Rank, ("#%d of %d eggs  by %s"):format(LN, vN, jN.Sort:lower()))
	        end

	        local function tN(LN, vN)
	          if LN <= 0 then
	            return
	          elseif vN <= 0 then
	            return
	          elseif T1 == LN then
	            if n2 == vN then
	              return
	            end
	          end

	          T1, n2 = LN, vN
	          local IN, zN, JN, RN = PN(8, mN(vN * 0.7)), PN(6, mN(vN * 0.45)), PN(4, mN(vN * 0.25)), PN(6, mN(LN * 0.012))
	          local SN, xN, UN, T1, frame, N1, D1 = (JN * 2) + RN, (LN - (JN * 2)) - RN, O1, O1, B1.Frame, B1.Frame, k(JN, JN)
	          local G1, L1, _1 = yN(1, -SN, 0, 0), k(JN, JN), yN(1, -SN, 1, -JN * 2)
	          UN.Position = D1
	          T1.Size = G1
	          frame.Position = L1
	          N1.Size = _1
	          L1 = math.clamp(mN((PN(1, xN) + IN) / ((vN * 8.2) + IN)), 4, 6)
	          RN = mN((PN(1, xN) - (IN * (L1 - 1))) / L1)
	          n3 = mN(RN * 1.42)
	          T1, N1, frame, UN, LN, D1 = UIGridLayout, UIGridLayout, UIListLayout, k(RN, n3), k(IN, IN), TN(0, IN)
	          T1.CellSize = UN
	          N1.CellPadding = LN
	          frame.Padding = D1
	          UN, RN, N1, D1 = v3, v6, v4, v7
	          UN.Size, RN.Padding, N1.Size, D1.Padding = yN(1, 0, 0, mN(vN * 2.9)), TN(0, zN), yN(1, 0, 0, mN(vN * 2.1)), TN(0, zN)

	          for UN, UN in ipairs(VN) do
	            UN.Frame.Size, UN.Limit.MaxTextSize, UN.Value:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke").Thickness = yN(UN.Share, -math.ceil((zN * 4) / 5), 1, 0), PN(8, mN(vN * 1.25)), PN(1, (vN * 1.25) * 0.08)
	            H1(UN.Caption, vN * 0.74)
	          end

	            H1(UN.Text, vN * 0.92)
	          end

	          T1 = v5
	          T1.Size = yN(1, 0, 0, mN(vN * 1.5))

	            fn8(UN)
	          end

	          SN, zN, IN = (7.6 * vN) - (JN * 2), B1.Stats, B1.ActionText
	          _1 = PN(1, (xN - SN) - (vN * 1.1))
	          B1.Body.Position, B1.Body.Size, B1.NameLimit.MaxSize = k(SN + (vN * 0.7), 0), yN(1, -(SN + (vN * 1.1)), 1, 0), I(PN(40, (_1 * 0.72) * 0.62), SN)
	          frame, G1, vN, xN, L1, LN, RN, JN = {}, B1.Name, B1.Mutation, B1.Rarity, B1.Value, zN.Size, zN.Weight, B1.Progress.Text
	          y:lo(frame, 0, G1, 0.16, vN, 0.1, xN, 0.1, L1, 0.18, LN, 0.105, RN, 0.105, JN, 0.105, B1.Rank, 0.105)
	          K1(SN, frame)

	          IN:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke").Thickness = PN(1, IN.TextSize * 0.07)

	        end

	        local function I(yN)
	          v2 = yN
	          O1 = o("Frame", {AutomaticSize = kN, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = C(1, 0)})
	          UIListLayout = o("UIListLayout", {Parent = O1, SortOrder = _N})
	          v3 = M1(O1, nil, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, {LayoutOrder = 1})
	          v6 = Q1(v3)
	          v4 = M1(O1, nil, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, {LayoutOrder = 2})
	          v7 = Q1(v4)
	          P1 = M1(O1, nil, 1, 0, 0, 1, 0, {AutomaticSize = kN, LayoutOrder = 3})
	          UIGridLayout = o("UIGridLayout", {Parent = P1, SortOrder = _N})
	          v5 = X1(O1, 0, 0, 0, 0, {LayoutOrder = 4, TextColor3 = HN})
	          local C, TN = ipairs, {{G.egg, XN(255, 255, 255, 190, 200, 225), "EGGS"}, {G.egg, WN[2][3], "READY"}, {G.night, WN[3][3], "GROWING"}, {"rbxassetid://70598530577515", WN[4][3], "IN BAG"}, {G.money, DN, "TOTAL / S"}}

	          for XN, DN in C(TN) do
	            local LN = fn(M1(v3, rN, 0.12, 0, 0, 0, 0, {LayoutOrder = XN}), 0.22)
	            fn2(LN, KN, 1.2)
	            fn3(LN, 0, 0.5, 0.04, 0.5, 0.28, 0.78, DN[1])
	            local vN = X1(LN, 0.33, 0.06, 0.65, 0.54, {TextScaled = true, TextXAlignment = GN})
	            F1(vN, DN[2])
	            VN[XN] = {Frame = LN, Value = vN, Caption = X1(LN, 0.33, 0.58, 0.65, 0.36, {TextColor3 = HN, TextXAlignment = GN, Text = DN[3]}), Share = ((XN == 5) and 0.28) or 0.18, Limit = o("UITextSizeConstraint", {Parent = vN, MinTextSize = 6})}
	          end

	          for XN, DN in ipairs(WN) do
	            TN = E1(v4, {BackgroundColor3 = bN, BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = XN})
	            fn(TN, 0.3)
	            C = {Key = DN[1], Title = DN[2], Button = TN, Stroke = fn2(TN, KN, 1.2), Gradient = F1(TN, DN[3]), Text = X1(TN, 0, 0, 1, 1)}

	            TN.Activated:Connect(function()
	              jN.Filter = DN[1]
	              pN = true
	            end)

	            nN[XN] = C
	          end

	          oN(yN:Dock())
	          yN:Attach(O1, yN:Root())

	          yN:OnResize(function(C, C, yN)
	            pcall(tN, C, yN)
	          end)
	        end

	        local function C()
	          local yN

	          task.spawn(function()
	            local TN, oN = pcall(YN)

	            if TN then
	              yN = oN
	            end
	          end)

	          if yN then
	            tbl, yN = yN, yN
	          else
	            yN = tbl
	          end

	          local TN, oN, XN, tN, DN = {All = #yN, Ready = 0, Growing = 0, Inventory = 0}, jN.Sort, yN, 0

	          for GN, GN in ipairs(XN) do
	            TN[GN.Status] += 1
	            tN, DN = tN + GN.Income, if DN then if GN.Income > DN.Income then GN else DN else GN
	          end

	          for GN, LN in ipairs(WN) do
	            Z1(VN[GN].Value, tostring(TN[LN[1]]))
	          end

	          Z1(VN[5].Value, "$" .. FN(tN))

	          for tN, tN in ipairs(nN) do
	            XN = jN.Filter == tN.Key
	            Z1(tN.Text, tN.Title .. ("  " .. TN[tN.Key]))

	            if tN.Active ~= XN then
	              tN.Active, tN.Gradient.Enabled, tN.Button.BackgroundColor3, tN.Button.BackgroundTransparency, tN.Text.TextColor3, tN.Stroke.Color = XN, XN, (XN and bN) or rN, (XN and 0) or 0.15, (XN and bN) or HN, (XN and ZN) or KN
	            end
	          end

	          table.sort(yN, function(ZN, rN)
	            if oN == wN[2] then
	              if ZN.Info.RarityNumber ~= rN.Info.RarityNumber then
	                return ZN.Info.RarityNumber > rN.Info.RarityNumber
	              end
	            end

	            if oN == wN[3] then
	              if ZN.Remaining ~= rN.Remaining then
	                return ZN.Remaining < rN.Remaining
	              end
	            end

	            if ZN.Income ~= rN.Income then
	              return ZN.Income > rN.Income
	            end
	            return tostring(ZN.Id) < tostring(rN.Id)
	          end)

	          local oN, ZN, rN = v2:Query():lower(), {}, yN

	          for HN, HN in ipairs(rN) do
	            if (jN.Filter == "All") or (HN.Status == jN.Filter) then
	              if qN(HN, oN) then
	                ZN[#ZN + 1] = HN
	              end
	            end
	          end

	          TN, XN = yN
	          rN = 0

	          for qN, HN in ipairs(TN) do
	            if HN.Id == id then
	              XN, rN = HN, qN
	              break
	            end

	            if HN == DN then
	              XN, rN = HN, qN
	            else
	            end
	          end

	          BN(XN, rN, #yN)
	          XN, rN = #ZN, 0

	          for qN = 1, XN, 1 do
	            TN = cN[qN]

	            if TN then
	            else
	              if rN >= 12 then
	                pN = true
	                XN = qN - 1
	                break
	              end

	              rN += 1
	              TN = fn7(qN)
	              cN[qN] = TN

	              if n3 > 0 then
	                fn8(TN)
	              end
	            end

	            fn9(TN, ZN[qN], qN)
	          end

	          for qN = XN + 1, #cN, 1 do
	            fn4(cN[qN].Frame, false)
	            cN[qN].Id = nil
	          end

	          TN, XN = v5, ((#yN == 0) and "No eggs yet") or ((#ZN > 0) and "")
	          Z1(TN, (XN or ((oN ~= "") and ("No eggs match \"%s\""):format(oN))) or "No eggs in this filter")
	          fn4(v5, v5.Text ~= "")
	        end

	        local yN, TN = lN["Egg Predictor"], aN.Predictor.page

	        yN:Dropdown("Sort By", wN, wN[1], function(aN)
	          if table.find(wN, aN) then
	            jN.Sort = aN
	            pN = true
	          end
	        end)

	        yN:Toggle("Preview Card", true, function(aN)
	          jN.Spot = aN == true
	          pN = true
	        end)

	        yN:Surface({Name = "Egg Predictor", Search = true, SearchPlaceholder = "Search eggs...", Layout = "free", OnSearch = function()
	          pN = true
	        end, Style = QN(34), Build = function(yN)
	          I(yN)
	          pN = true
	        end})

	        b(d, function(I)
	          local yN = gN.IsShown(TN) and (TN.AbsoluteSize.X > 0)
	          local TN = (yN and (O1 ~= nil)) and gN.IsShown(O1)

	          if TN then
	            if not (C1) then
	              pN = true
	            end
	          end

	          C1 = TN
	          if not (yN) then
	            return
	          end

	          if TN then
	            if B1.Frame.Visible then
	              n4 += I

	              if n4 >= 0.05 then
	                B1.Rays.Rotation = (B1.Rays.Rotation + (n4 * 6)) % 360
	                n4 = 0
	              end
	            end
	          end

	          A1 += I

	          if (TN and pN) or (A1 >= 1) then
	            A1 = 0

	            if TN then
	              pN = false
	              pcall(C)
	            end

	            pcall(NN)
	          end

	          fN()
	        end)
	      end

	      do
	        local C, I, yN, TN, aN, qN, BN, oN = "#9FB8FF", "#4DFF7A", "#FFC24D", "#FFFFFF", "#CDE7FF", "#FFDF8A", 2.9, 0.0909
	        local XN = gN.Paint
	        local ZN = gN.Bold
	        local rN = gN.Escape
	        local HN = gN.MutationText
	        local KN = gN.Separator()
	        local tN, DN, GN, LN = hN(0, 255, 255, 255, 0.5, 222, 238, 255, 1, 255, 255, 255), 3.15, {0, "#8F98A8", 0.3, "#C6CDDA", 0.85, "#FFFFFF", 1.45, "#7CFF9E", 1.9, "#4FE0FF", 2.85, "#6FA0FF", 3.8, "#C08BFF", 5.8, "#FF9A3D", 9.5, "#FF5C5C", 12, "#FFD34D", 20, "#FF4DE8"}
	        local hN, vN, kN, _N, wN, WN, IN = {}, {}, {}, {}, 0, 0, 0.06
	        local zN = -1
	        local JN = -1
	        local RN, YN, SN, xN, jN, cN = -1, 4, 3, false, 0
	        local VN, nN = 0.01, 150

	        local function UN(arg, A1)
	          return ZN(XN(arg, A1))
	        end

	        local function ZN(arg)
	          return ("%.2fx"):format(arg)
	        end

	        local function fn(A1)
	          return ("%.2fx - %.2fx"):format(A1.Min, A1.Max)
	        end

	        local function A1(C1, T1)
	          local v2 = s[26]
	          local Rec = v2("Rec", "WeightKgForScale", C1, T1)
	          return (((type(Rec) == "number") and (Rec > 0)) and Rec) or nil
	        end

	        local function C1(T1)
	          return (T1 and (KN .. XN(aN, MN(T1)))) or ""
	        end

	        local function T1(arg)
	          local str = "#FFFFFF"

	          for i2 = 1, #GN, 2 do
	            str = if (arg + 0.001) >= GN[i2] then GN[i2 + 1] else str
	          end

	          return str
	        end

	        local function GN()
	          if cN then
	            local v2 = cN
	            return v2
	          end

	          local ok, result = pcall(function()
	            local v2 = s[33]
	            return (debug.getupvalues or getupvalues)(v2.Rec.DrawAssetScale)
	          end)

	          local v2, tbl = pairs, ((ok and (type(result) == "table")) and result) or {}

	          for k2, O1 in v2(tbl) do
	            if type(O1) == "table" then
	              if type(O1.SCALE_BANDS) == "table" then
	                result, k2, ok = O1.SCALE_BANDS, tonumber(O1.SCALE_DOUBLING_ODDS) or VN, tonumber(O1.SCALE_HARD_CAP) or nN
	                cN, VN, nN = result, k2, ok
	                break
	              end

	              if type(O1[1]) == "table" then
	                if O1[1].min then
	                  if O1[1].weight then
	                    cN = O1
	                    break
	                  end
	                end
	              end
	            end
	          end

	          if not (cN) then
	            ok = {0.85, 1.05, 2000, 1.45, 1.55, 250, 1.9, 2.1, 125, 2.85, 3.15, 62.5, 3.8, 4.2, 31.25, 0.3, 0.45, 18, 0.1, 0.2, 5, 5.8, 6.2, 15.625, 9.5, 12.5, 3, 12, 17, 0.05, 20, 35, 0.0001}
	            cN = {}
	            tbl = #ok

	            for i2 = 1, tbl, 3 do
	              result = cN
	              result[#result + 1] = {min = ok[i2], max = ok[i2 + 1], weight = ok[i2 + 2]}
	            end
	          end

	          ok = cN
	          return ok
	        end

	        local function cN(arg, arg2, arg3)
	          local n2 = s[26]
	          local Fuse = n2("Fuse", "BandWeightBias", arg, arg2, arg3)
	          if type(Fuse) == "number" then
	            return Fuse
	          end
	          n2 = 0.6931471805599453
	          return math.exp(((math.log(((arg[1] + arg[2]) + arg[3]) / 3) / n2) * (math.log((arg2 + arg3) / 2) / n2)) * 0.6)
	        end

	        local function fn2()
	          local v2 = s[64]
	          local v3 = v2()
	          if not (v3) then
	            return
	          end
	          local v4, v5, O1, P1 = AN(v3.FusionSlots), AN(v3.Inventory), {}, v3.FusionEggReward

	          for i2 = 1, 3, 1 do
	            v2 = v5[v4[i2]]

	            if type(v2) == "table" then
	              O1[#O1 + 1] = {Category = v2.Category, Scale = tonumber(v2.Scale) or 1, Mutations = AN(v2.Mutations), Raw = v2}
	            end
	          end

	          return {Items = O1, Locked = v3.FusionLocked == true, Duration = tonumber(v3.FusionDuration) or 0, Reward = P1, Result = (((type(P1) == "table") and (type(P1.Egg) == "table")) and P1.Egg) or nil}
	        end

	        local function fn3(arg)
	          local v2 = arg.Get()

	          if WN > 0 then
	            if v2.Text == tostring(arg.Spec.Text or "") then
	              return v2
	            end
	          end

	          xN = true
	        end

	        local function fn4(arg)
	          local v2 = fn3(arg)
	          if not (v2) then
	            return
	          end
	          local O1, P1 = v2.Size, v2.TextWrapped
	          v2.TextWrapped = false
	          v2.Size = k(100000, PN(1, O1.Y.Offset))
	          arg = v2.TextBounds.X
	          v2.Size = O1
	          v2.TextWrapped = P1
	          if arg > 0 then
	            O1 = WN
	            return arg / O1
	          end
	          xN = true
	        end

	        local function fn5(size, O1)
	          local P1 = fn3(size)
	          if not (P1) then
	            return
	          end
	          size = P1.Size
	          P1.Size = k(PN(1, mN((O1 * WN) + 0.5)), 100000)
	          O1 = P1.TextBounds.Y
	          P1.Size = size
	          if O1 > 0 then
	            P1 = WN
	            return O1 / P1
	          end
	          xN = true
	        end

	        local function k(mN, arg, arg2, O1, P1)
	          local v2, tbl = LN, {Parent = mN, X = arg, Width = arg2, Height = arg2, Corner = 0.35, Background = "#000000", BackgroundTransparency = O1, StrokeThickness = oN, StrokeTransparency = 0, ZIndex = P1}
	          return v2:Image(tbl)
	        end

	        local function mN(oN, arg, arg2, O1)
	          local P1, tbl = LN, {Parent = oN, X = arg, Width = arg2, Height = 1.2, Scale = 1.2, Wrap = false, Gradient = tN, TextStrokeTransparency = 1, ZIndex = O1}
	          return P1:Text(tbl)
	        end

	        local function oN(tN, arg, arg2, O1, P1)
	          local v2, tbl = LN, {Parent = tN, X = arg, Width = arg2, Height = 1.2, Scale = 0.936, Wrap = false, Font = gN.RarityFont, TextStrokeTransparency = 1, StrokeTransparency = 0.08, StrokeThickness = P1, ZIndex = O1}
	          return v2:Text(tbl)
	        end

	        local function tN(arg, arg2)
	          arg.Set({Visible = arg2.Icon ~= nil, Image = arg2.Icon or "", StrokeColor = arg2.Color})
	        end

	        local function fn6(arg, O1)
	          local P1 = ((O1.Up == "SECRET") and gN.SecretGradient) or O1.Gradient
	          arg.Set({Text = O1.Up, Color = ((P1 ~= nil) and bN) or O1.Color, Gradient = P1, GradientRotation = ((O1.Up == "SECRET") and gN.SecretRotation) or nil})
	        end

	        local function bN(arg)
	          local O1 = LN:Frame({Name = "Slot", Background = "#000000", BackgroundTransparency = 0.74, Corner = 0.35, Width = 1, Height = 1, Visible = false})
	          local P1, v2 = {Frame = O1, Icon = k(O1, 0.25, 2.3, 0.45), Name = mN(O1, DN, 1), Rarity = oN(O1, DN, 1, nil, IN)}, LN
	          P1.Detail = v2:Text({Parent = O1, X = DN, Y = 1.2, Width = PN(1, (wN - BN) - 0.5), Height = 1, Wrap = true})
	          P1.Status = LN:Text({Parent = O1, Width = 1, Height = 1.2, Wrap = false, Align = "Right", Color = C})
	          vN[arg] = P1
	          return P1
	        end

	        local function fn7()
	          if wN <= 0 then
	            return
	          end
	          xN = false
	          local O1, P1 = PN(1, wN - 5.8), fn4(hN.Rarity)

	          if P1 then
	            SN = P1 + 0.1
	          end

	          P1 = fn4(hN.Name)

	          if P1 then
	            YN = math.min(P1 + 0.1, PN(1, (O1 - SN) - 0.24))
	          end

	          hN.Name.Set({Width = YN})
	          local set, tbl = hN.Rarity.Set, {X = (5.8 + YN) + 0.24}
	          tbl.Width = PN(0.5, math.min(SN, (O1 - YN) - 0.24))
	          set(tbl)
	          hN.Info.Set({Width = O1})
	          O1, tbl = PN(1, (wN - BN) - 0.5), 0

	          for BN, YN in ipairs(_N) do
	            if YN.Handle then
	              set = (YN.Gap and 0.5) or 0
	              P1 = fn5(YN.Handle, wN)

	              if P1 then
	                YN.Height = P1
	              end

	              BN = PN(1, YN.Height or 1)
	              YN.Handle.Set({Y = tbl + set, Width = wN, Height = BN})
	              tbl += (BN + 0.11) + set
	            else
	              local BN = YN.Slot
	              P1 = fn5(BN.Detail, O1)

	              if P1 then
	                BN.DU = P1
	              end

	              local YN = math.clamp(BN.DU or 1, 1, 4)
	              P1 = fn4(BN.Status)

	              if P1 then
	                BN.SU = P1 + 0.23
	              end

	              local SN = math.min(O1 * 0.42, PN(2.73, BN.SU or 2.73))
	              local n2 = PN(1, (O1 - SN) - 0.24)
	              P1 = fn4(BN.Rarity)

	              if P1 then
	                BN.RU = P1 + 0.1
	              end

	              local n3 = math.min(BN.RU or 3, n2 * 0.5)
	              P1 = fn4(BN.Name)

	              if P1 then
	                BN.NU = P1 + 0.1
	              end

	              local n4, P1 = math.min(PN(1, BN.NU or 4), PN(1, (n2 - n3) - 0.24)), PN(YN + 1.2, 2.3) + 0.36
	              n2 = ((P1 - YN) - 1.2) / 2
	              BN.Frame.Set({Y = tbl, Width = wN, Height = P1})
	              local set2, B1 = BN.Icon.Set, {}
	              B1.Y = (P1 - 2.3) / 2
	              set2(B1)
	              BN.Name.Set({Y = n2, Width = n4})
	              BN.Rarity.Set({X = (DN + n4) + 0.24, Y = n2, Width = PN(0.5, n3)})
	              BN.Detail.Set({Y = n2 + 1.2, Width = O1, Height = YN})
	              BN.Status.Set({X = (DN + O1) - SN, Y = n2, Width = PN(0.5, SN)})
	              tbl += P1 + 0.22
	            end
	          end

	          tbl = PN(1, tbl)

	          if math.abs(tbl - RN) > 0.01 then
	            RN = tbl
	            LN:SetContentLines(tbl)
	          end
	        end

	        local function BN(DN, RN)
	          local YN = DN ~= nil
	          LN:SetDock((YN and 5) or 0, {Gap = 0.22})

	          for SN, SN in next, hN, nil do
	            SN.Set({Visible = YN})
	          end

	          if not (YN) then
	            return
	          end
	          tN(hN.Icon, DN)
	          local SN, tbl = hN.Name.Set, {}
	          tbl.Text = rN(DN.Name)
	          SN(tbl)
	          fn6(hN.Rarity, DN)
	          tbl, SN = RN.Result, #RN.Items
	          YN = XN(TN, ("Fusing %d of 3 pets"):format(SN))

	          if tbl then
	            YN = (UN(I, "Result locked in, claim it at the machine"))
	          else
	            if RN.Reward then
	              YN = (UN(I, "Fuse finished, claim your egg"))
	            else
	              if RN.Locked then
	                local n2 = ((RN.Duration > 1000000000) and (RN.Duration - f())) or 0
	                YN = UN(yN, ((n2 > 0) and ("Fusing" .. (KN .. gN.FormatClock(n2)))) or "Fusing")
	              else
	              end
	            end
	          end

	          local gN, set, O1 = tbl and (tonumber(tbl.AssetScale) or 1), hN.Info.Set, {}
	          O1.Text = UN(I, FN((tbl and uN(DN, gN, tbl.Mutations)) or uN(DN, RN.Items[1].Scale, RN.Items[1].Mutations))) .. ("\n" .. (XN(TN, (tbl and ("%.2fx fused egg"):format(gN)) or ("%d/3 loaded"):format(SN)) .. ("\n" .. YN)))
	          set(O1)
	        end

	        NN = function()
	          if not (LN) then
	            return
	          end
	          jN = 2
	          table.clear(_N)
	          local gN, NN = 0, 0

	          local function DN(RN, YN)
	            gN += 1
	            local SN = kN[gN]

	            if SN then
	            else
	              SN = LN:Text({Name = "Line", Width = 1, Height = 1, Wrap = true, Visible = false})
	              kN[gN] = SN
	            end

	            SN.Set({Visible = true, Text = RN})
	            _N[#_N + 1] = {Handle = SN, Gap = YN}
	          end

	          local function RN(YN, SN)
	            DN(UN(SN, YN), #_N > 0)
	          end

	          local function YN(SN)
	            DN(XN(C, SN))
	          end

	          local function SN(arg, arg2)
	            DN(XN(C, arg) .. ("  " .. arg2))
	          end

	          local function fn8(arg)
	            SN("Species", UN(arg.Hex, "[" .. (arg.Up .. "]")) .. (" " .. UN(TN, rN(arg.Name))))
	          end

	          local v2 = fn2()

	          if v2 then
	            if v2.Result then
	              local result = v2.Result
	              local O1, P1, v3 = EN(result.AssetCategory), tonumber(result.AssetScale) or 1, AN(result.Mutations)
	              BN(O1, v2)
	              RN("FUSE RESULT (CONFIRMED)", I)
	              fn8(O1)
	              SN("Size", UN(T1(P1), ZN(P1)) .. C1(A1(result.AssetCategory, P1)))
	              result = HN(v3)
	              SN("Mutation", ((result ~= "") and result) or XN(TN, "Normal"))
	              SN("Income", UN(I, FN(uN(O1, P1, v3))))
	              YN("The server picks this egg when the fuse starts, it will not change")
	            else
	              if #v2.Items == 0 then
	                BN()
	                DN(UN(TN, "Machine is empty"))
	                YN("Load 3 pets of the same species to see the result odds")
	              else
	                local AN = v2.Items
	                local category, O1 = AN[1].Category, EN(AN[1].Category)
	                BN(O1, v2)
	                RN(("FUSE MACHINE STATUS (%d/3 PETS)"):format(#AN), TN)
	                fn8(O1)

	                for EN = 1, 3, 1 do
	                  local v3, P1 = AN[EN], vN[EN] or bN(EN)
	                  P1.Frame.Set({Visible = true})
	                  P1.Status.Set({Text = "SLOT " .. EN})

	                  if v3 then
	                    tN(P1.Icon, O1)
	                    local bN, tN = P1.Name.Set, {}
	                    tN.Text = rN(O1.Name)
	                    bN(tN)
	                    fn6(P1.Rarity, O1)
	                    local bN, rN, tN = HN(v3.Mutations), P1.Detail.Set, {}
	                    tN.Text = UN(qN, ZN(v3.Scale)) .. (C1(A1(v3.Category, v3.Scale)) .. (KN .. (UN(I, FN(uN(O1, v3.Scale, v3.Mutations))) .. (KN .. (((bN ~= "") and bN) or XN(C, "Normal"))))))
	                    rN(tN)
	                  else
	                    P1.Icon.Set({Visible = false})
	                    P1.Name.Set({Text = XN(C, "Empty")})
	                    P1.Rarity.Set({Text = ""})
	                    P1.Detail.Set({Text = XN(C, "Add a pet to this slot")})
	                  end

	                  _N[#_N + 1] = {Slot = P1}
	                  NN = EN
	                end

	                local bN = 0

	                for EN, EN in ipairs(AN) do
	                  bN += EN.Scale
	                end

	                local EN = bN / #AN
	                SN("Average Scale", UN(qN, ZN(EN)) .. C1(A1(category, EN)))
	                RN("PREDICTED SIZE PROBABILITIES", I)

	                if #AN == 3 then
	                  local qN, bN, ZN, rN = {AN[1].Scale, AN[2].Scale, AN[3].Scale}, {}, {}, 0

	                  for HN, tN in ipairs(GN()) do
	                    HN = tN.weight * cN(qN, tN.min, tN.max)
	                    rN += HN
	                    local GN, _N = #bN + 1, {Min = tN.min, Max = tN.max, Weight = HN}
	                    _N.Color, bN[GN] = T1(tN.min), _N
	                  end

	                  table.sort(bN, function(HN, tN)
	                    return HN.Weight > tN.Weight
	                  end)

	                  for HN, tN in ipairs(bN) do
	                    local GN, _N, cN = ((rN > 0) and ((tN.Weight / rN) * 100)) or 0, A1(category, tN.Min), A1(category, tN.Max)
	                    HN, qN = UN(tN.Color, fn(tN)), _N and cN
	                    local tN, A1 = (qN and (KN .. XN(aN, MN(_N) .. (" - " .. MN(cN))))) or "", ((GN >= 10) and I) or ((GN >= 1) and yN)
	                    DN(HN .. (tN .. (KN .. UN(A1 or C, (((GN >= 1) and "%.1f%%") or "%.3f%%"):format(GN)))))
	                  end

	                  SN("Size Doubling", UN(yN, ("%.2f%%"):format(VN * 100)) .. XN(C, (" per roll, x2 again and again up to %dx"):format(nN)))
	                  local aN, MN = 0, 0

	                  for HN, tN in ipairs(bN) do
	                    qN, HN = ((rN > 0) and (tN.Weight / rN)) or 0, PN(tN.Max - tN.Min, 1e-09)
	                    local rN, GN = math.clamp((tN.Max - EN) / HN, 0, 1), VN
	                    aN += qN * (rN + (GN * (math.clamp((tN.Max - (EN / 2)) / HN, 0, 1) - rN)))
	                    HN = (tN.Min + tN.Max) / 2
	                    tN, GN = (1 - VN) * uN(O1, HN), VN
	                    rN = GN * uN(O1, math.min(HN * 2, nN))
	                    MN = MN + (qN * (tN + rN))
	                  end

	                  RN("RESULT PREDICTION", TN)
	                  SN("Bigger Than Average Input", UN(((aN >= 0.5) and I) or yN, ("%.1f%%"):format(aN * 100)))
	                  SN("Expected Income", UN(I, FN(MN)))
	                  SN("Mutation", XN(TN, "Normal, input mutations are not kept"))

	                  for TN, aN in ipairs(AN) do
	                    ZN[TN] = aN.Raw
	                  end

	                  qN = s[26]
	                  local TN = qN("Fuse", "PriceFor", ZN)

	                  if type(TN) == "number" then
	                    SN("Fuse Cost", UN(yN, (FN(TN):gsub("/s$", ""))))
	                  end

	                  TN = bN[1]

	                  if TN then
	                    SN("Estimated Value", UN(I, FN(uN(O1, TN.Min)) .. (" ~ " .. FN(uN(O1, TN.Max)))) .. (KN .. (XN(C, "at ") .. UN(TN.Color, fn(TN)))))

	                    for yN, yN in ipairs(bN) do
	                      TN = if yN.Max > TN.Max then yN else TN
	                    end

	                    SN("Best Case", UN(TN.Color, fn(TN)) .. ("  " .. UN(I, FN(uN(O1, TN.Max)))))
	                  end
	                else
	                  YN(("Load %d more of the same species to see the odds"):format(3 - #AN))
	                end
	              end
	            end
	          else
	            BN()
	            DN(UN(C, "Fuse machine data is not available yet"))
	          end

	          fn8 = gN + 1

	          for C = fn8, #kN, 1 do
	            kN[C].Set({Visible = false})
	          end

	          for C = NN + 1, #vN, 1 do
	            vN[C].Frame.Set({Visible = false})
	          end

	          fn7()
	          jN = 2
	        end

	        fN = function()
	          if (jN > 0) or xN then
	            if jN > 0 then
	              jN -= 1
	            end

	            pcall(fn7)
	          end
	        end

	        lN["Fuse Predictor"]:Surface({Name = "Fuse Predictor", Layout = "free", Style = QN(42), Build = function(C)
	          LN = C
	          C:SetDock(5, {Gap = 0.22, DividerColor = A(170, 174, 184)})
	          local A = C:Dock()
	          hN.Icon, hN.Name, hN.Rarity, hN.Info = k(A, nil, 5, 0.26, 8), mN(A, 5.8, nil, 9), oN(A, 5.8, nil, 9), C:Text({Parent = A, X = 5.8, Y = 1.2, Height = 3.8, Wrap = false, ZIndex = 9})

	          C:OnResize(function(A, A, k)
	            if A == zN then
	              if k == JN then
	                return
	              end
	            end

	            local I, yN = A / PN(k, 1), 0.9 / PN(C:TextSize(), 1)
	            zN, JN, wN, WN, jN, IN = A, k, I, k, 2, yN
	            hN.Rarity.Set({StrokeThickness = IN})

	            for A, A in ipairs(vN) do
	              A.Rarity.Set({StrokeThickness = IN})
	            end
	          end)

	          pN = true
	        end})
	      end
	    end)

	    P("22_prio#1", function()
	      local A = Color3.fromRGB
	      local C = UDim2.fromOffset
	      local k = UDim2.fromScale
	      local I, yN = UDim2.new, Enum
	      local AN = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	      local TN = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
	      local aN = A(62, 64, 84)
	      local qN = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	      local PN = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
	      local mN = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
	      local BN, bN = yN.TextXAlignment.Center, {}
	      y:lo(bN, 0, A(150, 255, 110), A(40, 200, 60))
	      local gN = {}
	      y:lo(gN, 0, A(70, 72, 90), A(46, 48, 62))
	      local oN = {}
	      y:lo(oN, 0, A(96, 100, 124), A(52, 54, 70))
	      local pN = {}

	        end

	        return xN
	      end

	            end

	          else
	            v2.Frame.Position = C1
	          end

	        end

	      local function gN(oN, nN, UN)
	        local function fn(A1)
	          return math.floor(UN * A1)
	        end

	        IN, _N, wN = position, position2, n2

	        n2 = ((C1 + position) + T1) + fn(1.1)

	          v2.Sub.Position, v2.Sub.Size = I(0, n2, 0.56, 0), I(0, position2, 0.3, 0)

	      local function oN(ZN, _N)
	        local wN, WN = {}, {}

	        for IN, IN in ipairs(((type(ZN) == "table") and ZN) or {}) do
	          if EN[IN] then
	            if not (WN[IN]) then
	              WN[IN] = true
	              wN[#wN + 1] = IN
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        for IN, cN in ipairs(MN) do
	          if not (WN[cN]) then
	            ZN = 1

	            for WN = IN - 1, 1, -1 do
	              local IN = table.find(wN, MN[WN])
	              if IN then
	                ZN = IN + 1
	                break
	              end
	            end

	            table.insert(wN, ZN, cN)
	          end
	        end

	        HN, KN = wN, {}

	        for ZN, WN in ipairs(wN) do
	          KN[WN] = ZN
	        end

	        if _N then
	          return
	        end
	        T.wr(XN, T.enc({Version = 2, Order = wN}))

	        if tN then
	          pcall(VN, true)
	        end

	        pcall(function()
	          JN(wN)
	          local ZN = s[15]

	          if ZN.order then
	            local ZN = s[15]
	            ZN.order()
	          end
	        end)
	      end

	                return
	              end
	            else
	              return
	            end

	            C[I] = C[aN]

	                if os.clock() >= rN then
	                  pcall(VN, false)
	                end

	      end

	      oN(((type(uN) == "table") and (uN.Version == 2)) and uN.Order, true)

	        tN = FN("Frame", nil, {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = k(1, 0)})

	          zN, rN = nil, 0

	        C:OnResize(function(A, C, k)
	          gN(A, C, math.max(8, k))
	        end)
	      end})

	      local A, C = {pick = "picked egg", index = "missing index egg", wisp = "wisp egg", filter = "filter egg", ufo = "UFO egg", wait = "egg at daybreak"}, {Hollow = "The Hollow Race", Star = "Shooting Star", Place = "Auto Place Egg", Mutate = "Auto Mutate", Bloom = "Butterfly Bloom", WispGo = "Wisp quest", UFO = "UFO Sightings", Machine = "Butterfly machine", IslandWars = "Cursed Island Wars", Capture = "Capture The Egg", Stage = "Wait for the field", NightHome = "Night, safe zone", GoTo = "Go to egg", Combat = "Auto hit", Banjo = "Banjo cricket"}

	      local function I(yN, AN)
	        if yN.name == "Steal" then
	          local TN

	          if AN then
	            TN = AN
	          else
	            local AN = s[15]
	            TN = AN.stealKind
	          end

	          return "Steal " .. (A[TN] or "egg")
	        end

	        return C[yN.name] or yN.name
	      end

	      local A = n({name = "PrioInfo", gap = 1, info = true})

	      A.on = function()
	        local C = ON("Farm")
	        local yN

	        if C then
	          yN = C
	        else
	          local C = s[28]
	          yN = C.farmHud
	        end

	        return yN
	      end

	      A.fn = function()
	        local A = s[15]
	        local C, yN, AN, TN = A.queue or {}, {}, s[4]

	        if AN then
	          TN = AN
	        else
	          local aN, aN = s[15], (os.clock() - (s[15].mvEnd or 0)) < 2

	          if aN then
	            A = s[15]
	            TN = A.lastMV
	          else
	            TN = aN
	          end
	        end

	        AN, A = TN or nil, ipairs

	        for aN, qN in A(C) do
	          if qN ~= AN then
	            if qN.name ~= "Stage" then
	              if qN.name ~= "NightHome" then
	                local PN, mN, BN = #yN + 1, qN.name == "Steal"

	                if mN then
	                  aN = s[15]
	                  BN = aN.stealNext
	                else
	                  BN = mN
	                end

	                yN[PN] = I(qN, BN or nil)
	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        C, TN = AN and (I(AN, (((AN.name == "Steal") and (type(AN.plan) == "table")) and AN.plan.kind) or nil) .. ((AN.idle and " (waiting)") or ""))

	        if C then
	          TN = C
	        else
	          AN = nil
	          AN, TN = s[15], ((os.clock() - (s[15].treadAt or 0)) < 3) and "Treadmill"
	        end

	        A, C, AN = TN or "Idle", s[15], s[15]
	        C.doing = A
	        AN.next = yN
	        k("Doing now: " .. (A .. (" | Next 1: " .. ((yN[1] or "Nothing") .. (" | Next 2: " .. ((yN[2] or "--") .. (" | Next 3: " .. ((yN[3] or "--") .. (((#yN > 3) and (" +" .. (#yN - 3))) or "")))))))))
	      end

	      JN(HN)
	    end)

	    local function A(C)
	      local k, I, yN = ipairs, {}, s[1]
	      y:lo(I, 0, yN.Character or (L()), L())

	      for yN, yN in k(I) do
	        for k, k in ipairs((yN and yN:GetChildren()) or {}) do
	          if k:IsA("Tool") then
	            if C(k) then
	              return k
	            end
	          end
	        end
	      end
	    end

	    local C, k = {}, {}
	    local I, yN = -1000000000, false
	    s[15].weaponId, s[15].weaponName = {}, {}
	    s[15].wVer = 0

	    local function AN(TN, aN, qN)
	      if C[TN] == nil then
	        k[#k + 1] = TN
	      end

	      C[TN] = aN
	      local aN, PN = s[15]
	      PN, aN.weaponName[TN], s[15].weaponId[qN] = s[15], qN, TN
	      TN = s[15]
	      TN.wVer = TN.wVer + 1
	    end

	    local function TN()
	      if yN then
	        return
	      elseif (os.clock() - I) < 5 then
	        return
	      end

	      I = os.clock()
	      local I = s[33]
	      local aN, qN = I.Gears

	      if aN then
	        I = s[33]
	        qN = I.Gears.Directory
	      else
	        qN = aN
	      end

	      if type(qN) ~= "table" then
	        return
	      end

	      for aN, PN in next, qN, nil do
	        I = (type(PN) == "table") and PN.BatControllerData

	        if (I or ((type(PN) == "table") and (PN.ToolController == "Slap"))) or tostring(aN):lower():find("scrambl") then
	          AN(aN, (I and tonumber(I.RangeBonus)) or -1, ((type(PN) == "table") and PN.DisplayName) or aN)
	        end
	      end

	      table.sort(k, function(I, aN)
	        return (C[I] > C[aN]) or ((C[I] == C[aN]) and (I < aN))
	      end)

	      yN = #k > 0
	    end

	    TN()

	    local function I(yN)
	      return yN:GetAttribute("GearName") or yN.Name
	    end

	    s[15].ownedWeapons = function()
	      TN()
	      local yN = s[64]
	      local aN = yN()
	      local qN, PN, mN, BN, bN = (((type(aN) == "table") and (type(aN.GearInventory) == "table")) and table.clone(aN.GearInventory)) or {}, {}, ipairs, {}, s[1]
	      y:lo(BN, 0, bN.Character, L())

	      for bN, bN in mN(BN) do
	        for mN, mN in ipairs((bN and bN:GetChildren()) or {}) do
	          if mN:IsA("Tool") then
	            aN = I(mN)
	            qN[aN] = true

	            if C[aN] == nil then
	              if mN:GetAttribute("IsBat") == true then
	                AN(aN, 0, aN)
	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end
	      end

	      yN = ipairs

	      for AN, aN in yN(k) do
	        if qN[aN] then
	          AN = nil
	          AN, PN[#PN + 1] = s[15], s[15].weaponName[aN]
	        end
	      end

	      return PN
	    end

	    local function yN(AN)
	      local aN, qN = (C[I(AN)] ~= nil) or (AN:GetAttribute("IsBat") == true)

	      if aN then
	        qN = aN
	      else
	        local aN = AN:GetAttribute("ItemType")
	        qN = (if aN then not aN else not AN:GetAttribute("GearName")) and ((AN.Name:lower():find("bat") or AN.Name:lower():find("sword")) or AN.Name:lower():find("hammer"))
	      end

	      return qN
	    end

	    s[15].scrambler = function(AN)
	      return AN and (C[I(AN)] == -1)
	    end

	    local function AN(aN)
	      return f() >= (tonumber(aN:GetAttribute("CooldownEndTime")) or 0)
	    end

	    local aN = {i = 1, at = 0}

	    s[15].bat = function()
	      TN()
	      local TN, qN, PN, mN = {}, ipairs, {}, s[1]
	      y:lo(PN, 0, mN.Character or (L()), L())
	      mN = nil
	      local BN = -2

	      for bN, gN in qN(PN) do
	        local oN, pN = ipairs, (gN and gN:GetChildren()) or {}

	        for NN, fN in oN(pN) do
	          if fN:IsA("Tool") then
	            if yN(fN) then
	              gN, bN = C[I(fN)] or 0, s[28]

	              if bN.weap then
	                NN = s[28]

	                if NN.weap[I(fN)] then
	                  if not (TN[I(fN)]) then
	                    TN[I(fN)] = fN
	                  end
	                end
	              end

	              if gN >= 0 then
	                if mN then
	                  if gN > BN then
	                    mN, BN = fN, gN
	                  else
	                  end
	                else
	                  mN, BN = fN, gN
	                end
	              else
	              end
	            else
	            end
	          else
	          end
	        end
	      end

	      qN = {}

	      for yN, yN in ipairs(k) do
	        if TN[yN] then
	          qN[#qN + 1] = TN[yN]
	        end
	      end

	      if #qN == 0 then
	        return mN, math.max(BN, 0)
	      end

	      if #qN > 1 then
	        if aN.i > #qN then
	          aN.i = 1
	        end

	        local k, yN, BN, bN = (aN.i % #qN) + 1, os.clock() - aN.at, aN.i == 2

	        if BN then
	          TN = s[28]
	          bN = TN.swapT2
	        else
	          bN = BN
	        end

	        PN = nil

	        if bN then
	          PN = bN
	        else
	          BN = s[28]
	          PN = BN.swapT
	        end

	        mN, bN = yN >= (PN or 0.35)

	        if mN then
	          bN = mN
	        else
	          yN = AN(qN[aN.i])
	          bN = if yN then not yN else AN(qN[k])
	        end

	        if bN then
	          aN.i, aN.at = k, os.clock()
	        end
	      else
	        aN.i = 1
	      end

	      mN = qN[aN.i]
	      return mN, math.max(C[I(mN)] or 0, 0)
	    end

	            else

	            else
	              if FN.Magnitude > 2 then
	                local mN, mN = s[28], math.clamp(FN.Magnitude * 8, 16, s[28].bfSpeed)
	                gN.WalkSpeed = mN
	                gN:MoveTo(ZN.Position + FN)
	                local hN
	                hN, ZN.AssemblyLinearVelocity = s[29], (FN.Unit * mN) + s[29](0, rN.Y, 0)
	                hN = s[29]
	                fN = if hN(rN.X, 0, rN.Z).Magnitude > (mN * 0.3) then os.clock() else fN
	              else
	                gN:MoveTo(ZN.Position)
	                fN = os.clock()
	              end
	            end

	            if (os.clock() - fN) > 0.5 then
	              fN = os.clock()
	              gN.Jump = true
	            else
	            end

	            GN(FN)
	            FN = s[20]
	            FN()
	            continue
	          end

	          gN.PlatformStand = true
	          qN = s[29]
	          local mN, fN = uN(pN + qN(0, 0, 6)), os.clock() - BN

	          if vN == "Patrol" then
	            aN, qN = s[29]
	            qN, mN = s[28], uN(pN + aN(math.sin((fN * s[28].bfSpeed) / 180) * 170, 0, 0))
	          else
	            if vN == "Circle" then
	              local BN, hN, FN = s[29]
	              hN, FN, mN = s[28], s[28], uN(pN + BN(math.cos(((fN * s[28].bfSpeed) / 25) / 4) * 25, 0, math.sin(((fN * s[28].bfSpeed) / 25) / 4) * 25))
	            else
	            end
	          end

	          aN = mN - ZN.Position

	          if vN == "Stand" then
	            GN(aN)
	          end

	          if aN.Magnitude > 8 then
	            vN = nil
	            vN, ZN.AssemblyLinearVelocity = s[28], aN.Unit * math.min(s[28].bfSpeed, aN.Magnitude / 0.05)
	          else
	            fN, qN = s[17], s[18]
	            fN(qN(mN))
	          end

	          qN = s[20]
	          qN()
	        end

	        tN:Disconnect()

	        if gN then
	          gN.PlatformStand = false
	          oN = s[42]
	          gN:Move(oN)

	          if k then
	            gN.WalkSpeed = k
	          end
	        end
	      end

	      I.on = function()
	        local k = s[28]
	        return k.bloom
	      end

	      I.ready = function()
	        local k = s[15]
	        return k.bloomWants() and (w() > 0)
	      end

	      local k = n({name = "BloomInfo", gap = 1})

	      k.on = function()
	        return ON("Event")
	      end

	      k.fn = function()
	        local k = NN()
	        local aN, qN = k.Counts or {}, s[15]
	        local mN = qN.bfRec or {}

	        local function BN(gN)
	          gN = math.max(0, math.floor(gN + 0.5))
	          return ("%d:%02d"):format(gN // 60, gN % 60)
	        end

	        local gN, oN = tonumber(workspace:GetAttribute("ButterflyBloomEndsAt")), f()
	        local pN, fN = ((workspace:GetAttribute("Event_ButterflyBloom") == true) and ((gN and ("Butterfly Bloom live, " .. (BN(gN - oN) .. " left"))) or "Butterfly Bloom live")) or "Waiting for Butterfly Bloom", s[28]
	        gN = fN.bloom
	        oN, qN = if gN then not gN else "Off  |  " .. pN

	        if oN then
	          qN = oN
	        else
	          fN, BN = s[4] == I

	          if fN then
	            gN = s[15]
	            BN = gN.bfSay
	          else
	            BN = fN
	          end

	            local I = s[15]
	            qN = I.bfSay .. ("  |  " .. pN)
	          else
	            qN = BN
	          end
	        end

	        local I, BN = qN or pN, {}
	        BN.Green = aN.Green
	        BN.Blue = aN.Blue
	        BN.Purple = aN.Purple
	        BN.Golden = aN.Golden
	        BN.Essence = k.Essence

	        J("bst", function()
	          if not (TN.st) then
	            return
	          end
	          TN.st.Set({Text = I})
	          local k

	          for I, aN in next, TN.texts, nil do
	            local qN = (mN[I] and ("%d/%d"):format(BN[I] or 0, mN[I])) or tostring(BN[I] or 0)

	            if TN.shown[I] ~= qN then
	              TN.shown[I] = qN
	              aN.Set({Text = qN})
	              k = true
	            else
	            end
	          end

	          if k then
	            TN.lay()
	          end
	        end)
	      end

	      local k, I, TN = lN["Butterfly Bloom"], false, false

	      local function aN()
	        local qN, mN = s[28], s[28]
	        qN.bfTrade, mN.bfSmart = I and not TN, I and TN
	        S()
	      end

	      local qN, mN = k:Toggle("Auto Trade Up", false, function(BN)
	        I = BN
	        aN()
	      end), {}

	      yN, PN = {"Emerald To Sapphire", "Sapphire To Amethyst", "Amethyst To Radiant"}, function(I)
	        mN = {}

	        for BN, gN in ipairs(I) do

	          BN[#BN + 1] = C[gN:match("^(%a+)")]
	        end

	      k:Toggle("Smart Trade For Essence", false, function(C)
	        TN = C
	        aN()
	      end, nil, qN)

	      x(k, "Auto Craft Essence", "bfCraft", false)
	      local C, k = {Green = 10, Blue = 10, Purple = 50}, {Green = 240, Blue = 115, Purple = 40, Golden = 1}

	      pcall(function()
	        local I, yN = require(q.Shared.Flags.ButterflyFlags), {Green = "GreenToBlueCost", Blue = "BlueToPurpleCost", Purple = "PurpleToGoldenCost"}

	        end
	      end)

	      AN = s[15]
	      AN.bfRec = k

	      local function I(yN)
	        for AN, TN in next, k, nil do
	          if (yN[AN] or 0) < TN then
	            return false
	          end
	        end

	        return true
	      end

	      local function yN(AN)
	        local TN, aN, PN, BN = AN.Green or 0, AN.Blue or 0, AN.Purple or 0, AN.Golden or 0

	        for gN = 60, 1, -1 do
	          AN = math.max(0, (gN * k.Golden) - BN)
	          local BN = math.max(0, ((gN * k.Purple) + (C.Purple * AN)) - PN)
	          local PN = math.max(0, ((gN * k.Blue) + (C.Blue * BN)) - aN)
	          if TN >= ((gN * k.Green) + (C.Green * PN)) then
	            return {Green = PN, Blue = BN, Purple = AN}
	          end
	        end
	      end

	      local AN = n({name = "Machine", mover = true, gap = 20, pri = 6, soft = true})

	      AN.on = function()
	        local TN = s[28]

	      end

	      AN.want = function()
	        if bN() then
	          return
	        else
	          local TN = s[1]
	          if TN:GetAttribute("EnchantedTreeUnlocked") ~= true then
	            return
	          end
	        end

	        if aN.bfCraft then
	          if I(TN) then
	            return true
	          end
	        end

	        if aN.bfSmart then
	          local PN = yN(TN)
	          return (PN and (((PN.Green + PN.Blue) + PN.Purple) > 0)) or nil
	        end
	        local PN, BN = ipairs, mN

	            if aN >= C[bN] then

	            end
	          end
	        end
	      end

	      local function TN(aN)
	        local PN = s[1]
	        if PN:GetAttribute("InEnchantedTree") then
	          return true
	        end
	        PN = s[52]
	        local BN = PN("Build", "EnchantedTreeInterior", "Markers", "EnterTrigger", "EnchantedTreeEntrance", "EnchantedTreePrompt")
	        PN = BN and BN.Parent
	        local bN = PN and ((PN:IsA("Attachment") and PN.WorldPosition) or (PN:IsA("BasePart") and PN.Position))
	        if not (bN) then
	          return false
	        end
	        PN = s[1]
	        local gN = PN.Character

	        local function oN()
	          local pN = aN()
	          local aN

	          if pN then
	            local fN = s[1]
	            aN = fN.Character == gN
	          else
	            aN = pN
	          end

	          return aN
	        end

	        if not (XN(oN)) then
	          return false
	        end
	        PN = s[19]
	        local aN = PN()

	        if aN then
	          PN = s[21]

	          if PN(aN.Position) then
	            local pN = s[22]
	            if not (pN(oN, "Enchanted Tree", 0.3)) then
	              return false
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        if not (XN(oN)) then
	          return false
	        end
	        PN = s[29]
	        aN = bN + PN(0, 0, -12)

	        for pN = 1, 2, 1 do
	          pN = s[19]
	          PN = pN()

	          if PN then
	            if oN() then
	              if w() > 0 then
	                if not ((PN.Position - aN).Magnitude > 500) then
	                  break
	                end
	              else
	                break
	              end
	            else
	              break
	            end
	          else
	            break
	          end

	          pN = s[11]
	          pN("Ragdoll TP to the Enchanted Tree")
	          pN = s[29]
	          CN(aN + pN(0, 3, 0), oN)
	        end

	        PN = s[1]
	        if PN.Character ~= gN then
	          return false
	        end
	        PN = s[19]
	        local gN = PN()

	        if gN then
	          if (gN.Position - aN).Magnitude > 60 then
	            if not (QN({Pos = aN, Name = "the tree"}, oN)) then
	              return false
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        for pN = 1, 4, 1 do
	          pN, oN, gN = s[17], s[18], s[29]
	          pN(oN(bN + gN(0, 3, -6)))
	          pcall(fireproximityprompt, BN)
	          pN = os.clock() + 4

	          while true do
	            PN, aN = os.clock() < pN

	            if PN then
	              local BN = s[1]
	              aN = not BN:GetAttribute("InEnchantedTree")
	            else
	              aN = PN
	            end

	            if not aN then
	              break
	            end
	            local aN = s[20]
	            aN()
	          end

	          pN = s[1]
	          if pN:GetAttribute("InEnchantedTree") then
	            return true
	          end
	        end

	        return false
	      end

	        local aN = s[52]
	        local qN = aN("Build", "EnchantedTreeInterior", "Markers", "ExitTrigger")

	        if qN then
	          if qN:IsA("BasePart") then
	            aN = s[1]
	            if not (aN:GetAttribute("InEnchantedTree")) then
	              return
	            end
	          else

	          end
	        else
	          return
	        end

	        aN = s[20]
	        aN()
	        aN = s[17]
	        aN(qN.CFrame)
	        BN = os.clock() + 1.5

	        while true do
	          aN = s[1]
	          if not (aN:GetAttribute("InEnchantedTree") and (os.clock() < BN)) then
	            break
	          end
	          PN = s[20]
	          PN()
	        end

	        PN = s[1]

	        if PN:GetAttribute("InEnchantedTree") then
	          aN = s[19]
	          F(aN(), qN)
	        end
	      end

	      local function F(aN, qN, PN, ...)
	        local BN = MN(qN)
	        if not (BN) then
	          return false
	        end

	        for qN = 1, 3, 1 do
	          EN(BN, PN)
	          qN = s[9]
	          local PN, BN, bN = qN(aN, ...)

	          if PN then
	            if BN ~= false then
	              return true
	            end
	          end

	          if not (tostring(bN):lower():find("closer")) then
	            return false
	          end
	        end

	        return false
	      end

	      AN.run = function(AN, aN)
	        AN = s[15]
	        if not (AN.needBody()) then
	          return
	        end

	        if s[13](aN) then
	          if not (TN(aN)) then
	            AN = s[11]
	            return AN("Could not enter the tree", "warn")
	          end
	        else
	          local TN = s[11]
	          return TN("Could not enter the tree", "warn")
	        end

	        AN = s[28]
	        local TN, qN = AN.bfSmart and yN(NN().Counts or {}), ipairs
	        AN = (TN and {"Green", "Blue", "Purple"}) or {}

	        for yN, PN in qN(AN) do
	          yN = TN[PN]

	          for BN = 1, yN, 1 do
	            BN = s[28]

	            if BN.bfSmart then
	              if aN() then
	                if not (F("RF/Butterflies/AskTradeUp", "ButterflyMachine", aN, PN)) then
	                  break
	                end
	              else
	                break
	              end
	            else
	              break
	            end

	            task.wait(0.15)
	          end
	        end

	        TN, AN = ipairs, s[28]
	        qN = (AN.bfSmart and {}) or mN

	        for yN, PN in TN(qN) do
	          while true do
	            AN = s[28]
	            local TN, qN = (AN.bfTrade and aN()) and (NN().Counts or {})[PN]

	            if TN then
	              yN = nil
	              yN, qN = s[28], (NN().Counts[PN] - ((s[28].bfCraft and k[PN]) or 0)) >= C[PN]
	            else
	              qN = TN
	            end

	            if not qN then
	              break
	            end

	            if not (F("RF/Butterflies/AskTradeUp", "ButterflyMachine", aN, PN)) then
	              break
	            end
	            task.wait(0.15)
	          end
	        end

	        for C = 1, 50, 1 do
	          AN = s[28]

	          if AN.bfCraft then
	            if aN() then
	              if not (I(NN().Counts or {})) then
	                break
	              end
	            else
	              break
	            end
	          else
	            break
	          end

	          if not (F("RF/Butterflies/AskCraftEssence", "MutationShrine", aN)) then
	            break
	          end
	          task.wait(0.3)
	        end

	        AN = s[15]
	        AN.leaveTree()
	      end
	    end)

	    P("31_bloom#2", function()

	      C.on = function()

	      end

	      F = s[33]
	      local yN = F.Wisp

	      local function AN()
	        local TN = s[64]
	        local aN = TN()
	        TN = aN and aN.WispCompanion
	        return aN, ((type(TN) == "table") and TN) or nil
	      end

	      local function TN(aN)
	        if aN then
	          if aN.Accepted then
	            if aN.Unlocked then
	              return
	            end
	          else
	            return
	          end
	        else
	          return
	        end

	        return (((type(yN) == "table") and yN.List) or {})[tonumber(aN.Stage) or 1]
	      end

	      local function aN(qN, PN)
	        return ((type(qN.Progress) == "table") and tonumber(qN.Progress[PN.Id])) or 0
	      end

	      local function qN(PN, mN)
	        return (type(PN.Claimed) == "table") and PN.Claimed[mN.Id]
	      end

	      local PN = {}

	      local function mN(BN)
	        if not (PN[BN]) then
	          local bN = {}

	          pcall(function()
	            for gN, gN in ipairs(require(q.Data.Areas.Configs[BN]).DropTable) do
	              bN[tostring(gN[1] or gN.Category)] = true
	            end
	          end)

	          PN[BN] = bN
	        end

	        return PN[BN]
	      end

	      local PN = {at = 0}

	      local function BN(bN)
	        if PN.q == bN then
	          if (os.clock() - PN.at) < 2 then
	            local gN = PN
	            return gN
	          end
	        end

	        local gN = s[64]
	        local oN, pN = gN(), mN((type(bN.Match) == "table") and bN.Match.AreaId)
	        local mN, NN, fN = H(oN or {}), {}, {}

	        for H, hN in next, ((oN and (type(oN.Inventory) == "table")) and oN.Inventory) or {}, nil do
	          gN = (type(hN) == "table") and tostring(hN.AssetCategory)

	          if gN then
	            if pN[gN] then
	              if not (hN.IsFavorite) then
	                if not (hN.InFuse) then
	                  if not (mN[H]) then
	                    NN[gN] = NN[gN] or {}
	                    table.insert(NN[gN], H)
	                    fN[gN] = (fN[gN] or 0) + 1
	                  end
	                end
	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        for H, H in pairs(v()) do
	          gN = (type(H) == "table") and tostring(H.AssetCategory)

	          if gN then
	            if pN[gN] then
	              fN[gN] = (fN[gN] or 0) + 1
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        PN = {q = bN, at = os.clock(), supply = 0}

	        for H, mN in next, NN, nil do
	          if #mN >= 3 then
	            oN, gN = PN, PN
	            oN.ready = {mN[1], mN[2], mN[3]}
	            gN.cat = H
	          end
	        end

	        for H, H in next, fN, nil do
	          pN = PN
	          pN.supply = math.max(PN.supply, H)
	        end

	        oN = PN
	        return oN
	      end

	      local function H(mN)
	        local bN, bN = AN()
	        local gN = TN(bN)

	        if gN then
	          if qN(bN, gN) then
	            return false
	          end
	        else
	          return false
	        end

	        local oN = ((type(gN.Match) == "table") and gN.Match) or {}
	        if gN.Event == "PetFused" then
	          return (mN.a == oN.AreaId) and (BN(gN).supply < 3)
	        end

	        if gN.Event ~= "EggSecured" then
	          return false
	        elseif aN(bN, gN) >= (tonumber(gN.Target) or 1) then
	          return false
	        end

	        for bN, pN in next, oN, nil do
	          if bN == "AreaId" then
	            if pN ~= mN.a then
	              return false
	            end
	          elseif bN == "Rarity" then
	            gN = r(mN.cat)
	            gN = gN and gN.Rarity

	            if gN then
	              if not (gN.DisplayName == pN) then
	                if not (gN._id == pN) then
	                  return false
	                end
	              end
	            else
	              return false
	            end
	          else
	            return false
	          end
	        end

	        return true
	      end

	      local mN = s[15]

	      mN.wispSteals = function()
	        local mN = s[28]
	        if not (mN.wisp) then
	          return false
	        end
	        local bN, bN = AN()
	        mN = TN(bN)

	        if mN then
	          if qN(bN, mN) then
	            return false
	          end

	        end

	        end
	        return (mN.Event == "PetFused") and (BN(mN).supply < 3)
	      end

	      F = s[15]
	      local mN, bN = F.wantEgg, s[15]

	      bN.wantEgg = function(F)

	            return "wisp"
	          end
