	      end

	      local function F(...)
	        local H = s[52]
	        local mN = H("Build", ...)
	        return mN and ((mN:IsA("BasePart") and mN.Position) or (mN:IsA("Model") and mN:GetPivot().Position))
	      end

	      local function H(mN)
	        local bN = s[64]
	        local gN = bN()
	        if not (gN) then
	          return
	        end

	        if gN.FusionLocked then
	          if type(gN.FusionEggReward) == "table" then
	            bN = s[9]
	            bN("RF/Fusery/FinishReveal")
	          end

	          return k("Fusing for the wisp quest")
	        end

	        bN = BN(mN)
	        if not (bN.ready) then
	          return k(((bN.supply >= 3) and "Hatching eggs for the fuse quest") or "Stealing eggs for the fuse quest")
	        end
	        local mN, BN = {}, {}

	        for oN, oN in ipairs(bN.ready) do
	          mN[oN] = true
	        end

	        local oN, pN = ipairs, ((type(gN.FusionSlots) == "table") and gN.FusionSlots) or {}

	        for NN, fN in oN(pN) do
	          if mN[fN] then
	            BN[fN] = true
	          else
	            NN = s[9]
	            NN("RF/Fusery/EjectPet", fN)
	            task.wait(0.35)
	          end
	        end

	        if gN.FusionInfoAcknowledged == false then
	          oN = s[9]
	          oN("RF/Fusery/ConfirmBriefing")
	          task.wait(0.35)
	        end

	        pN, gN = ipairs, bN.ready

	        for oN, oN in pN(gN) do
	          if not (BN[oN]) then
	            mN = s[9]
	            mN("RF/Fusery/LoadPet", oN)
	            task.wait(0.35)
	          end
	        end

	        mN = s[9]
	        mN("RF/Fusery/BeginFuse")
	        PN.at = 0
	        pN = s[71]
	        k("Fusing 3 " .. (pN(bN.cat) .. " for the wisp quest"))
	      end

	      C.fn = function()
	        local C, PN = AN()
	        if not (PN) then
	          return k("Reading quest")
	        end
	        local AN = ((type(yN) == "table") and tonumber(yN.StageCount)) or 4
	        if PN.Unlocked then
	          Y("wst", I, {Stage = ("%d / %d"):format(AN, AN), Progress = "Complete"})
	          return k("Done, the Enchanted Tree is open")
	        end

	        if not (PN.Accepted) then
	          Y("wst", I, {Stage = ("0 / %d"):format(AN), Progress = "Not accepted"})
	        end

	        local mN = TN(PN)

	        if mN then
	          Y("wst", I, {Stage = ("%d / %d"):format(tonumber(PN.Stage) or 1, AN), Progress = ("%d / %d"):format(aN(PN, mN), tonumber(mN.Target) or 1)})
	        end

	        mN = s[28]
	        if not (mN.wisp) then
	          return k("Off")
	        end

	        if C.FusionLocked then
	          if type(C.FusionEggReward) == "table" then
	            mN = s[9]
	            mN("RF/Fusery/FinishReveal")
	          end
	        end

	        if not (PN.Accepted) then
	          mN = ((type(yN) == "table") and tonumber(yN.RequiredSpeedPower)) or 50000000000
	          if (tonumber(C.SpeedPower) or 0) < mN then
	            return k("Needs " .. (Z(mN) .. " speed power to meet the wisp"))
	          end
	          local I = s[52]
	          local yN = I("Build")

	          if yN then
	            if not (yN:GetAttribute("EnchantedForestZoneRevealed")) then
	              return k("Waiting for the Enchanted Forest to open")
	            end
	          else
	            return k("Waiting for the Enchanted Forest to open")
	          end

	          yN = s[15]
	          yN.wispGo = {F("EnchantedForestZone", "WispSpawn"), "Accept"}
	          return k("Going to accept the quest")
	        end

	        mN = TN(PN)

	        if mN then
	          if not (qN(PN, mN)) then
	            if aN(PN, mN) >= (tonumber(mN.Target) or 1) then
	              C = s[9]
	              C("RF/WispCompanion/Request", "Claim", mN.Id)
	              return k("Claimed " .. tostring(mN.Title))
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        if (tonumber(PN.Stage) or 1) >= AN then
	          if not (PN.Returning) then
	            C = s[9]
	            C("RF/WispCompanion/Request", "BeginReturn")
	          end

	          local C = s[15]
	          C.wispGo = {F("EnchantedForestZone", "Zone13Props", "EnchantedTree", "WispAnchor") or F("EnchantedForestZone", "WispAnchor"), "Return"}
	          return k("Returning the wisp")
	        end

	        if not mN then
	          return k("Reading quest")
	        end

	        if mN.Event == "PetFused" then
	          return H(mN)
	        end
	        k(tostring(mN.Title) .. ((((type(mN.Match) == "table") and mN.Match.Rarity) and " - waiting for that egg in this server") or ""))
	      end

	      local C = n({name = "WispGo", mover = true, gap = 3, pri = 4})

	      C.on = function()
	        local F = s[28]
	        return F.wisp
	      end

	      C.want = function()
	        local F = s[15]
	        local H, k = F.wispGo

	        if H then
	          F = s[15]
	          k = F.wispGo[1]
	        else
	          k = H
	        end

	        F = nil

	        if k then
	          H = s[15]
	          F = H.wispGo
	        else
	          F = k
	        end

	        return F or nil
	      end

	      C.run = function(C, F)
	        local H = C[1]

	        if s[13](F) then
	          local k = s[14]
	          if not (k(F)) then
	            return
	          end
	        else
	          return
	        end

	        local k = s[19]
	        local I = k()

	        if I then
	          k = s[21]

	          if k(I.Position) then
	            local yN = s[22]
	            if not (yN(F, "Wisp", 0.3)) then
	              return
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        I, k = s[1], s[1]
	        pcall(s[1].RequestStreamAroundAsync, s[1], H)
	        local k = s[19]
	        local I = k()
	        if not (I) then
	          return
	        end

	        if (I.Position - H).Magnitude > 1200 then
	          if w() > 0 then
	            k = s[29]
	            CN(H + k(0, 3, 3), F)
	          end
	        end

	        k = s[19]
	        I = k()

	        if I then
	          if (I.Position - H).Magnitude > 40 then
	            local I, yN = s[15], s[29]
	            if not (I.stepTo(H + yN(0, 0, 3), F)) then
	              return
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        k = s[8]
	        local I, yN = k(), 0

	        while true do
	          local AN, TN = (I and F()) and (yN < 3)

	          if AN then
	            k = s[19]
	            TN = (k())
	          else
	            TN = AN
	          end

	          AN = nil

	          if TN then
	            local F = s[19]
	            AN = (F().Position - H).Magnitude > 6
	          else
	            AN = TN
	          end

	          if not AN then
	            break
	          end
	          I:MoveTo(H)
	          yN += task.wait(0.2)
	        end

	        task.wait(0.5)
	        H = s[9]
	        H("RF/WispCompanion/Request", C[2])
	        C = s[15]
	        C.wispGo = nil
	      end
	    end)

	    P("33_hollow#1", function()
	      local C, F = {drop = 0, n = 0}, s[15]
	      F.HL = C
	      local F = game:GetService("CollectionService")

	      local function H()
	        return workspace:FindFirstChild("TheHollowTrack")
	      end

	      local function k()
	        local I = s[1]
	        return I:GetAttribute("RaceId") == "TheHollowTrack"
	      end

	      local function I()
	        local yN, AN = H(), s[1]
	        local TN = AN:GetAttribute("RaceHeat")
	        return ((yN and TN) and yN:FindFirstChild("RaceHeats")) and yN.RaceHeats:FindFirstChild(TN)
	      end

	      local function yN()
	        local AN = I()
	        return AN and (AN:GetAttribute("RacePhase"))
	      end

	      local function AN()
	        local TN = s[1]
	        return TN:GetAttribute("RaceFinishPlace") ~= nil
	      end

	      local function TN()
	        local aN = s[64]
	        local qN = aN()
	        return ((qN and (type(qN.TheHollow) == "table")) and tonumber(qN.TheHollow.Wins)) or 0
	      end

	      local function aN()
	        local qN, PN, PN = math.max, s[28], (tonumber(s[28].hlSpeed) or 300) - C.drop
	        return qN(PN, 140)
	      end

	      C.inRace = k
	      C.wins = TN
	      C.phase = yN
	      local qN = n({name = "Hollow", mover = true, gap = 1, pri = 3})
	      C.job = qN

	      qN.on = function()
	        local PN = s[28]
	        return (PN.hollow == true) and (k() or (TN() < 3))
	      end

	      qN.want = function()
	        return (k() and "race") or "enter"
	      end

	      local function PN()
	        p("RE/MountRace/AskLeave")
	        local mN = os.clock() + 8

	        while k() and (os.clock() < mN) do
	          task.wait(0.2)
	        end
	      end

	      local function mN(BN)
	        local bN = H()

	        local H, gN = pcall(function()
	          return require(q.Shared.Modules.RaceTrackPath).new(bN.AttachmentTrack)
	        end)

	        local oN = s[19]
	        local pN = oN()

	        if H then
	          if gN then
	            if not (pN) then
	              return false
	            end
	          else
	            return false
	          end
	        else
	          return false
	        end

	        oN = gN.Length / 2
	        local NN = gN:Project(pN.Position, oN, oN, oN)
	        oN = gN:FrameAt(NN)
	        local fN, hN = math.max((pN.Position - oN.Position):Dot(oN.UpVector), 2), s[37]
	        local FN, MN, uN, EN = hN:FindFirstChild("RE/MountRace/PowerUp"), 0, 0
	        H = {}

	        local function XN()
	          local QN, ZN = gN:FrameAt(NN), s[19]
	          local rN = ZN()
	          if not (rN) then
	            return
	          end

	          if os.clock() >= uN then
	            uN = os.clock() + 1

	            if (rN.Position - (QN.Position + (QN.UpVector * fN))).Magnitude > 40 then
	              NN = gN:Project(rN.Position, NN, 300, 300)
	              QN = gN:FrameAt(NN)

	              if EN then
	                if (os.clock() - EN) < 6 then
	                  if aN() > 140 then
	                    C.drop = C.drop + 20
	                    ZN = s[11]
	                    ZN("Pulled back, racing at " .. aN(), "warn")
	                  end
	                end
	              end

	              EN = os.clock()
	            else
	            end
	          else
	          end

	          rN.CFrame = QN + (QN.UpVector * fN)
	          rN.AssemblyLinearVelocity = QN.LookVector * aN()
	          ZN = s[42]
	          rN.AssemblyAngularVelocity = ZN
	        end

	        local function fN()
	          local uN, EN = workspace:GetServerTimeNow(), s[19]
	          local QN = EN()

	          if FN then
	            if not (QN) then
	              return
	            end
	          else
	            return
	          end

	          EN = s[1]
	          local ZN = EN:GetAttribute("RacePowerUp")

	          if type(ZN) == "string" then
	            if ZN ~= "" then
	              local ZN, ZN = s[1], tonumber(s[1]:GetAttribute("RacePowerUpRollEndsAt")) or 0

	              if ZN <= uN then
	                local ZN, ZN = s[1], tonumber(s[1]:GetAttribute("RacePowerUpEffectEndsAt")) or 0

	                if ZN <= uN then
	                  local ZN, ZN = s[1], tonumber(s[1]:GetAttribute("RacePowerUpStunEndsAt")) or 0

	                  if ZN <= uN then
	                    FN:FireServer("Use", QN.Position, gN:FrameAt(NN).LookVector)
	                  end
	                end
	              end

	              return
	            end
	          end

	          for ZN, rN in ipairs(F:GetTagged("RacePowerUpSpawn")) do
	            if rN:IsDescendantOf(bN) then
	              EN, ZN = (rN:FindFirstChild("Pad") or rN.PrimaryPart) or rN:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart"), rN:GetAttribute("RaceBoxReadyAt")

	              if EN and ((type(ZN) ~= "number") or (ZN <= uN)) then
	                if (EN.Position - QN.Position).Magnitude <= 30 then
	                  FN:FireServer("Pickup", rN)
	                  break
	                end
	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        H[1] = d:Connect(function(F)
	          if k() then
	            if AN() then
	              return
	            end
	          else
	            return
	          end

	          local bN = NN
	          NN = bN + (aN() * F)
	          pcall(XN)
	          bN = s[28]

	          if bN.hlPower ~= false then
	            if os.clock() >= MN then
	              MN = os.clock() + 0.2
	              pcall(fN)
	            end
	          end
	        end)

	        oN = s[59]

	        H[2] = oN.PreSimulation:Connect(function()
	          if k() then
	            if not (AN()) then
	              pcall(XN)
	            end
	          end
	        end)

	        hN = s[1]
	        pN, oN = (hN:GetAttribute("RaceLap")), os.clock()

	        while (BN() and (k())) and not AN() do
	          hN = s[1]
	          local F = hN:GetAttribute("RaceLap")

	          if F ~= pN then
	            pN, oN = F, os.clock()
	          else
	            if (os.clock() - oN) > (((gN.Length / aN()) * 1.4) + 6) then
	              oN = os.clock()

	              if aN() > 140 then
	                C.drop = C.drop + 20
	              end

	              local BN = s[19]
	              local bN = BN()

	              if bN then
	                NN = gN:Project(bN.Position, NN, 600, 600)
	              else
	              end

	              bN = s[11]
	              bN("Lap did not count, racing at " .. aN(), "warn")
	            else
	            end
	          end

	          F = nil
	          F, C.say = s[1], ("Racing lap %s, %d studs/s"):format(tostring(s[1]:GetAttribute("RaceLap") or 1), aN())
	          F = s[11]
	          F(C.say)
	          task.wait(0.25)
	        end

	        for F, F in ipairs(H) do
	          F:Disconnect()
	        end

	        return AN()
	      end

	      qN.run = function(F, H)
	        F = os.clock()

	        if not (k()) then
	          local aN = s[11]
	          aN("Joining The Hollow race")
	          aN = s[9]
	          local BN, bN, gN = aN("RF/TheHollow/AskEnter")

	          if if BN then bN == false else not BN then
	            aN = s[11]
	            aN("The Hollow: " .. tostring(gN or "could not join"), "warn")
	            qN.at = os.clock() + 15
	            return
	          end

	          aN = os.clock() + 6

	          while true do
	            BN = k()
	            if not (if BN then not BN else os.clock() < aN) then
	              break
	            end
	            task.wait(0.1)
	          end

	          if not (k()) then
	            qN.at = os.clock() + 10
	            return
	          end
	        end

	        while ((H() and (k())) and (yN() ~= "Racing")) and not AN() do
	          if yN() == "Joining" then
	            local yN = s[15]

	            if yN.better(qN) then
	              local yN = s[11]
	              yN("A higher task goes first, leaving The Hollow")
	              return PN()
	            end
	          end

	          local yN = I()
	          local I = yN and (tonumber(yN:GetAttribute("RaceGoAt")) or tonumber(yN:GetAttribute("RaceJoinEndsAt")))
	          C.say = "Waiting at the start" .. ((I and (", go in %ds"):format(math.max(math.ceil(I - workspace:GetServerTimeNow()), 0))) or "")
	          yN = s[11]
	          yN(C.say)
	          task.wait(0.25)
	        end

	        if H() then
	          if not (k()) then
	            return
	          end
	        else
	          return
	        end

	        local k = AN()
	        local I = if k then not k else mN(H)

	        if AN() then
	          k = s[1]
	          local yN, AN, AN = k:GetAttribute("RaceFinishPlace"), s[1], tonumber(s[1]:GetAttribute("RaceFinishTime"))
	          C.n = C.n + 1
	          task.wait(1.5)
	          PN()
	          C.time, C.cycle = AN, os.clock() - F
	          C.last = ("Finished #%s in %s, round trip %ds"):format(tostring(yN), (AN and ("%.1fs"):format(AN)) or "?", math.floor(C.cycle))
	          H = s[11]
	          H("The Hollow: " .. (C.last .. (", wins " .. TN())), "ok")
	          return true
	        end

	        return I
	      end
	    end)

	    P("33_hollow#2", function()
	      local C = lN["The Hollow"]
	      local F = R("hlSt", C:Text("The Hollow Status", "Off"))

	      x(C, "Auto Race The Hollow", "hollow", false, function(H)
	        if not (H) then
	          local H, k = s[4], s[15]

	          if H == k.HL.job then
	            U(s[4])
	          end
	        end
	      end)

	      local H = s[28]
	      H.hlSpeed = 300

	      C:Slider("Hollow Race Speed", 140, 400, 300, function(H)
	        local k, I = s[28], s[15]
	        k.hlSpeed, I.HL.drop = math.clamp(tonumber(H) or 300, 140, 400), 0
	      end, " st/s", 1).Quick = false

	      x(C, "Use Race Power-Ups", "hlPower", true)
	      local C = n({name = "HollowInfo", gap = 1, info = true})

	      C.on = function()
	        return ON("Event")
	      end

	      C.fn = function()
	        local C = s[15]
	        local H = C.HL
	        local k, I = H.inRace() and (H.say or "In race")

	        if k then
	          I = k
	        else
	          C = s[28]
	          I = C.hollow and ((((H.wins() >= 3) and "Done, 3 wins") or H.last) or "Waiting for its turn")
	        end

	        F((I or "Off") .. (" | Wins: " .. (H.wins() .. "/3")))
	      end
	    end)

	    P("34_star#1", function()
	      local C, F = {pay = nil, caught = 0, slipped = 0, askAt = 0, jumped = {}}, s[15]
	      F.SS = C
	      local H = n({name = "Star", mover = true, gap = 0.5, pri = 2})
	      F = lN["Shooting Star"]
	      local k = F:Text("Shooting Star Status", "Off")

	      local I = {Set = function(yN, yN, AN)
	        if yN == "Status" then
	          k:Set(AN)
	        end
	      end}

	      x(F, "Auto Catch Shooting Star", "star", false, function(F)
	        if not (F) then
	          U(H)
	        end
	      end)

	      local function F(k)
	        if type(k) == "table" then
	          if k.Id ~= nil then
	            if C.pay then
	              if not (C.pay.Id == k.Id) then
	                C.slipped = 0
	              end
	            else
	              C.slipped = 0
	            end

	            C.pay = k
	          end
	        end
	      end

	      local function k()
	        C.askAt = os.clock() + 15

	        task.spawn(function()
	          local yN = s[9]
	          local AN, TN = yN("RF/ShootingStar/AskState")

	          if AN then
	            F(TN)
	          end
	        end)
	      end

	      local function yN(AN, TN)
	        local aN = s[37]
	        local qN = aN:FindFirstChild(AN)

	        if qN then
	          if qN:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
	            b(qN.OnClientEvent, TN)
	          end
	        end
	      end

	      yN("RE/ShootingStar/Fall", F)

	      yN("RE/ShootingStar/Wander", function(F, AN)
	        if C.pay then
	          if C.pay.Id == F then
	            if type(AN) == "table" then
	              C.pay.Leg = AN
	            end
	          end
	        end
	      end)

	      yN("RE/ShootingStar/Slipped", function(F)
	        if C.pay then
	          if C.pay.Id == F then
	            C.slipped = C.slipped + 1
	            C.hold = nil
	          end
	        end
	      end)

	      yN("RE/ShootingStar/Claimed", function(F)
	        if C.pay then
	          if C.pay.Id == F then
	            C.caught = C.caught + 1
	            C.pay = nil
	            U(H)
	            z("Shooting Star", "Caught a star", "ok")
	          end
	        end
	      end)

	      yN("RE/ShootingStar/Expired", function(F)
	        if C.pay then
	          if C.pay.Id == F then
	            C.pay = nil
	            U(H)
	          end
	        end
	      end)

	      k()

	      local function F(yN)
	        local AN = workspace:FindFirstChild("ShootingStarClient")
	        local TN = AN and (AN:FindFirstChild("Buddy"))
	        AN = TN and (TN:FindFirstChild("RootPart"))

	        if AN then
	          if AN:IsA("BasePart") then
	            return AN.Position
	          end
	        end

	        AN, TN = f(), yN.Leg

	        if type(TN) == "table" then
	          if typeof(TN.From) == "Vector3" then
	            if typeof(TN.To) == "Vector3" then
	              local aN = tonumber(TN.StartAt) or AN
	              local qN = (tonumber(TN.ArriveAt) or aN) - aN
	              local PN, mN = ((qN > 0) and math.clamp((AN - aN) / qN, 0, 1)) or 1, TN.To - TN.From
	              local aN, BN, bN = TN.From:Lerp(TN.To, PN), (PN < 1) and (mN.Magnitude > 0.5)

	              if BN then
	                qN = s[29]
	                bN = qN(mN.X, 0, mN.Z).Unit
	              else
	                bN = BN
	              end

	              return aN, bN or nil
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        AN = yN.Landing
	        if typeof(AN) == "CFrame" then
	          return AN.Position
	        end
	        return ((typeof(AN) == "Vector3") and AN) or nil
	      end

	      local function yN(AN)
	        local TN = f()
	        return (AN and (TN >= (tonumber(AN.LandAt) or TN))) and ((tonumber(AN.ExpiresAt) or (1 / 0)) > TN)
	      end

	      local AN = s[15]

	      AN.starWants = function()
	        local AN = s[28]
	        return AN.star and yN(C.pay)
	      end

	      H.on = function()
	        if os.clock() >= C.askAt then
	          k()
	        end

	        if C.pay then
	          if (tonumber(C.pay.ExpiresAt) or (1 / 0)) <= f() then
	            C.pay = nil
	          end
	        end

	        local AN = s[28]
	        return AN.star and yN(C.pay)
	      end

	      H.want = function()
	        local yN = s[25]
	        if yN() then
	          return
	        end
	        return C.pay
	      end

	      H.run = function(yN, AN)
	        local TN = s[1]
	        local aN = TN.Character

	        local function qN()
	          local PN, mN = AN() and (C.pay == yN)

	          if PN then
	            local AN = s[1]
	            mN = AN.Character == aN
	          else
	            mN = PN
	          end

	          return mN
	        end

	        if s[13](qN) then
	          TN = s[14]
	          if not (TN(qN)) then
	            return
	          end
	        else
	          return
	        end

	        TN = s[19]
	        local AN = TN()
	        TN = nil

	        if AN then
	          local aN = s[21]
	          TN = aN(AN.Position)
	        else
	          TN = AN
	        end

	        if TN then
	          local aN = s[22]
	          if not (aN(qN, "Shooting Star")) then
	            return
	          end
	        end

	        local function aN()
	          local PN = F(yN)
	          local mN

	          if PN then
	            local BN = s[29]
	            mN = PN + BN(0, 3, 3)
	          else
	            mN = PN
	          end

	          return mN
	        end

	        local function PN()
	          local mN, BN = aN(), s[19]
	          local bN = BN()
	          return ((mN and bN) and (bN.Position - mN).Magnitude) or (1 / 0)
	        end

	        if PN() > 40 then
	          if not (C.jumped[yN.Id]) then
	            C.jumped[yN.Id] = true
	            TN = s[11]
	            TN("Ragdoll jump to the star")
	            CN(aN, qN)
	          end
	        end

	        if qN() then

	          else

	                aN = qN
	              else

	                aN = AN.stealWants()
	              end
	            else
	              aN = TN
	            end
	          end
	        else
	          aN = I
	        end

	        yN = nil

	        if aN then
	          I = s[15]
	          yN = (I.rank["Steal:filter"] or 9) < k
	        else
	          yN = aN
	        end

	        return yN
	      end

	            aN = k and (k.Position - BN.Position).Magnitude

	              if aN < oN then
	                oN, gN = aN, k
	              else
	              end
	            else
	            end
	          end

	          gN = if k.ciwT == "Walls and crystal" then nil else gN

	            local aN, NN = PN("Bat"), PN("PumpkinLauncher")

	              k = s[11]

	              AN(gN.Position)

	            elseif NN then
	              if pN >= (C.cd.PumpkinLauncher or 0) then
	                local k, aN = (type(bN.Barriers) == "table") and bN.Barriers[F], (type(bN.Crystals) == "table") and bN.Crystals[F]
	                local F = ((gN and (gN.Position + (gN.AssemblyLinearVelocity * (oN / 110)))) or (((k and ((tonumber(k.Health) or 0) > 0)) and (typeof(k.Model) == "Instance")) and I(k.Model:FindFirstChild("Hitbox") or k.Model))) or (((aN and ((tonumber(aN.Health) or 0) > 0)) and (typeof(aN.Model) == "Instance")) and I(aN.Model:FindFirstChild("Pivot", true) or aN.Model))

	    P("39_ufo#1", function()

	      local function w(TN, aN, qN, PN)
	        local mN = s[1]

	            CN(TN + mN(0, 3, 0), bN)
	          end

	      local function CN(TN, aN)
	        local qN = os.clock() + TN

	        while os.clock() < qN do
	          if not (aN()) then
	            return false
	          end
	          TN = s[20]
	          TN()
	        end

	      end

	      local function TN(aN, qN)
	        local PN = os.clock() + 1.2

	        while (os.clock() < PN) and (qN()) do
	          local PN, mN, BN = s[17], s[18], s[29]
	          PN(mN(aN + BN(0, 1, 2)))
	          BN = s[20]
	          BN()
	        end

	        return qN()
	      end

	      local aN, qN = {Straight = 5, Elbow = 3, Cross = 15}, {{1, -1, 0, 4}, {2, 0, 1, 8}, {4, 1, 0, 1}, {8, 0, -1, 2}}

	      local function PN(mN, BN)
	        for bN = 1, BN, 1 do
	          mN = bit32.band(bit32.bor(bit32.lshift(mN, 1), bit32.rshift(mN, 3)), 15)
	        end

	        return mN
	      end

	      local function mN(BN)
	        local bN, gN, oN = require(q.Shared.Modules.UFOSightingsPipePuzzle).Generate(BN), {}, {}

	        for BN = 1, 5, 1 do
	          for pN = 1, 5, 1 do
	            oN[((BN - 1) * 5) + pN] = bN[BN][pN].Rotation
	          end
	        end

	        local function BN(pN, NN, fN)
	          local hN = ((pN - 1) * 5) + NN
	          gN[hN] = true

	          for FN = 0, 3, 1 do
	            local MN = PN(aN[bN[pN][NN].Piece], FN)

	            if bit32.band(MN, fN) ~= 0 then
	              oN[hN] = FN

	              if pN == 4 then
	                if NN == 5 then
	                  if bit32.band(MN, 2) ~= 0 then
	                    return true
	                  end
	                end
	              end

	                              end
	                            end
	                          end
	                        end
	                      end
	                    end
	                  end
	                end
	              end
	            end
	          end

	          gN[hN] = nil
	          return false
	        end

	        return (BN(2, 1, 8) and oN) or nil
	      end

	      local aN = {repair = function(qN)

	          local F = s[20]
	          I += F()
	        end

	        if (C.st.Delivered or 0) > w then
	          C.eggs = C.eggs + 1

	            return

	          end

	      end

	          end

	            end

	            return (GN and ("Chasing " .. GN.DisplayName)) or "Chasing"
	          end

	        end

	        local function GN(LN, vN)

	        end

	        end

	          if not (pN) then
	            return
	          end
	          fN[gN] = nil
	          rN(pN.cs)
	          pN.hl:Destroy()
	          pN.b:Destroy()
	        end

	        local function gN(pN, hN)
	          if fN[hN] then
	            return
	          end
	          local LN = hN:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	          LN = (((LN and LN:IsA("BasePart")) and LN) or hN.PrimaryPart) or hN:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
	          if not (LN) then
	            return
	          end
	          DN = C(DN)
	          local vN, kN, _N, wN = ZN(hN, DN), pcall(hN.GetBoundingBox, hN)
	          local WN, IN, zN = if kN then if typeof(_N) == "CFrame" then ((_N.Position.Y + (wN.Y * 0.5)) - LN.Position.Y) + 1 else 6 else 6, MN(DN)
	          kN = s[29]
	          IN.Adornee, IN.StudsOffsetWorldSpace = LN, kN(0, WN, 0)
	          wN, kN = XN(zN, PN, 1, 0.45), XN(zN, PN, 2, 0.55)
	          QN(wN, tostring(pN) .. " Guard", NN)
	          local PN = {hl = vN, b = IN, sr = kN, cs = {}}
	          fN[hN] = PN
	          Q(PN)
	          GN(PN, hN)

	          local function pN()

	        end

	        end

	      local function Q(L)

	        local I = s[28]
	        local yN, AN = I.invis

	          I = nil
	          I, AN = s[15], os.clock() >= s[15].keepVis
	        else
	          AN = yN
	        end

	        I = nil

	        if AN then
	          yN = nil
	          yN, I = s[15], (os.clock() - (s[15].flipAt or -99)) > 20
	        else
	          I = AN
	        end

	          AN = s[15]

	          if CN then
	            local AN = s[15]
	            TN = AN.lock()
	          else
	            TN = CN
	          end

	          yN = not TN
	        else
	          yN = I
	        end

	        return yN and not L()
	      end

	        if F() == I then
	          return true
	        end

	          table.remove(A, 1)

	        if I then
	          local CN = s[28]
	          AN = not CN.invis
	        else
	          AN = I
	        end

	          return false
	        else

	          if CN() then
	            return false
	          elseif #A >= 6 then
	            return false
	          end

	          end

	          end
	          task.wait(0.1)
	        end

	      end

	              end
	            elseif C.Anchored then
	              if Q then
	                Q.AutoRotate = true
	              end
	            elseif Q.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.1 then

	              C.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(C.Position, C.Position + Q.MoveDirection)

	        tN = math.abs(R(tN) or 0)
	        local DN = 1

	        while (tN >= 1000) and (DN < #rN) do
	          tN, DN = tN / 1000, DN + 1
	        end

	        return ((DN == 1) and I(w(tN + 0.5))) or ((((tN >= 100) and "%.0f%s") or ((tN >= 10) and "%.1f%s")) or "%.2f%s"):format(tN, rN[DN])
	      end

	      local function rN(tN)
	        return "$" .. (KN(tN) .. "/s")
	      end

	      local function tN(DN)
	        if type(DN) ~= "string" then
	          return
	        end
	        local GN, LN = DN:gsub("<[^>]->", ""):gsub("[%$,%s]", ""):match("^([%d%.]+)(%a*)")
	        GN = R(GN)
	        DN = GN and HN[LN:lower()]
	        return DN and (GN * DN)
	      end

	        DN = math.max(0, w(DN))

	                return wN.Text
	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end
	      end

	      local function GN(LN)
	        local kN = s[1]
	        local _N = kN:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
	        kN = _N and (_N:FindFirstChild(LN))
	        return kN and R(kN.Value)
	      end

	      local function LN(kN)
	        local _N = kN and kN.Rarity
	        if not (QN(_N)) then
	          return "", 0
	        end
	        return I((_N.DisplayName or _N._id) or ""), R(_N.Rank or _N.RarityNumber) or 0, _N
	      end

	      local function kN(_N, wN, WN)
	        local IN, zN = t(_N, wN, (K({Mutations = WN}))), r(_N)
	        return (((IN > 0) and IN) or (zN and R(zN.EarningRate))) or 0
	      end

	      local K, t, _N = W:State("Farm HUD Stolen Eggs", {}), {}, 0
	      local wN = -1
	      local WN = k()
	      local IN, zN, JN, RN, YN, SN = GN("Speed"), 0

	      local function xN(jN)
	        if not (QN(jN)) then
	          return
	        end
	        table.clear(t)
	        local cN
	        cN, SN = R(jN.Count) or 0, true
	        _N = cN

	        if QN(jN.History) then
	          for cN, cN in ipairs(jN.History) do
	            if QN(cN) then
	              if #t < 20 then
	                table.insert(t, cN)
	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        if QN(jN.Best) then
	          local cN, VN, nN = jN.Best, jN.Best
	          nN, YN = R(VN.Rank) or -1, cN
	          wN = nN
	        end
	      end

	      xN(K:Get())

	      table.insert(K.ls, function(jN)
	        if QN(jN) then
	          if jN.History ~= t then
	            xN(jN)
	          end
	        end
	      end)

	      local xN = s[33]
	      local jN, cN = xN.Egg, s[37]
	      xN = cN:FindFirstChild("RE/EggWorld/FieldEggRedeemVerdict")

	      if jN then
	        if jN.CarryChanged then
	          pcall(b, jN.CarryChanged, function(jN)
	            if QN(jN) then
	              if jN.IsCarrying ~= true then
	                return
	              elseif type(jN.Uid) ~= "string" then
	                return
	              end
	            else
	              return
	            end

	            local cN = jN.Uid

	            task.spawn(function()
	              local VN = s[23]
	              local nN = VN()
	              VN = nN[cN]

	              if QN(VN) then
	              else
	                VN = nil

	                for UN, UN in next, nN, nil do
	                  if QN(UN) then
	                    if UN.Uid == cN then
	                      VN = UN
	                      break
	                    end
	                  end
	                end
	              end

	              RN = {Cat = I((jN.AssetCategory or (VN and VN.AssetCategory)) or ""), Scale = R((VN and VN.AssetScale) or jN.AssetScale), Mut = ((VN and QN(VN.Mutations)) and VN.Mutations) or ZN(jN.Mutations)}
	            end)
	          end)
	        end
	      end

	      if xN then
	        b(xN.OnClientEvent, function(xN)
	          zN += 1
	          _N += 1

	          if QN(xN) then
	            local jN, cN, VN, nN = I(xN.AssetCategory or ""), I((xN.DisplayName or xN.AssetCategory) or "Egg"), LN(r(xN.AssetCategory))
	            VN = ((VN ~= "") and VN) or I(xN.Rarity or "")

	            if nN >= wN then
	              local xN
	              xN, wN = {Name = cN, Rarity = VN, Category = jN, Rank = nN}, nN
	              YN = xN
	            end

	            local wN, xN, UN = RN
	            RN = nil

	            if wN then
	              if wN.Cat == jN then
	                if wN.Scale then
	                  local RN, v2 = wN.Scale, kN(jN, wN.Scale, wN.Mut)
	                  xN, UN = RN, v2
	                else
	                end
	              else
	              end
	            else
	            end

	            nN, wN = table.insert, {Name = cN, Category = jN, Rarity = VN, Scale = xN, Value = UN}
	            wN.At = os.time()
	            nN(t, 1, wN)

	            while #t > 20 do
	              table.remove(t)
	            end
	          end

	          SN = true
	          K:Set({Count = _N, Best = YN, History = t})
	        end)
	      end

	      local function K(wN)
	        return wN / math.max((k() - WN) / 3600, 0.016666666666666666)
	      end

	      local function wN(RN)
	        return ((RN > 0) and ("+" .. (KN(K(RN)) .. "/h"))) or ""
	      end

	      local function RN(xN, jN)
	        local cN = jN.Placement
	        if not (QN(cN)) then
	          return
	        end

	        if cN.ReadyAt ~= nil then
	          return "Ready"
	        end
	        local VN, nN

	        task.spawn(function()
	          VN, nN = pcall(function()
	            local UN = s[33]
	            local rec, A1 = UN.Rec, s[33]
	            UN = A1.Egg
	            local C1, T1 = UN and UN.ReadOwnedEgg

	            if C1 then
	              A1 = nil
	              A1, T1 = s[1], UN.ReadOwnedEgg(s[1].UserId, xN)
	            else
	              T1 = C1
	            end

	            local xN, UN, A1, C1 = ((QN(T1) and T1.Placement) and T1) or jN, (QN(T1) and R(T1.GrowthSpeedMultiplier)) or 1, f(), s[1]
	            return rec.WallSecondsRemaining(xN, A1, UN, rec.CurrentNightCredit(xN, A1, UN, C1))
	          end)
	        end)

	        if not VN or (type(nN) ~= "number") then
	          return
	        end
	        cN = ((nN <= 0) and "Ready") or M(nN)
	        return cN
	      end

	      local function M(xN)
	        local jN, cN = pcall(function()
	          local VN = s[33]
	          return VN.Rec.EggIcon(xN)
	        end)

	        if jN then
	          if type(cN) == "string" then
	            if cN ~= "" then
	              return cN
	            end

	        end

	        return (((jN and QN(jN.Egg)) and jN.Egg.Icon) or (jN and jN.Icon)) or ""
	      end

	      local xN, jN, cN, VN, nN = false, {At = 0, ["Top Pets"] = {}, ["Top Eggs"] = {}}, {}, {}, 0

	      local function UN(arg, A1, C1, T1, arg2, arg3)
	        local mutations = C1.Mutations
	        local n2, tbl = (QN(mutations) and #mutations) or 0, arg[A1]

	        if tbl then
	          if tbl.Rec == C1 then
	            if tbl.Cat == T1 then
	              if tbl.Scale == arg2 then
	                if tbl.Mut == mutations then
	                  if tbl.N == n2 then
	                    return tbl
	                  end
	                end
	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        local O1 = r(T1)
	        local P1, P1 = LN(O1)
	        tbl = {Uid = A1, Rec = C1, En = O1, Rank = P1, Cat = T1, Scale = arg2, Mut = mutations, N = n2, Size = R(arg2) or arg3, Rate = kN(T1, arg2, mutations)}
	        arg[A1] = tbl
	        return tbl
	      end

	      local function kN(arg, A1)
	        return (arg.Rate > A1.Rate) or ((arg.Rate == A1.Rate) and (arg.Uid < A1.Uid))
	      end

	      local function fn(A1, C1)
	        return (A1.Rank > C1.Rank) or ((A1.Rank == C1.Rank) and ((A1.Size > C1.Size) or ((A1.Size == C1.Size) and (A1.Uid < C1.Uid))))
	      end

	      local function A1(C1, T1, arg)
	        local n2 = #C1

	        if n2 >= 6 then
	          if not (arg(T1, C1[n2])) then
	            return
	          end
	        end

	        local n3 = n2 + 1

	        while (n3 > 1) and arg(T1, C1[n3 - 1]) do
	          n3 -= 1
	        end

	        table.insert(C1, n3, T1)

	        if #C1 > 6 then
	          C1[#C1] = nil
	        end
	      end

	      local function C1()
	        if (k() - jN.At) < 3 then
	          return jN
	        end
	        jN.At = k()
	        local T1 = s[64]
	        local v2 = T1()
	        if not v2 then
	          return jN
	        end

	        if k() >= nN then
	          nN = k() + 300
	          table.clear(cN)
	          table.clear(VN)
	        end

	        local nN, tbl, v3, v4, v5 = {}, {}, next, ZN(v2.EquippedAssets)
	        T1 = 0

	        for O1 in v3, v4, v5 do
	          T1 += 1
	        end

	        v5, v3, v4 = next, ZN(v2.Inventory)
	        local O1 = 0

	        for P1, v6 in v5, v3, v4 do
	          if QN(v6) then
	            O1 += 1
	            A1(nN, UN(cN, P1, v6, v6.Category, v6.Scale, 1), kN)
	          else
	          end
	        end

	        v3, v5, v4 = next, ZN(v2.EggInventory)
	        local ZN, kN = 0, 0

	        for cN, P1 in v3, v5, v4 do
	          if QN(P1) then
	            ZN += 1
	            kN = if P1.Placement then kN + 1 else kN
	            A1(tbl, UN(VN, cN, P1, P1.AssetCategory, P1.AssetScale, 0), fn)
	          else
	          end
	        end

	        jN.Pets = O1
	        jN.Placed = T1
	        jN.Eggs = ZN
	        jN.Grow = kN
	        jN["Top Pets"] = nN
	        jN["Top Eggs"] = tbl
	        jN.Tread = nil

	        pcall(function()
	          local ZN = s[33]
	          local kN, cN = ZN.Tread, R(v2.TreadmillUpgradeLevel) or 0
	          ZN = kN.GetByUpgradeLevel(cN)
	          if not (QN(ZN)) then
	            return
	          end
	          local VN, nN = pcall(kN.GetByUpgradeLevel, cN + 1)

	            return (I((cN.DisplayName or cN._id) or "Treadmill"):gsub("%s*Treadmill$", ""))
	          end

	          jN.Tread = {Name = kN(ZN), Mult = R(ZN.SpeedMultiplier) or 1, Icon = ZN.Icon and I(ZN.Icon), Note = ((VN and QN(nN)) and ("next " .. (kN(nN) .. (" $" .. KN(R(nN.Price) or 0))))) or "max treadmill"}
	        end)

	        return jN
	      end

	      local ZN, kN, jN = 0

	      local function cN()
	        if not (((kN and kN.Parent) and jN) and jN.Parent) then
	          kN, jN = nil, nil

	          if k() >= ZN then
	            ZN = k() + 2
	            local ZN = DN()
	            kN = ZN and ZN:FindFirstChild("ReadyNotification", true)
	            jN = kN and kN:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
	          end
	        end

	        return ZN
	      end

	      local function ZN()
	        local DN = s[15]
	        local kN, jN, VN, nN = DN.doing or "Idle", s[4] and s[4].name, s[4]

	        if VN then
	          DN = s[15]
	          nN = DN.status
	        else
	          nN = VN
	        end

	        DN = nil

	        if nN then
	          VN = s[15]
	          DN = VN.status ~= ""
	        else
	          DN = nN
	        end

	        VN = nil

	        if DN then
	          nN = s[15]
	          VN = nN.status
	        else
	          VN = DN
	        end

	        local UN, flag, A1 = VN or kN, jN == "Steal"

	        if flag then
	          A1 = flag
	        else
	          DN = s[25]
	          A1 = (DN())
	        end

	        if A1 then
	          VN = s[25]
	          return UN, (VN() and "Stealing  |  carrying") or "Stealing", G.egg
	        end

	          return UN, "Placing Eggs", MN
	        end

	          return UN, "Auto Mutate", "rbxassetid://103838374075457"
	        end

	        if jN == "Combat" then
	          return "Fighting a player", "Combat", "rbxassetid://135238935953633"
	        end
	        A1 = s[15]
	        if A1.invisBusy then
	          return "Turning invisible", "Invisibility", G.night
	        end
	        A1, DN = jN == "Treadmill"

	        if A1 then
	          DN = A1
	        else
	          VN = nil
	          VN, DN = s[15], (k() - (s[15].treadAt or 0)) < 3
	        end

	        if DN then
	          A1 = GN("Speed")
	          nN = ((A1 and IN) and (A1 - IN)) or 0
	          return "Running on the treadmill", ((nN > 0) and ("Treadmill  |  +" .. (KN(K(nN)) .. " speed/h"))) or "Treadmill", FN
	        end

	        if jN then
	          return UN, kN, G.egg
	        end
	        return "Nothing running", "Idle", G.egg
	      end

	      local function K(DN, kN, jN, VN, nN, UN, arg, A1, T1, arg2)
	        return {Id = DN, Icon = kN, A = C(jN, VN, nN), B = C(UN, arg, A1), Read = T1, Wide = arg2}
	      end

	        return {Id = kN, Col = jN, A = C(VN, nN, UN), B = C(arg, A1, T1), Items = {...}}
	      end

	      local kN = {}

	      end))), DN("Stealing", "Left", 255, 0, 4, 255, 83, 86, K("Eggs Stolen", G.egg, 255, 150, 150, 255, 40, 60, function()

	      end), (K("Session Time", G.night, 255, 255, 255, 215, 215, 230, function()
	        return HN(k() - WN), #a:GetPlayers() .. " players"
	      end))), DN("Status", "Center", 255, 204, 0, 255, 150, 30, (K("Activity", G.egg, 255, 240, 170, 255, 170, 60, ZN, true))), DN("Events", "Right", 255, 212, 82, 255, 150, 40, (K("Shooting Star", "rbxassetid://118070800707365", 255, 250, 200, 255, 220, 90, function()
	        local FN = s[15]
	        local MN = FN.SS
	        local ZN, KN = (((MN.caught or 0) > 0) and (MN.caught .. " caught")) or "", MN.pay
	        if not (KN) then
	          return "None", ZN
	        end
	        FN = f()
	        MN = R(KN.LandAt) or FN
	        if FN < MN then
	          return "Falling", HN(MN - FN) .. " to land"
	        end
	        MN = ((s[4] and (s[4].name == "Star")) and "catching") or ZN
	        return "Landed", MN
	      end)))

	      y:lo(kN, 0, jN, VN, nN, UN, Events, (DN("Collection", "Right", 255, 116, 16, 255, 185, 87, K("Pets", G.pet, 255, 225, 170, 255, 150, 60, function()
	        local f = C1()
	        return I(f.Pets or 0), (f.Placed or 0) .. " placed"
	      end), (K("Eggs", G.egg, 255, 200, 200, 255, 90, 100, function()

	        local K, G = I(f.Eggs or 0), cN()
	        return K, ("%d ready  %d growing"):format(G, f.Grow or 0)

	      y:lo(f, 0, DN("Top Pets", "Center", 255, 116, 16, 255, 185, 87), (DN("Top Eggs", "Center", 255, 0, 4, 255, 83, 86)))
	      f[2].Egg = true

	      local function K(G)
	        return W:State("Farm HUD " .. (G .. " Position"), {})
	      end

	      local G = {}

	      for FN, FN in ipairs(kN) do

	        end

	        G[#G + 1] = FN.Id
	      end

	      G[#G + 1] = "Steal History"
	      K("Steal History")
	      local FN, MN, ZN = _(G), false, true
	      local HN = (((H.TouchEnabled and not H.KeyboardEnabled) and not H.MouseEnabled) and 70) or 90
	      local KN, tN, GN, vN, _N, wN, WN, IN, zN, JN = HN / 100, 4, {}, {}, {}, {}, {}, {}

	      local function YN(jN, cN)
	        GN[#GN + 1] = jN:Connect(cN)
	      end

	      local function jN(cN, VN)
	        if cN.On ~= VN then
	          cN.On = VN
	          cN.F.Visible = VN
	        end
	      end

	      local function cN(VN, nN, UN)
	        UN.Parent = nN
	        UN.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	        return o(VN, UN)
	      end

	      local function VN(nN, UN, backgroundTransparency, A1)
	        A1.Parent = nN
	        A1.BackgroundColor3 = UN
	        A1.BackgroundTransparency = backgroundTransparency
	        A1.BorderSizePixel = 0
	        return o("Frame", A1)
	      end

	      local function nN(UN, arg)
	        o("UICorner", {Parent = UN, CornerRadius = Q(0, arg)})
	      end

	      local function UN(arg, A1, T1, arg2, arg3)
	        return o("UIStroke", {Parent = arg, Color = A1, Thickness = T1, ApplyStrokeMode = arg2, Transparency = arg3})
	      end

	      local function fn2(A1, T1, arg)
	        cN("ImageLabel", A1, {Image = "rbxassetid://90325592797235", ImageTransparency = T1, ScaleType = A.ScaleType.Tile, TileSize = h(arg, arg), Size = p(1, 1)})
	      end

	      local function A1(T1, arg)
	        o("UIListLayout", {Parent = T1, SortOrder = bN, Padding = Q(0, arg)})
	      end

	      local function T1(parent, arg, rotation)
	        local rarityGradient = arg and arg.RarityGradient

	        if typeof(rarityGradient) == "Instance" then
	          if rarityGradient:IsA("UIGradient") then
	            local clone = rarityGradient:Clone()
	            clone.Name, clone.Rotation, clone.Offset = m:GenerateGUID(false), rotation or clone.Rotation, Vector2.zero
	            clone.Parent = parent
	            return clone
	          end
	        end

	        rarityGradient = ((arg and (typeof(arg.Color) == "Color3")) and arg.Color) or C(200, 200, 210)
	        return XN(parent, rarityGradient:Lerp(S, 0.35), rarityGradient, rotation)
	      end

	      local function fn3(arg, arg2)
	        for i2, v2 in ipairs(arg.G) do
	          v2:Destroy()
	        end

	        arg.G = {T1(arg.Stroke, arg2, 45), T1(arg.Rarity, arg2)}

	        if arg.Glow then
	          arg.G[3] = T1(arg.Glow, arg2)
	        end
	      end

	      local function T1(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
	        local O1 = cN("TextLabel", arg, {TextColor3 = S, TextSize = arg2, TextXAlignment = aN, TextTruncate = A.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Text = "", FontFace = EN})

	        for aN, EN in next, arg3, nil do
	          O1[aN] = EN
	        end

	        o("UIStroke", {Parent = O1, Color = yN, Thickness = (arg4 and 1.8) or 2.4, LineJoinMode = A.LineJoinMode.Round})
	        return O1
	      end

	      local function aN()
	        local EN = workspace.CurrentCamera
	        local viewportSize = (EN and EN.ViewportSize) or F(1280, 720)
	        return ((viewportSize.Y < 1) and F(1280, 720)) or viewportSize
	      end

	      local function EN()
	        local v2 = aN()
	        local n2, v3 = math.clamp(math.min(v2.Y / 900, v2.X / 1500), 0.6, 1.7), KN
	        return n2 * v3
	      end

	      local function fn4(arg, arg2)
	        local absoluteSize = zN.AbsoluteSize

	        if absoluteSize.X <= 0 then
	          return
	        elseif absoluteSize.Y <= 0 then
	          return
	        end

	        local O1, P1, n2 = arg.AbsolutePosition - zN.AbsolutePosition, arg.AbsoluteSize, (arg2 and w(14 * EN())) or 0
	        local n3, n4 = (((O1.X < 0) and -O1.X) or (((O1.X + P1.X) > absoluteSize.X) and ((absoluteSize.X - P1.X) - O1.X))) or 0, ((((O1.Y - n2) < 0) and (n2 - O1.Y)) or (((O1.Y + P1.Y) > absoluteSize.Y) and ((absoluteSize.Y - P1.Y) - O1.Y))) or 0

	        if (n3 ~= 0) or (n4 ~= 0) then
	          arg.Position = arg.Position + h(w(n3), w(n4))
	        end
	      end

	      local function fn5(arg, arg2)
	        local O1, P1, v2, v3

	        local function fn6(B1, arg3)
	          local flag = O1 and ((P1 and (B1 == P1)) or (if P1 then not P1 else B1.UserInputType == arg3))
	          return flag
	        end

	        YN(cN("TextButton", arg, {Active = true, AutoButtonColor = false, Position = h(-12, -36), Size = q(1, 24, 1, 45), Text = "", ZIndex = 30}).InputBegan, function(B1)
	          if O1 then
	            return
	          elseif B1.UserInputState ~= A.UserInputState.Begin then
	            return
	          else
	            local v4 = s[15]
	            if not (v4.press(B1)) then
	              return
	            end
	          end

	          local flag, v4, position = ((B1.UserInputType == A.UserInputType.Touch) and B1) or nil, F(B1.Position.X, B1.Position.Y), arg.Position
	          O1, P1, v2, v3 = true, flag, v4, position
	        end)

	        YN(H.InputChanged, function(B1)
	          if fn6(B1, A.UserInputType.MouseMovement) then
	            local n2 = F(B1.Position.X, B1.Position.Y) - v2
	            arg.Position = q(v3.X.Scale, v3.X.Offset + n2.X, v3.Y.Scale, v3.Y.Offset + n2.Y)
	            fn4(arg, true)
	          end
	        end)

	        YN(H.InputEnded, function(H)
	          if fn6(H, A.UserInputType.MouseButton1) then
	            local H = arg.Position
	            O1, P1 = false, nil
	            K(arg2):Set({XScale = H.X.Scale, XOffset = H.X.Offset, YScale = H.Y.Scale, YOffset = H.Y.Offset, Unit = EN(), Top = 16})
	          end
	        end)
	      end

	      local function H(arg, arg2)
	        local O1, P1 = arg.Id, arg.Col
	        local v2 = VN(zN, C(24, 24, 34), 0.22, {Active = true, AnchorPoint = F((((P1 == "Left") and 1) or ((P1 == "Right") and 0)) or 0.5, 0), AutomaticSize = sN, Size = h(0, 0)})
	        nN(v2, 14)
	        UN(v2, C(82, 82, 107), 1.6, mN, 0.3)
	        o("UIPadding", {Parent = v2, PaddingTop = Q(0, 24), PaddingBottom = Q(0, 9), PaddingLeft = Q(0, 12), PaddingRight = Q(0, 12)})
	        vN[#vN + 1] = o("UIScale", {Parent = v2})
	        local tbl = VN(v2, S, nil, {AutomaticSize = TN, Position = q(0, -2, 0, -36), Size = h(0, 24)})
	        nN(tbl, 8)
	        UN(tbl, yN, 2, mN, 0.15)
	        XN(tbl, arg.A, arg.B, -90)
	        fn2(tbl, 0.55, 28)
	        o("UIPadding", {Parent = tbl, PaddingLeft = Q(0, 10), PaddingRight = Q(0, 10)})
	        T1(tbl, 15, {AutomaticSize = TN, Text = O1:upper(), Size = h(0, 24), TextTruncate = PN})
	        tbl = {Id = O1, F = v2, Col = P1, Order = arg2, Body = cN("Frame", v2, {AutomaticSize = sN, Size = h(0, 0)})}
	        _N[#_N + 1] = tbl
	        fn5(v2, O1)
	        return tbl
	      end

	      local sN = {}
	      sN.Value = {C(170, 255, 140), C(40, 230, 90)}
	      sN.Size = {C(255, 244, 200), C(235, 190, 95)}
	      sN.Time = {C(255, 200, 170), C(255, 120, 80)}

	      local function fn6(arg, arg2, O1, P1)
	        local v2 = VN(arg, nil, 1, {AutomaticSize = TN, LayoutOrder = O1, Size = h(0, P1 + 5), Visible = false})
	        arg = T1(v2, P1, {AutomaticSize = TN, Size = p(0, 1), TextXAlignment = qN, TextTruncate = PN})
	        XN(arg, sN[arg2][1], sN[arg2][2])
	        return {F = v2, Text = arg}
	      end

	      local function sN(arg, last)
	        last = (last and I(last)) or ""

	        if arg.Last ~= last then
	          arg.Last, arg.Text.Text, arg.F.Visible = last, last, last ~= ""
	        end
	      end

	      local function fn7(arg, O1, P1, arg2)
	        local Frame = cN("Frame", arg, {Position = q(0, (arg2 and 4) or 52, 0, O1), Size = q(1, (arg2 and -8) or -146, 0, P1)})
	        o("UIListLayout", {Parent = Frame, FillDirection = gN, HorizontalAlignment = (arg2 and A.HorizontalAlignment.Center) or A.HorizontalAlignment.Left, VerticalAlignment = A.VerticalAlignment.Center, SortOrder = bN, Padding = Q(0, 5)})
	        return Frame
	      end

	      local function fn8(O1, P1, arg, arg2, arg3)
	        local B1 = fn7(O1, P1, arg, arg3)
	        arg = T1(B1, 17, {AutomaticSize = TN, LayoutOrder = 1, Size = h(0, 21)})
	        o("UISizeConstraint", {Parent = arg, MaxSize = F(arg2, 21)})
	        return arg, fn6(B1, "Size", 2, 16)
	      end

	      local function O1(P1, arg)
	        local v2 = VN(P1, hN, 0.02, {LayoutOrder = arg, Size = h(180, 200), Visible = false})
	        nN(v2, 14)
	        P1, arg = UN(v2, S, 3, mN), VN(v2, S, 0.7, {Size = q(1, 0, 0, 136)})
	        nN(arg, 14)
	        fn2(arg, 0.82, 30)
	        local ImageLabel, B1 = cN("ImageLabel", v2, {AnchorPoint = F(0.5, 0), Position = q(0.5, 0, 0, 6), ScaleType = BN, Size = h(126, 126)}), VN(v2, hN, nil, {Position = h(7, 7), Size = h(32, 24)})
	        nN(B1, 7)
	        UN(B1, yN, 1.6)
	        local yN, hN, v3, v4 = T1(B1, 15, {Size = p(1, 1), TextXAlignment = qN}), T1(v2, 13, {AnchorPoint = F(1, 0), Position = q(1, -7, 0, 10), Size = q(1, -46, 0, 16), TextXAlignment = ON, TextColor3 = C(255, 230, 120)}), fn8(v2, 135, 20, 108, true)
	        local B1, v5 = T1(v2, 15, {Position = q(0, 6, 0, 155), Size = q(1, -12, 0, 16), TextXAlignment = qN}), fn7(v2, 172, 23, true)
	        return {F = v2, Stroke = P1, Glow = arg, Image = ImageLabel, Rank = yN, Mu = hN, Name = v3, Rarity = B1, SC = v4, VC = fn6(v5, "Value", 1, 18), HC = fn6(v5, "Time", 2, 16), G = {}}
	      end

	      local function yN(hN, P1)
	        local v2 = VN(hN, C(14, 14, 20), 0.25, {LayoutOrder = P1, Size = h(330, 52), Visible = false})
	        nN(v2, 10)
	        local P1, ImageLabel, v3, B1 = UN(v2, S, 2, mN), cN("ImageLabel", v2, {Position = h(4, 3), ScaleType = BN, Size = h(46, 46)}), fn8(v2, 3, 22, 122)
	        hN = fn7(v2, 27, 22)
	        local v4, value, v5 = T1(hN, 14, {AutomaticSize = TN, LayoutOrder = 1, Size = h(0, 18), TextTruncate = PN}), fn6(hN, "Value", 2, 16), T1(v2, 15, {AnchorPoint = F(1, 0.5), Position = q(1, -10, 0.5, 0), Size = h(84, 20), TextXAlignment = ON, TextColor3 = C(255, 194, 77)})
	        return {F = v2, Stroke = P1, Image = ImageLabel, Name = v3, Rarity = v4, SC = B1, VC = value, When = {F = v5, Text = v5}, G = {}}
	      end

	      local function TN(ON, PN, hN, arg)
	        local key = I(hN.Uid) .. ("|" .. arg)
	        jN(ON, true)

	        if ON.Key ~= key then
	          local en, P1 = hN.En, hN.Rec.Mutations
	          local v2, v3, v4 = LN(en)
	          ON.Key = key
	          fn3(ON, v4)
	          local image, icon = ON.Image, ((PN.Egg and not ((xN and en) and en.Icon)) and M(hN.Rec)) or (en and en.Icon)
	          image.Image = I(icon or "")
	          ON.Rank.Text, ON.Mu.Text, ON.Name.Text, ON.Rarity.Text = "#" .. arg, (QN(P1) and table.concat(P1, " ")) or "", I((en and en.DisplayName) or hN.Cat), v2:upper()
	        end

	        sN(ON.VC, rN(hN.Rate))
	        sN(ON.SC, ("x%.2f"):format(hN.Size))
	        sN(ON.HC, PN.Egg and RN(hN.Uid, hN.Rec))
	      end

	      local function M(ON)
	        local PN = math.max(0, os.time() - (R(ON) or os.time()))
	        return ((((PN < 60) and (PN .. "s ago")) or ((PN < 3600) and ((PN // 60) .. "m ago"))) or ((PN < 86400) and ((PN // 3600) .. "h ago"))) or ((PN // 86400) .. "d ago")
	      end

	      local function ON()
	        for PN, hN in ipairs(WN) do
	          PN = FN
	          local RN = PN[hN.Show.Id] == true
	          jN(hN.Panel, RN)

	          if RN then
	            local RN, v2, cards = C1()[hN.Show.Id], ipairs, hN.Cards

	            for C1, card in v2(cards) do
	              PN = RN[C1]

	              if PN then
	                if C1 <= tN then
	                  TN(card, hN.Show, PN, C1)
	                else
	                  jN(card, false)
	                end
	              else
	                jN(card, false)
	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        local TN = JN
	        if not (TN) then
	          return
	        end
	        jN(TN, FN["Steal History"] == true)
	        local PN = false

	        for hN, RN in ipairs(IN) do
	          local C1 = t[hN]

	          if C1 then
	            if hN <= (tN + 1) then
	              jN(RN, true)
	              local t = I(C1.At) .. ("|" .. (I(C1.Category) .. ("|" .. I(xN))))

	              if RN.Key ~= t then
	                local hN = r(C1.Category)
	                local v2, v3, v4 = LN(hN)
	                RN.Key = t
	                fn3(RN, v4)
	                local t, LN = RN.Image, (((xN and hN) and hN.Icon) and I(hN.Icon)) or ((hN and QN(hN.Egg)) and I(hN.Egg.Icon or ""))
	                t.Image = (LN or (hN and I(hN.Icon or ""))) or ""
	                RN.Name.Text, RN.Rarity.Text = I(C1.Name or C1.Category), I(C1.Rarity or ""):upper()
	                sN(RN.VC, (R(C1.Value) and rN(C1.Value)) or "")
	                sN(RN.SC, (R(C1.Scale) and ("x%.2f"):format(R(C1.Scale))) or "")
	              end

	              sN(RN.When, M(C1.At))
	              PN = true
	            else
	              jN(RN, false)
	            end
	          else
	            jN(RN, false)
	          end
	        end

	        jN(TN.E, not PN)
	      end

	      local function M()
	        for t, TN in next, wN, nil do
	          jN(TN, FN[t] == true)
	        end

	        for t, TN in ipairs(_N) do
	          if TN.Group then
	            local sN, PN, hN = ipairs, TN.Group.Items, false

	            for rN, rN in sN(PN) do
	              t = FN
	              hN = if t[rN.Id] then true else hN
	            end

	            jN(TN, hN)
	          end
	        end

	        ON()
	      end

	      local function t()
	        local TN = VN(zN, C(20, 18, 24), 0.12, {Active = true, AnchorPoint = F(0.5, 0), Size = h(470, 62)})
	        local F, sN = C(255, 196, 60), C(255, 60, 40)
	        nN(TN, 16)
	        XN(UN(TN, S, 2.4, mN), F, sN, 0)
	        fn2(TN, 0.9, 40)
	        vN[#vN + 1] = o("UIScale", {Parent = TN})
	        local PN = VN(TN, C(30, 24, 30), nil, {Position = h(9, 8), Size = h(46, 46)})
	        nN(PN, 12)
	        XN(UN(PN, S, 2), F, sN, 45)
	        nN(cN("ImageLabel", PN, {Image = AN, ScaleType = A.ScaleType.Crop, Size = p(1, 1)}), 12)
	        XN(T1(TN, 24, {Text = "CHILLI HUB", Position = h(64, 8), Size = h(200, 26)}), C(255, 226, 120), C(255, 80, 40))
	        T1(TN, 12, {Text = "FARM STATS  |  STEAL AN EGG", TextColor3 = fN, Position = h(65, 36), Size = h(200, 16)}, true)
	        o("UICorner", {Parent = VN(TN, S, 0.82, {Position = h(282, 12), Size = h(2, 38)}), CornerRadius = Q(1, 0)})

	        local function C(p, F, S)
	          local AN = T1(TN, 26, {Position = h(p, 6), Size = h(F, 30), TextXAlignment = qN, TextColor3 = oN})
	          T1(TN, 12, {Text = S, TextColor3 = fN, Position = h(p, 38), Size = h(F, 14), TextXAlignment = qN}, true)
	          return AN
	        end

	        _N[1] = {F = TN, Order = 0}
	        return C(296, 78, "FPS"), (C(380, 80, "PING MS"))
	      end

	      local function C(p, F, S)
	        local AN = K(p):Get()

	        if QN(AN) then
	          if type(AN.XOffset) == "number" then
	            if type(AN.YOffset) == "number" then
	              p = R(AN.Unit)
	              local K = ((p and (p > 0)) and (F / p)) or 1
	              return q(R(AN.XScale) or 0, w((AN.XOffset * K) + 0.5), 0, w((16 + (((((R(AN.YScale) or 0) * S) + AN.YOffset) - (R(AN.Top) or 16)) * K)) + 0.5))
	            end
	          end
	        end
	      end

	      local function p()
	        local F, K = EN(), 0

	        for R, R in ipairs(_N) do
	          K = if R.Col == "Center" then if R.F.Visible then math.max(K, R.F.AbsoluteSize.X) else K else K
	        end

	        local R, S = w((K / 2) + (26 * F)), _N[1].F
	        S.Position = q(0.5, 0, 0, 16)
	        local K = (16 + S.AbsoluteSize.Y) + w(46 * F)
	        local AN, TN = {Left = K, Right = K, Center = K}, aN().Y

	        for sN = 2, #_N, 1 do
	          K = _N[sN]
	          S, sN = K.Col, K.F
	          local aN = AN[S]
	          sN.Position = C(K.Id, F, TN) or q(0.5, (((S == "Left") and -R) or ((S == "Right") and R)) or 0, 0, aN)

	          if sN.Visible then
	            AN[S] = (aN + sN.AbsoluteSize.Y) + w(42 * F)
	          end
	        end
	      end

	      local C = 0

	      local function F()
	        if not (zN) then
	          return
	        end
	        C += 1
	        local K = C
	        local R = EN()

	        for S, S in ipairs(vN) do
	          S.Scale = R
	        end

	        task.defer(function()
	          local R = s[20]
	          R()
	          R = s[20]
	          R()

	          if K ~= C then
	            return
	          elseif not (zN) then
	            return
	          end

	          p()
	          R = s[20]
	          R()

	          if K ~= C then
	            return
	          elseif not (zN) then
	            return
	          end

	          for C, C in ipairs(_N) do
	            fn4(C.F, C.Id)
	          end
	        end)
	      end

	      local function C()
	        for p, p in ipairs(GN) do
	          p:Disconnect()
	        end

	        if zN then
	          zN:Destroy()
	        end

	        zN, JN = nil, nil
	        local p = {GN, vN, _N, wN, WN, IN}

	        for K, K in ipairs(p) do
	          table.clear(K)
	        end
	      end

	      local function p()
	        C()
	        zN = o("ScreenGui", {Archivable = false, DisplayOrder = -101, IgnoreGuiInset = true, ResetOnSpawn = false, ZIndexBehavior = A.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling})
	        local A, K = t()

	        for t, R in ipairs(kN) do
	          local S = H(R, t)
	          S.Group = R
	          A1(S.Body, 4)

	          for t, AN in ipairs(R.Items) do
	            local R, TN = cN("Frame", S.Body, {LayoutOrder = t, Size = h((AN.Wide and 380) or 250, 46)}), AN.Id:upper()
	            local t, S, sN = cN("ImageLabel", R, {Image = AN.Icon, Position = h(0, 2), ScaleType = BN, Size = h(42, 42)}), T1(R, 13, {RichText = true, Text = TN, TextColor3 = fN, Position = h(50, 3), Size = q(1, -50, 0, 14)}, true), T1(R, (AN.Wide and 18) or 24, {Position = h(50, 18), Size = q(1, -50, 0, 26)})
	            XN(sN, AN.A, AN.B)
	            wN[AN.Id] = {F = R, Icon = t, Value = sN, Cap = S, Title = TN, Item = AN}
	          end
	        end

	        for q, t in ipairs(f) do
	          local f, R = H(t, 100 + q), {}
	          o("UIListLayout", {Parent = f.Body, FillDirection = gN, SortOrder = bN, Padding = Q(0, 10)})

	          for q = 1, 6, 1 do
	            R[q] = O1(f.Body, q)
	          end

	          WN[#WN + 1] = {Panel = f, Show = t, Cards = R}
	        end

	        JN = H(DN("Steal History", "Left", 255, 60, 90, 255, 140, 160), 2.5)
	        A1(JN.Body, 6)

	        for q = 1, 7, 1 do
	          IN[q] = yN(JN.Body, q)
	        end

	        local q, f = JN, {}
	        f.F, q.E = T1(q.Body, 16, {LayoutOrder = 99, Text = "No steals yet", TextColor3 = fN, Size = h(330, 30)}), f

	        table.sort(_N, function(f, h)
	          return f.Order < h.Order
	        end)

	        M()
	        zN.Parent = CN
	        F()
	        q = workspace.CurrentCamera

	        if q then
	          YN(q:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"), F)
	        end

	        local f, h, Q, H, t, R = 0, 0, 0, 0
	        q = s[59]

	        YN(q.RenderStepped, function(q)
	          q = math.clamp(q, 0.001, 1)
	          local S, yN = (f <= 0) and (1 / q)

	          if S then
	            yN = S
	          else
	            local AN = f
	            yN = AN + (((1 / q) - f) * (1 - math.exp(-q * 8)))
	          end

	          f = yN
	          S = k()
	          if S < h then
	            return
	          end
	          h = S + 0.25
	          yN = w(f + 0.5)

	          if yN ~= t then
	            t = yN
	            A.Text, A.TextColor3 = I(yN), (((yN >= 100) and oN) or ((yN >= 50) and pN)) or NN
	          end

	          yN, S = pcall(function()
	            return L.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
	          end)

	          S = w(((yN and S) or 0) + 0.5)

	          if S ~= R then
	            R = S
	            K.Text, K.TextColor3 = I(S), (((S <= 90) and oN) or ((S <= 180) and pN)) or NN
	          end
	        end)

	        YN(d, function()
	          local A = k()
	          if A < Q then
	            return
	          end
	          Q = A + 0.5

	          for q, f in next, wN, nil do
	            if FN[q] then
	              local q, h, Q, K = pcall(f.Item.Read)

	              if q then
	                h, Q, K = I(h or "-"), I(Q or ""), K or f.Item.Icon

	                if h ~= f.LV then
	                  f.LV = h
	                  f.Value.Text = h
	                end

	                if Q ~= f.LQ then
	                  f.LQ = Q
	                  f.Cap.Text = f.Title .. (((Q ~= "") and (uN .. (Q .. "</font>"))) or "")
	                end

	                if K ~= f.LI then
	                  f.LI = K
	                  f.Icon.Image = K
	                end
	              end
	            end
	          end

	          if (A >= H) or SN then
	            H, SN = A + 1, false
	            pcall(ON)
	          end
	        end)
	      end

	      local function A()
	        local q = s[15]
	        q.hideUI(ZN and MN)
	      end

	      local q = s[15]

	      q.hudUI = function(q)
	        local f = x(q, "Farm HUD", "farmHud", false, function(h)
	          MN = h
	          local Q, H = pcall, s[15]
	          local K, t, L = H.fpsShow, not h

	          if h then
	            L = t
	          else
	            H = s[28]
	            L = H.fpsHud ~= false
	          end

	          Q(K, L)

	          if h then
	            p()
	          else
	            C()
	          end

	          A()
	        end, "Drag any panel to place it where you like")

	        q:Toggle("Hide Game UI", true, function(p)
	          ZN = p
	          A()
	        end, nil, f):When(f)

	        q:Dropdown("HUD Items", G, G, function(p)
	          FN = _(p) or _(G)
	          M()
	          F()
	        end, true, nil, f):When(f)

	          tN = p
	          pcall(ON)
	        end, nil, 1, nil, f):When(f)

	        q:Slider("HUD Size", 50, 120, HN, function(p)
	          KN = p / 100
	          F()
	        end, "%", 1, nil, f):When(f)

	        q:Dropdown("Egg Card Image", {"Egg Image", "Pet Image"}, "Egg Image", function(q)
	          xN = q == "Pet Image"

	          for q, q in ipairs(WN) do
	            for p, p in ipairs(q.Cards) do
	              p.Key = nil
	            end
	          end

	          pcall(ON)
	        end, false, "Picture used for Top Eggs and Steal History", f):When(f)
	      end

	      table.insert(s[15].cleanup, function()
	        MN = false
	        C()
	        A()
	      end)
	    end)

	    P("50_system#1", function()
	      local A, C, q = lN.Server, ((queue_on_teleport or queueonteleport) or (syn and syn.queue_on_teleport)) or (fluxus and fluxus.queue_on_teleport), "local ok, on = pcall(function() return game:GetService(\"TeleportService\"):GetTeleportSetting(\"__ChilliAutoLoadScriptEnabled\") end)\nif ok and on == true then\n  if not game:IsLoaded() then game.Loaded:Wait() end\n  loadstring(game:HttpGet(\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tienkhanh1/spicy/main/Chilli.lua\"))()\nend"

	      local function p(f)
	        pcall(function()
	          i:SetTeleportSetting("__ChilliAutoLoadScriptEnabled", f == true)
	        end)

	        if f and C then
	          if rawget(_G, "__ChilliAutoLoadQueued") ~= true then
	            if pcall(C, q) then
	              _G.__ChilliAutoLoadQueued = true
	            end
	          end
	        end
	      end

	      x(A, "Auto Load Script", "autoExec", true, function(q)
	        p(q)

	        if q then
	          if not (C) then
	            if W.ready then
	              z("Auto Load", "This executor cannot queue a script for the next server")
	            end
	          end
	        end
	      end)

	      local C = s[15]

	      C.beforeTeleport = function()
	        local C = s[28]
	        p(C.autoExec)
	      end

	      local C = s[1]

	      b(C.OnTeleport, function(q)
	        if q == Enum.TeleportState.Started then
	          local q = s[15]
	          q.beforeTeleport()
	        end
	      end)

	      j(A, "Server Hop Mode", "hopMode", {"Most Players", "Random", "Least Players"}, "Least Players")

	        end

	          return string.format("%.2fB/s", h / 1000000000)
	        end

	        if h >= 1000000 then
	          return string.format("%.2fM/s", h / 1000000)
	        end
	        return string.format("%d/s", math.floor(h))
	      end

	          end
	        end

	      local function xN()
	        if not (JN) then
	          return
	        end
	        JN.tries += 1
	        HN.tried[JN.e.jobId] = os.clock()
	        local jN, cN = HN.ajSend, {a = "t"}
	        cN.j = JN.e.jobId
	        jN(cN)
	        yN.ChilliFinderJoinAt = os.clock()

	        pcall(function()
	          A[5]:SetTeleportSetting("__ChilliFinderAt", os.time())
	        end)

	        cN.fly = nil
	        VN.next = nN

	          A[5]:SetTeleportSetting("__ChilliFinderAuto", (HN.auto and JN.e.jobId) or false)
	        end)

	            A[5]:SetTeleportSetting("__ChilliFinderAutoLoad", true)
	          end)

	                if rawget(_G, "__ChilliFinderQueued") ~= true then
	                  if pcall(jN, ("local ok, on = pcall(function() return game:GetService(\"TeleportService\"):GetTeleportSetting(\"__ChilliFinderAutoLoad\") end)\nif ok and on == true then if not game:IsLoaded() then game.Loaded:Wait() end loadstring(game:HttpGet(%q))() end"):format(cN)) then

	                  end

	              end
	            end
	          end

	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end)
	      end

	      local function jN(cN, VN)
	        if not (GN()) then
	          return zN((HN.who and "No active booking. Book hours at chillihub.pro") or "Sign in with an active Finder booking first")
	        end

	        if not (hN()) then
	          return zN("Finder runs in another script")
	        end

	        if cN then
	          if cN.jobId then
	            if cN.placeId then
	              if cN.jobId == A[1].JobId then
	                return
	              end
	            else
	              return
	            end
	          else
	            return
	          end
	        else
	          return
	        end

	        if SN() then
	          return zN("Night is close, join again after the reset")
	        end
	        local nN, UN, gap = {e = cN, tries = 0, next = 0, why = "", a = VN, force = if VN then not VN else fN.gap > 0}, os.clock(), (VN and 8) or ((fN.gap > 0) and fN.gap)
	        nN.stop = UN + (gap or 15)
	        JN = nN
	        HN.dirty = true
	        xN()
	      end

	      sN(A[5].TeleportInitFailed, function(cN, VN, nN)
	        if cN ~= A[9] then
	          return
	        elseif not (JN) then
	          return
	        end

	        local UN, teleportResult = A[19](), A[11].TeleportResult

	        if VN == teleportResult.IsTeleporting then
	          if JN.a then
	            local A1, C1, T1, next_ = JN, JN, JN, UN + 0.25
	            A1.fly = nil
	            C1.next = next_
	            T1.tries = 0
	            return
	          end
	        end

	        cN = (((VN == teleportResult.GameFull) and "full") or (((VN == teleportResult.GameEnded) or (VN == teleportResult.GameNotFound)) and "dead")) or "other"

	        if cN == "full" then
	          HN.fullAt[JN.e.jobId] = UN + 3
	        end

	        if VN == teleportResult.Flooded then
	          if JN.a then
	            HN.floodAt = UN + 2
	          end
	        end

	        local A1, C1, T1, why, floodAt = JN, JN, JN, K(((nN ~= "") and nN) or VN):gsub("Enum%.TeleportResult%.", ""):sub(1, 40), (JN.a and HN.floodAt) or 0
	        A1.fly = nil
	        C1.why = why
	        T1.next = floodAt

	        if JN.force then
	          A1, floodAt = HN.ajSend, {a = "x"}
	          floodAt.j, floodAt.r = JN.e.jobId, cN
	          A1(floodAt)
	          IN()
	          return
	        end

	        teleportResult = JN.a
	        HN.failed[JN.e.jobId] = UN + ((((cN == "dead") or (fN.gap == 0)) and 120) or 0)
	        RN((if teleportResult then not teleportResult else "Could not join: " .. JN.why) or nil, cN)

	        if teleportResult then
	          task.defer(HN.autoNext)
	        end
	      end)

	      sN(A[9].OnTeleport, function(cN)

	      end)

	      local cN

	      local function VN()
	        cN = nil

	          cN = {}
