	          end
	        end
	      end

	      VN()

	      local function nN(UN)
	        local v2 = YN()
	        return eN() >= (((C(((UN.at or 0) / 1000) / v2) + 1) * v2) - (t(workspace:GetAttribute("AreaEggCycleDefaultNightSeconds")) or 10))
	      end

	      local function UN(arg)
	        local A1, C1 = ((arg.value or 0) >= fN.min) and ((arg.rarity or 0) >= fN.rar)

	        if A1 then
	          local T1 = cN
	          local flag = not T1

	          if T1 then
	            local T1 = cN
	            C1 = T1[arg.name] == true
	          else
	            C1 = flag
	          end
	        else
	          C1 = A1
	        end

	        return C1
	      end

	      local function fn()
	        local A1 = YN()
	        return ((C(eN() / A1) * A1) - 5) * 1000
	      end

	      local A1 = {}

	      local function C1()
	        local T1 = os.clock()

	        if T1 >= (HN.ownAt or 0) then
	          if not (HN.ownBusy) then
	            HN.ownAt = T1 + 2
	            HN.ownBusy = true

	            task.spawn(function()
	              local T1 = {}

	              pcall(function()
	                local directory = require(A[3].Data.Assets).Directory

	                for k2, record in pairs(require(A[3].Client.EggState).ReadFieldEggs().Records) do
	                  if type(record) == "table" then
	                    if record.State ~= "Claimed" then
	                      k2 = directory[record.AssetCategory]
	                      T1[tostring((k2 and k2.DisplayName) or record.AssetCategory) .. ("|%.2f"):format(tonumber(record.AssetScale) or 0)] = true
	                    end
	                  end
	                end
	              end)

	              A1 = T1
	              HN.ownBusy = false
	            end)
	          end
	        end

	        local T1, v2, sample = {}, ipairs, HN.sample

	        for k2, v3 in v2(sample) do
	          k2 = A1

	          if not (k2[tostring(v3.name) .. ("|%.2f"):format(tonumber(v3.size) or 0)]) then
	            T1[#T1 + 1] = v3
	            if #T1 == 5 then
	              break
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        return T1
	      end

	      local function A1()
	        local T1 = fn()
	        if not (GN()) then
	          HN.top = C1()
	          return
	        end
	        local C1, tbl = {}, {}

	        for i2, egg in ipairs(HN.eggs) do
	          if (egg.at or 0) >= T1 then
	            if UN(egg) then
	              if egg.jobId then
	                if egg.jh ~= PN then
	                  if egg.jobId ~= A[1].JobId then
	                    if HN.auto then
	                      if not (((egg.gone or nN(egg)) or (HN.aj and HN.aj.g[K(egg.id)])) or ((fN.age > 0) and (LN(egg) > fN.age))) then
	                        if fN.age == 0 then
	                          if not ((egg.at or 0) <= (HN.autoAt or 0)) then
	                            C1[#C1 + 1] = egg
	                          end
	                        else
	                          C1[#C1 + 1] = egg
	                        end
	                      end
	                    else
	                      C1[#C1 + 1] = egg
	                    end
	                  end
	                end
	              else
	                tbl[#tbl + 1] = egg
	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        table.sort(C1, function(PN, arg)
	          if PN.value ~= arg.value then
	            return PN.value > arg.value
	          end
	          return (PN.at or 0) > (arg.at or 0)
	        end)

	        HN.pick = C1
	        HN.pickSet = {}

	        for PN, PN in ipairs(C1) do
	          HN.pickSet[PN] = true
	        end

	        if fN.list > 0 then
	          local PN = {}

	          for i2, egg in ipairs(HN.eggs) do
	            if (egg.at or 0) >= T1 then
	              PN[#PN + 1] = egg
	            end
	          end

	          if fN.list == 2 then
	            table.sort(PN, function(arg, arg2)
	              return (arg.at or 0) > (arg2.at or 0)
	            end)
	          else
	            table.sort(PN, function(arg, arg2)
	              return (arg.value or 0) > (arg2.value or 0)
	            end)
	          end

	          C1 = PN
	        else
	          C1 = table.clone(C1)

	          for PN, PN in ipairs(tbl) do
	            C1[#C1 + 1] = PN
	          end

	          for PN, PN in ipairs(HN.eggs) do
	            if PN.jobId == A[1].JobId then
	              if (PN.at or 0) >= T1 then
	                if UN(PN) then
	                  C1[#C1 + 1] = PN
	                end
	              end
	            end
	          end

	          table.sort(C1, function(PN, arg)
	            if (PN.jobId == nil) ~= (arg.jobId == nil) then
	              return PN.jobId ~= nil
	            end
	            return (PN.at or 0) > (arg.at or 0)
	          end)
	        end

	        for PN = #C1, 31, -1 do
	          C1[PN] = nil
	        end

	        tbl, T1 = false, false

	        for PN, PN in ipairs(C1) do
	          if PN.jobId then
	            tbl = true
	          else
	            T1 = true
	          end
	        end

	        if tbl then
	          HN.roomNew = false
	        end

	        HN.newWait = if tbl then not tbl else HN.roomNew or T1
	        HN.top = C1
	      end

	      HN.pickNext = function()
	        local PN = {}
	        A1()
	        local C1, T1, f2, go, n2 = A[19](), (HN.aj and HN.aj.m) or {}, (HN.aj and HN.aj.f) or {}, HN.go and K(HN.go), 0

	        if HN.go then
	          if (C1 - HN.goAt) > 2 then
	            HN.go = nil
	            go = nil
	          else
	          end
	        else
	        end

	        if HN.holdT and (C1 < HN.holdT) then
	          if not (go) then
	            HN.gate = nil
	            return
	          end
	        elseif HN.ws then
	          if not (HN.aj) then
	            if C1 < (HN.ajT or 0) then
	              if not (go) then
	                HN.gate = nil
	                return
	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        local n3, pick = ipairs, HN.pick or {}

	        for O1, O1 in n3(pick) do
	          n2 = T(n2, O1.at or 0)
	        end

	        local function O1(P1, arg)
	          local flag = (P1.at or 0) >= (n2 - 250)
	          if flag ~= ((arg.at or 0) >= (n2 - 250)) then
	            return flag
	          end

	          if flag then
	            if P1.value ~= arg.value then
	              return P1.value > arg.value
	            end
	          end

	          return (P1.at or 0) > (arg.at or 0)
	        end

	        local v2, P1, v3

	        for i2, v4 in ipairs(HN.pick or {}) do
	          if v4.jobId then
	            if (HN.failed[v4.jobId] or 0) <= C1 then
	              pick, n3 = K(v4.id), HN.tried[v4.jobId] or 0

	              if go == pick then
	                i2 = -1
	                v2, P1, v3 = v4, i2, n3
	                break
	              end

	              local n4 = ((((f2[pick] or ((HN.fullAt[v4.jobId] or 0) > C1)) or (((v4.pm or 0) > 0) and ((v4.pc or 0) >= v4.pm))) and 10000) or t(T1[pick])) or 0

	              if (if v2 then n4 < P1 else not v2) or ((n4 == P1) and ((n3 < v3) or ((n3 == v3) and O1(v4, v2)))) then
	                v2, P1, v3 = v4, n4, n3
	              else
	              end
	            else
	            end
	          else
	          end
	        end

	        PN[1] = HN
	        PN[2] = (v2 and fN.hold) and (eN() < ((((v2.at or 0) / 1000) + A[9]:GetNetworkPing()) + 0.1))
	        PN[1].gate = PN[2]
	        PN[3] = ((((v2 and not HN.gate) and not SN()) and (C1 >= (HN.floodAt or 0))) and (v2.placeId == A[1].PlaceId)) and v2.jobId
	        n3, T1 = PN[3], nil

	        if n3 then
	          pick = JN
	          C1 = not pick

	          if pick then
	            f2 = (JN.a and not JN.fly) and (v2.jobId ~= JN.e.jobId)

	            if f2 then
	              O1 = P1 == -1

	              if O1 then
	                T1 = O1
	              else
	                local PN = HN.tried[v2.jobId]
	                T1 = if PN then not PN else (v2.at or 0) > ((JN.e.at or 0) + 250)
	              end
	            else
	              T1 = f2
	            end
	          else
	            T1 = C1
	          end
	        else
	          T1 = n3
	        end

	        if T1 then
	          if P1 == -1 then
	            HN.go = nil
	          end

	          JN = nil
	          jN(v2, true)
	        end
	      end

	      HN.autoNext = function()
	        if HN.auto then
	          if GN() then
	            if hN() then
	              if not (HN.hold) then
	                HN.pickNext()
	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end
	      end

	      HN.ajIn = function(PN)
	        local C1 = {}

	        if PN.aj then

	      local function PN(YN, arg)
	        for C1, T1 in ipairs(((type(YN) == "table") and YN) or {}) do
	          C1 = (type(T1) == "table") and ((T1.id or T1.uid) or (K(T1.name) .. K(T1.at)))

	            end
	          end
	        end

	          for i2 = #HN.eggs, 1, -1 do

	              HN.seen[HN.eggs[i2].id or ""] = nil
	              table.remove(HN.eggs, i2)
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        if #HN.eggs > 60 then
	          local function YN(arg2)
	            return ((UN(arg2) and (LN(arg2) <= 300)) and 1) or 0
	          end

	          table.sort(HN.eggs, function(LN, UN)
	            local v2, C1 = YN(LN), YN(UN)

	              return v2 > C1
	            end

	          end)

	          for LN = 1, #HN.eggs - 60, 1 do

	          end
	        end

	      HN.reclaim = function()
	        HN.sw = HN.sw or {}
	        local F, M = pairs, HN.tx or {}

	        for u, X in F(M) do
	          local Q = u.Parent

	          if Q then
	            local Z, v = HN.mask(X[1]), HN.sw[u]

	            if v then
	              if v.Parent ~= Q then
	                pcall(v.Destroy, v)
	                HN.sw[u] = nil
	                u.Text = ""
	                v = nil
	              else
	              end
	            else
	            end

	            if v then
	            else
	              u.RichText = true

	              if HN.txOn then
	                if u.Text ~= Z then
	                  local k = u:Clone()
	                  k.Name = "t" .. math.random(1000000000)
	                  k.RichText = true
	                  k.Text = Z
	                  k.Visible = true
	                  k.TextTransparency = 0

	                  for I, I in ipairs(k:GetChildren()) do
	                    if I:IsA("UIStroke") then
	                      I.Enabled = true
	                    end
	                  end

	                  for I, I in ipairs(u:GetChildren()) do
	                    if I:IsA("UIStroke") then
	                      I.Enabled = false
	                    end
	                  end

	                  k.Parent, HN.sw[u] = Q, k
	                  v = k
	                else
	                  if u.Text ~= Z then
	                    u.Text = Z
	                  end
	                end
	              else
	                if u.Text ~= Z then
	                  u.Text = Z
	                end
	              end

	              if not (HN.txOn) then
	                u.Visible = X[2]
	              end
	            end

	            if v then
	              if v.Text ~= Z then
	                v.Text = Z
	              end

	              local X = (u ~= M1.stat) or u.Visible

	              if v.Visible ~= X then
	                v.Visible = X
	              end

	              if u.TextTransparency ~= 1 then
	                u.TextTransparency = 1
	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        M, F = M1.B.acct, os.clock()
	        M.par = M.par or M.b.Parent
	        local u, X = HN.txOn

	        if u then
	          local Q = M.b.Visible
	          X = if Q then M.b.Parent ~= M.par else not Q
	        else
	          X = u
	        end

	        if X and (F >= (M.at or 0)) then
	          local Q, Z = M.b, M.b:Clone()

	          for v, k in pairs(M) do
	            if typeof(k) == "Instance" then

	                    end
	                  end
	                end
	              end
	            end
	          end

	      end

	      local function C()
	        if HN.ws then
	          return
	        elseif HN.wsP then
	          return
	        elseif HN.wsC then
	          return
	        elseif os.clock() < HN.wsAt then
	          return
	        elseif GN() then
	          if not (hN()) then
	            return
	          end
	        else
	          return
	        end

	        HN.wsAt = os.clock() + 3
	        local a = (WebSocket and (WebSocket.connect or WebSocket.new)) or ((syn and syn.websocket) and syn.websocket.connect)
	        if not a then
	          return
	        end
	        HN.wsC = true

	        task.spawn(function()
	          local q, f = pcall(a, "wss://chillihub.pro/roblox-mcp/eggws")
	          HN.wsC = nil

	          if q then
	            if not f then
	              return
	            end
	          else
	            return
	          end

	          local a = HN.sess
	          local q, h = f.OnMessage or f.MessageReceived, f.OnClose or f.Closed

	          if not ((CN and a) and q) then
	            pcall(function()
	              f:Close()
	            end)

	            return
	          end

	          q:Connect(function(q)
	            HN.wsMsg = os.clock()

	            if HN.wsP == f then
	              HN.wsP = nil
	              HN.ws = f
	              HN.dirty = true
	            end

	            if #HN.inbox < 200 then
	              HN.inbox[#HN.inbox + 1] = {q, a.secret}
	            end
	          end)

	          if h then
	            h:Connect(function()
	              if HN.wsP == f then
	                HN.wsP = nil
	              end

	              if HN.ws == f then
	                HN.ws = nil
	                HN.aj = nil
	                HN.ajKey = nil
	                HN.dirty = true
	              end
	            end)
	          end

	          pcall(function()
	            f:Send(uN(A[6]:JSONEncode({session = a.token, ts = math.floor(eN() * 1000)}), qN))
	          end)

	          HN.wsP = f

	          task.delay(6, function()
	            if HN.wsP == f then
	              HN.wsP = nil

	              pcall(function()
	                f:Close()
	              end)
	            end
	          end)
	        end)
	      end

	      local function q()

	          end
	        end)

	        return x:NextInteger(c, AN)
	      end

	      local function c(AN, mN)
	        return x:NextNumber(AN or 0, mN or 1)
	      end

	      local function x(AN)

	        if BN then
	          if BN.Parent then
	            BN.Text, BN.Position, BN.Rotation, BN.TextTransparency = bN.t, bN.p, bN.r, bN.a

	              end
	            end

	                gN.Offset = bN.f
	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end

	            AN.TextTransparency = 1
	          end

	      local function mN(BN)
	        local bN = {}
	        local gN = BN.Parent
	        local oN, pN = S(BN, H), {all = {}, gh = {}, sh = {}}

	        local function NN(FN)
	          table.insert(pN.all, FN)
	          return FN
	        end

	        local function FN()

	          MN:ClearAllChildren()
	          MN.Name = fn2()
	          MN.BackgroundTransparency = 1

	            oN:Clone().Parent = MN
	          end

	          return MN
	        end

	          return NN(L({Name = fn2(), bt = 1, bs = 0, ClipsDescendants = true, a = BN.AnchorPoint, z = BN.ZIndex, v = false}, gN))

	      local function y(o)
	        local D = {}

	        for G = 1, #o, 1 do
	          D[G] = o:sub(G, G)
	        end

	        for G = 1, j(1, 2), 1 do
	          o = j(1, #D)

	          if D[o] ~= " " then
	            D[o] = Z[j(1, #Z)]
	          end
	        end

	        return table.concat(D)
	      end

	      local function o(Z)
	        local D = {}
	        local G, L, v = Y.p, Y.l, Y.b

	        if G then
	          if L then
	            if not (L.Parent) then
	              return
	            end
	          else
	            return
	          end
	        else
	          return
	        end

	        if not (v) then
	          if Z < Y.n then
	            return
	          end

	          if L.AbsoluteSize.X <= 0 then
	            Y.n = Z + 1
	            return
	          end
	          local w, BN = S(L, H), S(L, r)
	          Y.b = {c = Z, nx = 0, e = 0, d = c(0.14, 0.4), s = c(0.38, 0.62), o = {t = L.Text, p = L.Position, r = L.Rotation, a = L.TextTransparency, s = (w and w.Color) or nil, f = (BN and BN.Offset) or nil}}
	          return
	        end

	        if Z < v.nx then
	          return
	        end
	        v.nx, v.e = Z + 0.03, Z - v.c

	        if v.e >= v.d then
	          x(v)
	          Y.b, Y.n = nil, Z + c(1.4, 4)
	          return
	        end

	        local w, BN, bN, gN = v.o, e(v.e / v.d, 0, 1), j(-4, 4), j(-3, 3)
	        local oN, pN = w.r + c(-2, 2), ((c() < 0.6) and y(w.t)) or w.t
	        D[1] = w.p + A[17](bN, gN)
	        L.Position = D[1]
	        L.Rotation = oN
	        L.Text = pN
	        local y, NN, FN, MN = L.Size, v.s, j(3, 12), j(0, 5)
	        local uN, XN, ZN, tN, DN = y.X.Scale, y.X.Offset, y.Y.Scale, y.Y.Offset, 1 - NN
	        y = (A[14](0, 0, ZN * NN, tN * NN))
	        L.TextTransparency = 1
	        D[2] = G.top
	        D[3] = G.top
	        D[4] = G.top
	        D[5] = (A[14](uN, XN, ZN * NN, tN * NN))
	        D[6] = w.p + A[17](FN, -MN)
	        D[2].Size = D[5]
	        D[3].Position = D[6]

	      local function Z(r)
	        return ((type(r) == "table") and r) or {}
	      end

	      local function r(H)
	        return table.find(E, (tostring(H or ""):gsub("^%d+ %- ", "")))
	      end

	      local function H(K, t)
	        local D = {}

	        for G, L in ipairs(Z(K)) do
	          G = t(tostring(L))

	          if G then
	            D[#D + 1] = G
	          end
	        end

	        return D
	      end

	      local function K(t, D, G)
	        return H(t, function(t)
	          return (t:gsub(D, G))
	        end)
	      end

	      local t = _({"Icon", "Name", "Rarity", "Value", "Mutation", "Size", "Weight", "Area"})

	      local D = {r = function(G)
	        if G == "Any" then
	          return table.clone(E)
	        end

	        if G == "Off" then
	          return {}
	        end
	        local L = r(G)
	        return L and {table.unpack(E, 1, L)}
	      end, x = function(G)
	        if G == "Off" then
	          return {}
	        end
	        local L = r(G)
	        return L and {table.unpack(E, L)}
	      end, m = function(E)
	        E = tonumber(E) or 0
	        return ((E > 0) and math.floor((10 ^ (3 + (E * 0.009))) + 0.5)) or 0
	      end, e = function(E)
	        return K(K(E, " %(%d+%)$", ""), " %[[^%]]*%]$", "")
	      end, z = function(E)
	        return ((#Z(E) < 13) and Z(E)) or {}
	      end, l = function(E)
	        return ((#Z(E) > 0) and E) or nil

	        return K(E, " To ", " to ")
	      end, i = function(E)
	        return H(E, function(E)
	          return t[E] and E
	        end)
	      end}

	      local function E(r)
	        local K, t, G = {}, Z(r.Values)

	        for L in Q:gmatch("[^\n]+") do
	          local Q, v = L:match("^@(.-)|(.+)$")

	          if Q then
	            G = ((type(t[Q]) == "table") and t[Q][v]) or nil

	            if G then
	            else
	              for Q, Q in next, t, nil do
	                if (type(Q) == "table") and (type(Q[v]) == "table") then
	                  G = Q[v]
	                  break
	                end
	              end
	            end
	          else
	            if G then
	              for Q in L:gmatch("[^|]+") do
	                local L, v, k, w = Q:match("^(.-)=(.-):([^:]*):?(.*)$")
	                k = if (w ~= "w") and ((k == "") or (k:sub(-1) == "/")) then k .. (L:sub(1, 1) .. L:sub(2):lower()) else k
	                local I, z = G[L], (u[v] or v) .. k
	                v = (type(I) == "table") and I.Value

	                if (type(I) == "table") and (v ~= nil) then
	                  if w == "h" then
	                    if v == true then
	                      K[u.Cb .. "Auto hit/Auto hit"], K[z] = true, (L:find("Holders") and "Egg holders") or "Nearest player"
	                    end
	                  elseif w == "w" then
	                    Q = _(Z(v)) or {}
	                    K[z .. "Post stolen eggs"], K[z .. "Post fused eggs"], K[z .. "Post when bag is full"] = Q["Egg Stolen"] == true, Q["Egg Fused"] == true, Q["Inventory Full"] == true
	                  else
	                    if D[w] then
	                      v = D[w](v)
	                    else
	                      if w ~= "" then
	                        local Q = {}

	                        for Z, D in w:gmatch("([^,>]+)>([^,]+)") do
	                          Q[Z] = D
	                        end

	                        v = ((type(v) == "table") and H(v, function(Z)
	                          return Q[Z] or Z
	                        end)) or Q[v]
	                      else
	                      end
	                    end

	                    if v ~= nil then
	                      K[z] = v
	                    end
	                  end
	                end
	              end
	            end
	          end
	        end

	        t = (type(r.States) == "table") and r.States["Anti Guard Enabled"]

	        if type(t) == "table" then
	          if type(t.Value) == "boolean" then
	            K[u.A .. "Anti guard"] = t.Value
	          end
	        end

	        return K
	      end
